Experiments on LasHeR, and zero-shot evaluations on RGBT234 and GTOT under multiple cross-modal protocols demonstrate improvements over representative state-of-the-art trackers. For instance, under the modality-switch protocol on RGBT234, Pre-AFA TSDA-Track achieves an SR/PR of 43.2/56.0, compared with 36.8/50.0 for ToMP-101 baseline. In addition, a study on Anti-UAV-024 further verifies the applicability of TSDA-Track to aerial tracking. Our study highlights the effectiveness of feature alignment domain adaptation for cross-modal tracking.

</details>


### [37] [Vision-Language-Action Autonomous Driving Agent with Language-based Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38641)
*Kai Yan,Xiangyu Chen,Yulong Cao,Alex Naumann,Peter Karkus,Yan Wang,Jef Packer,Alex Schwing,Yuxiong Wang,Boris Ivanovic,Wenjie Luo,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AD-Memo：以语言记忆扩展VLA的思维链，解决长时序驾驶记忆问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型因图像token成本高，只能输入有限帧，难以处理依赖记忆的任务（如全向停车到达顺序判断、长时域场景理解）。现有隐向量记忆+交叉注意力方案不可解释也不可迁移。

Method: 提出AD-Memo，通用于VLA驾驶智能体的语言化记忆：将记忆作为CoT的延伸输出，记录对驾驶关键的环境物体，并作为未来输入的一部分。构建基于记忆的数据集，采用两阶段训练：SFT与Da Capo——半闭环RL算法，对记忆使用轨迹级优势、对驾驶动作使用步级优势以改进信用分配。

Result: 在全向停车与一般驾驶等场景中，AD-Memo提升了驾驶质量，改善了驾驶场景问答表现，并可为其他模型提供即插即用的记忆。

Conclusion: 语言化记忆结合分层优势RL，是提升VLA驾驶智能体长时序记忆能力的可解释、可迁移方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) foundation models have recently emerged as one of the prevailing solutions for autonomous driving, as they can utilize knowledge acquired during vision-language pretraining for accurate and interpretable driving. However, VLAs can take only a limited number of frames as visual input due to the high token cost of an image, which is problematic for memory-dependent tasks such as determining the arrival order at all-way stops and long-horizon driving scene understanding. Existing solutions use latent vector memories accessed through cross-attention, which are neither interpretable nor portable. In this paper, we propose AD-Memo, a general-purpose VLA driving agent with language-based memory. The agent outputs memory as an extension of its Chain-of-Thought (CoT) to record surrounding objects critical to driving; this memory becomes part of the agent's future input. We curate memory-based datasets and train VLAs with a two-stage recipe: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and \textit{Da Capo}, a novel semi-closed-loop Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithm which uses trajectory-level advantage for memory and step-level advantage for driving, leading to better credit assignment. Across scenarios such as all-way stops and general driving, AD-Memo improves driving quality, enables better question answering on driving scenes, and produces plug-and-play memory for other models.

</details>


### [38] [ReGain: Restoring Subject Fidelity in Personalization on Synthetic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38680)
*Shubhang Bhatnagar,Ishan Bhatnagar,Viraj Shah,Narendra Ahuja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发现用扩散模型生成的合成图像对DreamBooth进行个性化微调会造成主体保真度下降（过饱和色彩与高频细节过多），并将该现象归因于无分类器引导（CFG）的膨胀，进而提出一种无需真实照片的训练后采样校正方法ReGain。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型的个性化（如DreamBooth）所需的主体图像越来越多地来自扩散模型而非真实相机拍摄。作者观察到，在这种合成图像上微调会降低主体保真度，产生过饱和颜色和过度的高频细节，因此希望定位其根本原因并提出解决方案。

Method: 作者在同一基座模型上用相同DreamBooth配方分别对主体的真实照片与合成图像进行微调以做受控对比，发现退化源于无分类器引导（CFG）：在合成图像上个性化的模型中，条件与无条件噪声预测之间的夹角及二者差值的范数显著更大，该膨胀随频率升高而增强，并出现在与主体语义相近的提示（如类名词）上而不出现在无关提示上。据此提出ReGain，一种训练无关的采样时校正，通过测量各频段引导相对于基座模型的膨胀程度并相应缩放到该频段，且无需真实照片。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion v1.5上，ReGain以DINO、DINOv2和CLIP-I为指标弥合了与真实照片个性化模型之间51-64%的主体保真度差距；在SDXL与SD 3.5上同样提升了主体保真度，并在三个骨干网络上均保持了文本对齐能力。

Conclusion: 用合成图像进行DreamBooth个性化会因CFG引导膨胀而损害主体保真度，ReGain作为一种无需真实照片、训练无关的频段缩放校正，可有效缓解该问题并保持文本对齐，具有良好的跨骨干泛化性。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models are personalized to a subject by DreamBooth fine-tuning on a handful of its images. Increasingly, these images come from a diffusion model rather than a camera. We show that fine-tuning on such synthetic images degrades subject fidelity, producing oversaturated color and excess high-frequency detail. To isolate the cause, we fine-tune two models from the same base model with the same DreamBooth recipe, one on real photos of a subject and one on synthetic images of that subject generated by the first. We trace the degradation to classifier-free guidance (CFG). For the model personalized on synthetic images, the angle between the conditional and unconditional noise predictions, and with it the norm of their difference, is much larger than for the model personalized on real photos. This inflation grows toward high frequencies and also appears at other prompts semantically close to the subject, such as its class noun, but not at unrelated ones. We propose ReGain, a training-free correction applied at sampling time that measures how much each frequency band of the guidance is inflated relative to the base model and scales that band down accordingly. ReGain needs no real photos. On Stable Diffusion v1.5, ReGain closes 51-64% of the subject-fidelity gap to the model personalized on real photos, as measured by DINO, DINOv2 and CLIP-I. It also improves subject fidelity on SDXL and SD 3.5 and preserves text alignment on all three backbones.

</details>


### [39] [Unveiling the Value of Motion for Cinematic Camera Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38683)
*Ziqi Zhou,Yujian Yuan,Laura Sevilla-Lara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将相机轨迹从逐帧位姿表示改为方向与速度表示，在轨迹-文本对齐和文本-轨迹生成任务上均带来显著提升，并提出CineGEN模型与CineScript数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电影相机运动是核心叙事工具，其语义不仅取决于相机位置，还取决于运动的方向与速度。现有相机轨迹生成与文本对齐工作依赖以位姿为中心的表示（per-frame poses），理论上网络可以从位姿中推导方向与速度，但实践中未必能学到。因此作者希望探究表示方式的改变是否能带来更好的对齐与生成效果，并解决现有评估协议的局限。

Method: 作者将传统逐帧位姿的相机轨迹表示分解为方向（direction）与速度（speed）两个维度，并验证其在多个任务中的收益。针对轨迹到文本的对齐任务，提出一个简单可靠的评估协议以克服以往基线的不足；针对文本到轨迹生成任务，基于该表示洞见提出新的生成模型CineGEN；同时构建新数据集CineScript，包含带有场景描述和更高层元数据的电影片段，用于测试模型捕捉高层电影语言信息的能力。

Result: 在轨迹-文本对齐与文本-轨迹生成两类任务上，方向与速度表示均带来更好的数值表现，CineGEN在多项指标上取得更优性能。CineScript数据集使模型的高层电影语言理解能力得以评估，且实验表明该表示不仅提升了对齐与生成指标，还内在编码了复杂的导演意图。

Conclusion: 尽管表示形式简单，用方向与速度刻画相机轨迹在数值上优于传统位姿表示，并能自然地编码高层导演意图，为相机轨迹理解与生成提供了新的表示范式、模型与评测数据基础。

Abstract: Cinematic camera motion is a fundamental storytelling tool, defined not only by where the camera is positioned in the scene, but also by how it moves in terms of direction and speed. Recent work on camera trajectory generation and alignment to text relies on pose-centric representations. While in principle a network could derive direction of movement and speed, we find that in practice this might not happen. In fact, in this paper we discover that decomposing the camera trajectory representation from the traditional per-frame poses to direction and speed has surprising benefits across multiple tasks, including trajectory-to-text alignment as well as text-to-trajectory generation. To accurately evaluate the former, we introduce a simple and reliable protocol that overcomes the limitations of prior evaluation baselines. For the latter, building on this representational insight, we propose a novel generative model for camera trajectories, CineGEN, that achieves superior performance across a variety of metrics. We also propose a novel dataset, CineScript, containing movie clips that are enriched with scene descriptions as well as higher-level metadata. This novel data allows us to test models' ability to capture high-level cinematographic information. We show that, despite its simplicity, representing camera trajectories through direction and speed not only helps numerically to achieve better alignment and generation, but also inherently encodes complex directorial intent.

</details>


### [40] [EPIC: Epipolar-Consistent 360° Immersive Stereo Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38689)
*Debabrata Mandal,Dongdong Fu,Jonathon Miller,William Villareal,Xi Peng,Praneeth Chakravarthula*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出零样本生成流程，将现有视频扩散模型扩展为4K立体360°视频，用于沉浸式显示内容生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉浸式显示需要丰富多样的虚拟体验，但手动制作所有内容成本高昂、难以规模化。现有生成式AI模型面向传统显示，无法生成沉浸式观看所需的高分辨率立体360°内容，且时间与立体不一致在头显中会非常明显。

Method: 受双目视觉和深度感知启发，设计极线感知的360°图像匹配度量，用于捕捉跨视角的时间与立体几何不一致；将该度量作为偏好信号，在有限训练数据下进行直接偏好优化，扩展视频扩散模型。

Result: 实现360°立体视频生成，可生成4K立体360°视频，并支持按需的多样化混合现实体验。

Conclusion: 该工作为生成式内容进入沉浸式显示提供了可扩展路径，推动按需混合现实体验的发展。

Abstract: Immersive displays can enable rich and diverse virtual experiences. Manually authoring every possible experience to realize this potential, however, is prohibitively expensive, difficult to scale, and impractical. Generative AI models could remove this bottleneck, but today's models are built for conventional displays and cannot generate the high-resolution, stereoscopic $360^\circ$ content required for immersive viewing. Further, temporal and stereo inconsistencies that may be tolerable on conventional displays can become highly disruptive when viewed through an immersive headset.
  Here, we address this gap with a zero-shot generative pipeline that extends existing video diffusion models into 4K stereoscopic $360^\circ$ videos. Inspired from binocular vision and depth perception, we develop an epipolar-aware $360^\circ$ image matching metric that captures the temporal and stereo geometric inconsistencies across views. We then use this metric as a preference signal for direct preference optimization with limited training data. Our work enables $360^\circ$ stereo video generation and provides a scalable path for bringing generative content to immersive displays, allowing diverse mixed reality experiences on demand.

</details>


### [41] [No Corners Cut: State-Grounded Transitions for Mid-Stream Prompt Switches in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38691)
*Zejing Rao,Ketong Ren,Xiaoqiang Liu,Yiping Meng,Guoxin Zhang,Fan Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEGUE 提出一种流式视频生成框架，通过训练无关的规划器在提示切换时生成过渡提示，并配合 SPANDMD 训练策略，使视频状态转换更连贯，在 OpenTrans-360 等基准上取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频生成方法在中途切换提示时存在"抄近路"问题：会过早实现目标或采用启发式捷径，跳过实现合理过渡所需的中间状态变化，导致视频过渡不自然。因此需要一种显式建模并忠实执行状态转换的方法。

Method: 提出 SEGUE 框架：在每次提示切换时，使用无需训练（training-free）的规划器解析最新帧和提示，生成若干带有角色和时长的 segue 过渡提示，之后再交还给用户提示。为训练因果模型在短暂时序调度下而不破坏预备监督，提出 SPANDMD 策略：以完整 rollout 作为时序上下文评估每个活跃提示，但仅在提示所分配的时间跨度内保留其 DMD 残差。

Result: 在 OpenTrans-360（包含 1,800 次切换、评估旧状态退出与新状态开始质量的基准）上，SEGUE 在全部八项过渡指标上排名第一，总体分数从最强基线的 0.866 提升至 0.887；在 StreamAV-Bench 的六项指令响应指标中四项排名第一；规划器无需重新训练即可迁移到冻结的自回归生成器。

Conclusion: SEGUE 通过显式的过渡提示规划与针对性的训练策略，有效提升了流式视频生成中提示切换的过渡质量，并在多个基准上验证了其有效性与良好的迁移能力。

Abstract: Streaming video generators allow users to dynamically modulate video synthesis via mid-stream prompt switching. Existing streaming methods can respond to the updated instruction while still cutting corners, prematurely realizing goals or taking heuristic shortcuts that bypass necessary intermediate state changes needed for a plausible transition. In this study, we present SEGUE, a novel framework that makes this process explicit and trains the generator to execute these transitions faithfully. At each switch, a training-free planner parses the latest frame and prompts, writes a few segue prompts with roles and durations, and then hands control back to the user's prompt. Furthermore, to address the inherent difficulty of training causal models on short-lived temporal schedules without corrupting preparatory supervision, we introduce SPANDMD, which evaluates each active prompt using the full rollout as temporal context while retaining its DMD residual only within the prompt's assigned span. On OpenTrans-360, a benchmark of 1,800 switches that scores how the old state exits and the new one begins, SEGUE ranks first on all eight transition metrics and raises the overall score over the strongest baseline from 0.866 to 0.887. It also ranks first on four of six instruction-response metrics of StreamAV-Bench, while the planner transfers to frozen autoregressive generators without retraining.

</details>


### [42] [SCALE: Synthetic Calibration via Agreement Labeling in Embedding Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38705)
*Wenjun Liu,Saeed Hassanpour*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究病理基础模型的校准问题，发现低病理医生一致性（诊断困难）样本上的校准误差更高，并提出"合成一致性校准"方法，无需多标注者标签即可改善校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算病理基础模型通常只用AUC和准确率评估，校准常被忽略。模型可能平均表现准确，却对连病理医生都难以判断的病例给出过度自信的概率，这在高风险临床场景中十分重要。

Method: 研究八个病理基础模型的校准表现，以病理医生一致性衡量诊断难度。提出合成一致性校准：基于已训练线性探针，选取高置信度嵌入作为类别锚点，在异类锚点间做插值，用插值权重编码连续的诊断模糊性，作为合成一致性信号，结合一致性感知的标签平滑重新训练探针。

Result: 在含七位病理医生标注的MHIST上，该方法恢复了大部分基于真实病理医生一致性的标签平滑所带来的校准提升，同时相较未校准基线显著降低低一致性ECE，且判别指标保持不变。在无多标注者标签的PatchCamelyon和BreakHis上，该方法对多个基础模型均改善了校准，而依赖标注者的方法则无法适用。

Conclusion: 校准误差在低一致性病例上系统性更高且不易从聚合ECE中发现；合成一致性校准可无需额外专家标注即有效改善病理基础模型的校准，同时保持判别性能。

Abstract: Foundation models for computational pathology are usually evaluated using AUC and accuracy, while calibration is often left untested. This matters because a model can be accurate on average but still assign overly confident probabilities to cases that are difficult even for pathologists. We study calibration across eight pathology foundation models. Using pathologist agreement as a measure of diagnostic difficulty, we find that calibration error is consistently higher on low-agreement cases than on high-agreement cases. This pattern is not apparent from aggregate expected calibration error (ECE) alone. We then propose synthetic agreement calibration, a method for improving calibration without collecting multi-annotator labels. Given a trained linear probe, we select high-confidence embeddings as class anchors and interpolate between anchors from opposite classes. The interpolation weights encode a continuous notion of diagnostic ambiguity, which we use as a synthetic agreement signal to retrain the probe with agreement-aware label smoothing. On MHIST, which includes annotations from seven pathologists, synthetic agreement calibration recovers most of the calibration improvement obtained by label smoothing based on real pathologist agreement, while substantially reducing low-agreement ECE relative to the uncalibrated baseline. Discrimination metrics are preserved. On PatchCamelyon and BreakHis, public histopathology datasets without multi-annotator labels, the method improves calibration across the evaluated foundation models, whereas annotator-dependent approaches cannot be used without additional expert annotation.

</details>


### [43] [Hard-Region Supervision: #1 on the Waymo Open Dataset 2D Video Panoptic Segmentation Leaderboard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38714)
*Jinghan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于 DVIS++ 的视频全景分割方法，通过难区域监督与测试时优化，在 Waymo 2D 视频全景分割挑战赛中取得三项指标第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作面向 Waymo Open Dataset 2D 视频全景分割挑战赛任务，要求对每帧每个像素给出语义类别，并对可数目标在 100 帧、跨五个重叠相机范围内保持一致的身份标识。目标是在已有基线 DVIS++ 基础上进一步提升分割与跟踪效果，取得竞赛最优成绩。

Method: 方法以 DVIS++（分割器、跟踪器、精化器的级联结构）为基线。提出难区域监督（HRS）：利用基线模型定位其犯错区域作为难区域，针对该区域设计损失函数与辅助预测头；辅助头仅用于训练，测试时移除，因此推理时模型结构与基线一致。此外提出三个测试时步骤：双模型集成、将分割器输出融合进最终全景图、跨相机身份关联。

Result: 在挑战赛测试集上，该方法达到 0.3547 wSTQ、0.2071 wAQ 和 0.6075 mIoU，三项指标均排名第一；wSTQ 比第二名高出 3.6 点，比 DVIS++ 基线高出 2.4 点。

Conclusion: 通过难区域监督与测试时集成、输出融合及跨相机身份关联，可在不改变推理结构的前提下显著提升视频全景分割性能，并在 Waymo 挑战赛中全面领先，验证了所提方法的有效性。该工作属于视频理解/分割方向，归为 Other 类别。

Abstract: We describe our winning entry to the Waymo Open Dataset 2D Video Panoptic Segmentation Challenge. The task asks for a semantic class at every pixel of every frame and, for countable objects, an identity that holds across 100 frames and across five overlapping cameras. We build on DVIS++, a cascade of a segmenter, a tracker, and a refiner, as our baseline. We propose hard region supervision (HRS) to improve the baseline. In particular, we use the baseline to define the hard region as where it makes mistakes, and design a loss and an auxiliary prediction head for this region. The auxiliary head is used only in training and removed at test time, so at inference the model trained with HRS has the same architecture as the baseline. In addition, we propose three test-time steps that further improve the results: a two-model ensemble, a merge of the segmenter's output into the final panoptic map, and cross-camera identity linking. On the challenge test set, our entry reaches 0.3547 wSTQ, 0.2071 wAQ, and 0.6075 mIoU, ranking first on all three metrics. It is 3.6 wSTQ points ahead of the second entry and 2.4 points ahead of our DVIS++ baseline.

</details>


### [44] [SpatialCORE: Confidence-Aware Grounded Spatial Reasoning in Large Vision--Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38716)
*Rafi Ibn Sultan,Xiangyu Zhou,Md. Sajid Alam Chowdhury,Chengyin Li,Prashant Khanduri,Marco Brocanelli,Dongxiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SpatialCORE 后训练框架，将生成式定位的置信度作为空间推理的学习信号，通过自调节空间奖励和答案门控，在多个基准上取得最优结果并具零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在视觉感知任务上进展显著，但空间推理仍是持续弱点，尤其是需要基于视觉空间进行推理的问题。现有方法引入生成式定位（预测边界框、掩码等），但通常只优化最终答案正确性，导致即使模型未从高置信度定位的任务相关对象推理，只要答案正确也会被奖励。

Method: SpatialCORE 是一个后训练框架，核心思想是强化既准确又高置信度的定位，促使模型从置信定位的任务相关对象出发进行推理。具体通过自调节空间奖励（self-regulating spatial reward），将每个预测边界框的匹配质量按其坐标 token 置信度加权；并引入答案门控（answer gate），将定位优化与最终答案正确性绑定。

Result: 在多个多样化基准上，SpatialCORE 在开源模型和专门的空间推理模型中取得最先进（state-of-the-art）结果，并在零样本设置下有效迁移到未见数据分布。

Conclusion: 将模型自身对生成式定位的置信度转化为学习信号，可有效提升 LVLMs 的空间推理能力；SpatialCORE 为空间推理后训练提供了新范式，并具备良好泛化性。源码已开源。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have made remarkable progress across visual perception tasks, yet spatial reasoning remains a persistent weakness, especially for questions that require reasoning over visual space. Recent spatial-reasoning methods incorporate generated grounding, where models predict bounding boxes, masks, or other localization outputs for task-relevant objects as part of their reasoning trace. However, these approaches typically optimize final-answer correctness alone, allowing correct answers to be rewarded even when the model does not reason from confidently localized task-relevant objects. We introduce SpatialCORE (Spatially COnfident REasoning), a post-training framework that turns the model's own confidence in generated grounding into a learning signal for spatial reasoning. Its central idea is to reinforce grounding that is both accurate and confident, encouraging the model to reason from confidently localized task-relevant objects. SpatialCORE realizes this through a self-regulating spatial reward that weights each predicted bounding box's matching quality by its coordinate-token confidence. An answer gate further ties grounding optimization to final-answer correctness. SpatialCORE achieves state-of-the-art results among open-source and specialized spatial reasoning models across diverse benchmarks, and transfers effectively in zero-shot settings to unseen data distributions. The source code is available at https://github.com/rafiibnsultan/SpatialCORE.

</details>


### [45] [Soft Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38717)
*Rafi Ibn Sultan,Md. Sajid Alam Chowdhury,Saleh Zare Zade,Chengyin Li,Prashant Khanduri,Marco Brocanelli,Dongxiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Soft Spatial Reasoning后训练框架，用AdaptSoft控制器自适应调节软思考程度，缓解LVLM空间推理中的过早离散化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM通常以链式思维（CoT）做空间推理，将中间推理编码为离散语言token的自回归序列。这种"硬思考"要求每步锁定单一token，即使正确空间解释尚不确定。这种过早离散化会导致错误token沿后续推理传播。

Method: 一个面向LVLM空间任务的软思考后训练框架。每个中间推理步不再选单一token，而是混合token嵌入形成连续软状态，使多个候选续写影响下一步。核心为AdaptSoft控制器，依据当前隐状态与预测不确定性自适应调节每步软度。为训练AdaptSoft，引入梯度对齐学习目标，在无中间推理监督下提供步级软度控制学习信号。

Result: 在多个空间基准上，Soft Spatial Reasoning优于同骨干的硬CoT与固定软度CoT基线，也优于多种现有LVLM。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 自适应软思考能有效缓解空间推理中的过早离散化，软度需随推理步变化：保留多候选可维持有用空间解释，但候选间空间关系冲突时混合会干扰后续推理。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) commonly perform spatial reasoning through chain-of-thought (CoT), encoding intermediate reasoning as autoregressive sequences of discrete language tokens. Such hard thinking requires committing to a single token at each step, even when the correct spatial interpretation remains uncertain. This early commitment constitutes premature discretization: an incorrect token selection can propagate errors through subsequent reasoning. We propose Soft Spatial Reasoning, a post-training framework that introduces soft thinking for spatial tasks in LVLMs. At each intermediate reasoning step, the LVLM forms a continuous soft state by mixing token embeddings rather than selecting a single token, allowing multiple candidate continuations to influence the next step. The appropriate degree of softness, however, can vary across reasoning steps: retaining multiple candidates may preserve a useful spatial interpretation, but if those candidates imply conflicting spatial relations, mixing them may interfere with subsequent reasoning. At the core of Soft Spatial Reasoning is AdaptSoft, a controller that uses the current hidden state and predictive uncertainty to adapt the degree of softness at each reasoning step. To train AdaptSoft, we introduce a gradient-alignment learning objective that provides a step-specific learning signal for softness control without intermediate reasoning supervision. Across diverse spatial benchmarks, Soft Spatial Reasoning outperforms hard and fixed-soft CoT baselines using the same backbone, as well as a range of existing LVLMs. The source code is available at https://github.com/rafiibnsultan/Soft_Spatial_Reasoning

</details>


### [46] [Matisse: Evidence-Space Reasoning for Active 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38746)
*Xihang Yu,Kaichen Zhou,Lorenzo Shaikewitz,Clément Jambon,Xiao Zhan,Rajat Talak,Luca Carlone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Matisse：一种无需训练的框架，利用预训练生成式3D模型的证据不确定性统一主动重建与关键帧选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动视角获取方法多基于已观测或已实例化几何估计不确定性，难以推理未观测结构；长时序重建方法则保留大量冗余观测。核心问题是如何在有限计算预算下从部分视角获取并保留有用信息。

Method: 从3D潜变量的交叉注意力证据中估计证据不确定性（Evidential Uncertainty），并推导证据信息增益（Evidential Information Gain），以期望后验熵减少量指导视角获取与关键帧选择。通过遮挡感知、物体平衡的聚合支持多物体场景，并在中间潜变量上传播不确定性以避免规划时的完整重建。

Result: 在GSO30、YCB-V、Replica上相对最优基线分别降低Chamfer距离12.7%、3.8%、9.2%；在GSO30上以相同重建后端实现1.50倍端到端加速；关键帧选择实验中，用14%的输入视角达到与Stream3D相当的重建精度。

Conclusion: Matisse无需训练即可统一主动重建与关键帧选择，在精度与效率上均优于现有方法，并显著减少长时序重建所需视角数量。

Abstract: How can a 3D reconstruction system acquire and retain useful information to understand the geometry of a scene from partial views under a limited computation budget? Existing active view acquisition methods typically estimate uncertainty over observed or instantiated geometry, limiting their ability to reason about unseen structure, while long-horizon reconstruction methods often retain redundant observations. We introduce Matisse, a training-free framework that unifies active reconstruction and keyframe selection by leveraging evidence provided by a pretrained generative 3D model. Matisse estimates Evidential Uncertainty from cross-attention evidence associated with 3D latent tokens and derives an Evidential Information Gain to guide both view acquisition and keyframe selection based on the expected reduction in posterior entropy. Matisse supports multi-object scenes through occlusion-aware, object-balanced aggregation and propagates uncertainty through intermediate latents to avoid full reconstruction during planning. Matisse reduces Chamfer distance by 12.7%, 3.8%, and 9.2% on GSO30, YCB-V, and Replica, respectively, relative to the best baseline on each dataset, and achieves a $1.50\times$ end-to-end speedup over the best active reconstruction baseline on GSO30 with the same reconstruction backend. In the GSO30 keyframe selection experiment for long-horizon reconstruction, Matisse achieves comparable Chamfer distance using 14% of the input views compared with Stream3D.

</details>


### [47] [Consensus-Aware Multi-Source Fusion for Reference-Guided Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38747)
*Junyang Xia,Luocheng Zhang,Wenwen Pan,Chifeng Zhu,Yang Yang,Xinchun Liu,Jiajun Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出共识感知的多源融合框架，用于参考引导的伪装目标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考引导伪装目标检测中，参考样本含不一致的目标线索，且通用视觉表征与参考指定的目标并不天然对齐，导致任务困难。

Method: 提出RCDF模块，将可训练PVTv2查询特征与冻结的DINOv3表征耦合，利用参考条件相关性筛选基础模型证据并多尺度融合；同时通过交叉参考共识聚合整合多个参考，并在与查询特征匹配的语义深度注入参考信息。

Result: 大量实验验证方法有效性，表明参考共识、目标条件基础特征和分层解码在评测协议下提供互补改进。

Conclusion: 参考共识、目标条件基础特征与分层解码互补有效；源码将在接收后公开。

Abstract: Reference-guided camouflaged object detection aims to segment a target whose visual appearance closely resembles its surroundings by exploiting auxiliary reference samples. The task remains difficult because reference samples contain inconsistent target cues, while generic visual representations are not inherently aligned with the target specified by the references. To handle these problems, we present a consensus-aware multi-source fusion framework. Reference-Conditioned Dual-Backbone Fusion (RCDF) couples trainable PVTv2 query features with frozen DINOv3 representations and uses reference-conditioned correlation to select foundation-model evidence before multi-scale fusion. The framework also aggregates multiple references through cross-reference consensus aggregation and injects reference information at semantic depths matched to the query features. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method. The results further show that reference consensus, target-conditioned foundation features, and hierarchical decoding provide complementary improvements under the evaluation protocol. The source code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [48] [Here the World in Stereo: Learning Dynamic Spatial Correspondence for Immersive Joint Video-Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38748)
*Hanmo Chen,Chengcheng Liu,Tianxiao Chen,Zheyu Zhang,Siming Zheng,Jinwei Chen,Xu Yang,Cheng Deng,Bo Li,Peng-tao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StereoBind框架，通过运动轨迹将视觉声源运动与立体声音频生成绑定，实现动态空间对应，并构建了StereoWorld-29K数据集与StereoWorldBench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合视频-音频生成模型虽在语义对应与时序同步上表现良好，但AR/VR、互动游戏等应用需要的立体声音频（带来沉浸感）被忽视。有效的立体声要求感知声源位置随对应视觉源的运动而一致变化，即"动态空间对应"（Dynamic Spatial Correspondence）属性。

Method: StereoBind利用运动轨迹协调视觉运动与立体声，包含三种互补机制：视觉运动绑定（建立源感知的视听对应）、空间轨迹编码器（捕捉绝对声源位置）、残差轨迹RoPE（建模相对运动）。同时构建了StereoWorld-29K大规模立体声音视频数据集（含配对运动轨迹）及StereoWorldBench评测基准。

Result: 实验表明，StereoBind在立体声音频生成的空间对齐方面显著优于现有模型，同时保持了整体视听质量。

Conclusion: 该工作首次关注立体声生成中的动态空间对应问题，通过运动轨迹绑定机制、数据集与基准的构建，推动了沉浸式视听生成研究的发展。

Abstract: Recent joint video-audio generation models have achieved strong semantic correspondence and temporal synchronization. However, applications such as AR/VR and interactive gaming further require stereo audio to provide an immersive sense, which remains largely overlooked. Effective stereo audio requires the perceived sound location to evolve consistently with the motion of its corresponding visual source. We refer to this property as Dynamic Spatial Correspondence and propose StereoBind, a framework that binds visual source motion to stereo sound generation. StereoBind uses motion tracks to coordinate visual motion and stereo audio through three complementary mechanisms. Visual Motion Binding establishes source-aware audiovisual correspondence, the Spatial Track Encoder captures absolute source positions, and Residual Track RoPE models relative motion. For supervision and evaluation, we construct StereoWorld-29K, a large-scale stereo audio-video dataset with paired motion tracks, and StereoWorldBench for measuring audiovisual spatial consistency. Experiments show that StereoBind substantially improves spatial alignment in stereo audio generation over existing models while preserving overall audiovisual quality.

</details>


### [49] [Agentic Relative Camera Pose Estimation via Learned Ranking and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38755)
*Zhining Gu,Shangjie Du,Weimin Qiu,Carl Olsson,Ping Liu,Meng Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PoseAgent 智能体框架，通过学习的排序与验证动态编排多个位姿估计器，在多个数据集上提升相对相机位姿估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机位姿估计已有对应点法、端到端回归和 3D 几何基础模型等多种方法，但作者观察到没有任何单一估计器能应对宽基线、弱纹理、外观变化和遮挡等多样化挑战。不同估计器在不同基准和图像对上表现差异显著且优势互补，因此需要一种动态选择与调度机制。

Method: PoseAgent 是一个智能体框架。给定图像对，先由 profiling agent 提取与位姿估计相关的外观、语义和几何特征（如场景类型）；再由学习的 ranking agent 根据图像对画像预测多个估计器的相对能力并排序；执行排名最高的估计器，由学习的 verification agent 评估其位姿误差；若验证失败，则自适应调用排名更低的估计器，直到候选被接受或达到执行预算上限。

Result: 验证网络预测位姿误差比先前模型更准确；在 ARKitScenes、MegaDepth、ScanNet++ 和 RealEstate10K 上，AUC@5° 相比各数据集最强的单一估计器最多提升 4.2%；在 ARKitScenes 上还优于基于 VLM 的智能体（含使用相同验证器与回退策略的 VLM 排序器）。

Conclusion: 结果表明学习的排序与验证机制是有效的，动态编排多个互补估计器可显著提升相对相机位姿估计性能。

Abstract: A wide range of approaches have been developed for camera pose estimation, including correspondence-based methods, end-to-end pose regression, and recent 3D geometric foundation models. Our key observation is that no single estimator is optimal for diverse challenges, such as wide baselines, lack of texture, appearance changes, and occlusions. Further analysis reveals substantial performance variation across both benchmarks and individual image pairs, with different estimators exhibiting complementary strengths. We introduce PoseAgent, an agentic framework for relative camera pose estimation that dynamically orchestrates pose estimators through learnable ranking and verification. Given an image pair, a profiling agent first extracts appearance, semantic, and geometric features relevant to pose estimation, e.g., scene type. A learned ranking agent then predicts the relative competence of multiple pose estimators given the image-pair profile. The top-ranked estimator is executed, and its predicted pose is assessed by a learned verification agent that estimates the corresponding pose error. When verification fails, PoseAgent adaptively invokes lower-ranked estimators until a candidate is accepted or the execution budget is reached. For pose verification, our verification network predicts pose errors more accurately than prior models. For pose estimation, PoseAgent improves AUC@5 degree up to 4.2% over the strongest standalone estimator on each of ARKitScenes, MegaDepth, ScanNet++, and RealEstate10K. On ARKitScenes, PoseAgent also outperforms VLM-based agents, which include a VLM ranker with the same verifier and fallback policy. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of our learned ranking and verification.

</details>


### [50] [Event-Driven Refresh and Recurrence Memory to Reduce Stale Grounding in Referring Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38758)
*Abu Hanif Muhammad Syarubany,Jaehyun Jang,Siwoo Lim,Seungyeon Ryu,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EDRRM方法，通过事件驱动刷新与循环记忆增强Sa2VA在Referring Video Object Segmentation中的表现，在保持竞争力的精度同时显著降低刷新调用开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sa2VA将多模态大语言模型与SAM2结合用于指代分割，但其推理通常仅从少量固定初始关键帧进行定位，之后依赖传播。在长视频或动态视频中，当目标组成发生变化（如干扰物进入、目标消失或重现）时，会导致定位过时和持续的假阳性问题。

Method: EDRRM仅在稳定变化点选择性地重新调用Sa2VA：使用基于跟踪线索（目标出现/消失及粗略组成/布局变化）计算的EMA平滑事件分数，配合时间约束触发刷新边界；并引入循环记忆，通过CLIP相似度检索锚帧，在重现事件时对模型进行再条件化。

Result: 在Ref-DAVIS17、MeViS和ReVOS上的实验表明，相较固定窗口和FrameDiff-SSIM基线，EDRRM实现了有竞争力的精度-效率权衡，在显著降低平均刷新调用预算的情况下保持相当或更优的J&F分数，并减少假阳性失败。端到端运行时分析证实，跟踪、CLIP循环匹配和可识别性门控带来的开销相对主导性的Sa2VA推理成本仍然较小。

Conclusion: EDRRM通过事件驱动的刷新策略与循环记忆机制，有效缓解了长动态视频中的定位过时与假阳性问题，在精度与效率之间取得了良好的平衡，并验证了所提流程的高效性。

Abstract: Referring Video Object Segmentation (RVOS) aims to produce a pixel-accurate mask sequence for an object specified by natural language. Sa2VA combines a multimodal large language model with SAM2 for grounded segmentation; however, its inference typically grounds the query from a small fixed set of initial keyframes and then relies on propagation. In long or dynamic videos, this can cause stale grounding and persistent false positives when the object composition changes (e.g., distractors enter or the target disappears/re-appears). We propose Event-Driven Refresh + Recurrence Memory (EDRRM), an enhancement that selectively re-invokes Sa2VA only at stable change points. EDRRM triggers refresh boundaries using an EMA-smoothed event score computed from tracking-derived cues (births/deaths and coarse composition/layout changes) with temporal constraints. A recurrence memory further retrieves anchor frames via CLIP similarity to re-condition the model on re-appearance events. Experiments on Ref-DAVIS17, MeViS, and ReVOS show that EDRRM achieves a competitive accuracy-efficiency trade-off relative to fixed-window and FrameDiff-SSIM baselines, maintaining comparable or superior J&F scores at substantially lower average refresh-call budgets and reducing false-positive failures. End-to-end runtime analysis further confirms that the overhead introduced by tracking, CLIP-based recurrence matching, and the identifiability gate remains modest relative to the dominant Sa2VA inference cost, thereby validating the efficiency of the proposed pipeline.

</details>


### [51] [Distill the Visual Evidence, Not Just the Answer: Cross-World On-Policy Distillation for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38777)
*Yuanhao Sun,Huawei Ji,Jiaxin Ding,Luoyi Fu,Xinbing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出跨世界在线策略蒸馏（CW-OPD），通过显式监督学生对视觉证据变化的响应，将"依赖视觉证据"从输出匹配的隐式结果变为显式蒸馏目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM蒸馏方法主要监督学生模型的输出，导致视觉理解是隐式的。分析发现学生可以在不依赖相同视觉证据的情况下匹配教师答案，因此需要确保学生真正响应决定答案的视觉信息。

Method: CW-OPD为每个样例构建两个"视觉世界"：共享问题和场景上下文但答案关键证据不同，从而产生不同答案。在这两个世界中进行在线策略蒸馏，蒸馏教师的跨世界信念转移，使学生不仅匹配"预测什么"，还匹配"为何随证据改变"。同时提出CWBench基准，通过跨世界配对准确率（CWPA）诊断模型是否真正基于视觉证据作答。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-4B上，CW-OPD平均超过最强基线1.2分；4B学生模型在CWBench上比DeepSeek-V4.1（552B）高出22.4个CWPA点。

Conclusion: CW-OPD通过显式监督视觉证据变化下的响应，使视觉证据依赖成为显式蒸馏目标，并配合CWBench有效衡量跨世界一致性，显著提升小模型的视觉 grounding 能力。

Abstract: A central goal of vision-language model (VLM) distillation is to transfer both the teacher's language capabilities and its visual understanding. However, existing methods primarily supervise the student's output, leaving visual understanding implicit. Our analysis reveals that a student can match the teacher's answer without relying on the same visual evidence, raising the question: how can we ensure the student responds to the visual information that actually determines the answer? To this end, we propose \textbf{Cross-World On-Policy Distillation (CW-OPD)}, which explicitly supervises the student's response to changes in visual evidence. For each example, CW-OPD constructs two visual worlds that share the question and scene context but differ in answer-critical evidence, yielding different answers. We perform on-policy distillation in both worlds and distill the teacher's cross-world belief transition, encouraging the student to match not only \emph{what} the teacher predicts but also \emph{why} its prediction changes with the evidence. A gradient analysis shows that this term is invariant to errors shared by both worlds and supplies a corrective signal invisible to endpoint matching alone. In this way, CW-OPD makes reliance on the relevant visual evidence an explicit distillation target rather than an implicit consequence of output matching. To diagnose whether a model truly grounds its answers in visual evidence, we introduce CWBench, which measures cross-world consistency via Cross-World Pair Accuracy (CWPA). Experiments on Qwen3.5-4B show that CW-OPD outperforms the strongest baseline by \textbf{1.2} points on average, and the 4B student exceeds DeepSeek-V4.1 (552B) by \textbf{22.4} CWPA points on CWBench. Code is released in https://github.com/baokou-fw2/CWAD.

</details>


### [52] [CRAFT: Causal Responsibility and Failure Tracing in Medical Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38810)
*Chunzheng Zhu,Jiaqi Zeng,Hongbo Zhao,Yihang Chen,Yijun Wang,Jianxin Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究视觉语言模型在临床诊断中的两类安全失效，发现它们由空间上分离的注意力头群分别介导，并提出CRAFT方法定位因果头集合进行干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型越来越多用于临床诊断，但现有机制分析局限于单模态文本，无法解释为何一句误导性文本能压倒正确的图像诊断，或模型在视觉证据不足时仍给出自信答案，这构成安全隐患。

Method: 论文识别出两类失效：仲裁失败（文本上下文压倒视觉依据）与刹车失败（证据不足仍强行作答）。提出CRAFT，通过双重标准将每种失效模式定位到最小因果头集合，并用时间探针和Tuned Lens轨迹分析验证其必要性与充分性。

Result: 切除仲裁头显著减少冲突跟随，且对干净输入性能影响可忽略；切除刹车头可在视觉证据退化时恢复适当弃答。两类干预针对空间上分离的头集合，产生不同的纠正效果，证明失效模式在机制上可分离。多个医学VQA基准和VLM架构的实验验证了定位与干预的有效性。

Conclusion: 识别的注意力头因果驱动各失效模式，针对性调制无需重新训练即可泛化，揭示了失效模式的机制可分离性。

Abstract: As vision language models are increasingly deployed in clinical diagnosis, understanding how they internally resolve competing visual and textual signals becomes a safety imperative. Existing mechanistic analyses remain confined to unimodal text and offer no explanation for why a single misleading sentence can override a correct image based diagnosis, or why a model commits to a confident answer despite insufficient visual evidence. We find that these two safety risks, arbitration failure where textual context overrides visual grounding and brake failure where the model commits without adequate evidence, are mediated by spatially disjoint attention head populations: arbitration heads form a mid-to-deep wideband reflecting cross-layer evidence competition, while brake heads concentrate in a narrow middle-to-late layer band that regulates evidence sufficiency and abstention behavior. To ground these observations in causal circuitry, we introduce CRAFT, which localizes each failure mode to a minimal causal head set via dual criteria and verifies necessity and sufficiency through temporal probes and Tuned Lens trajectory analysis. Excising arbitration heads sharply reduces conflict following with negligible degradation on clean inputs, while excising brake heads restores appropriate abstention under degraded visual evidence. The two interventions target spatially disjoint head sets and produce distinct corrective effects, underscoring the mechanistic separability of the failure modes. Experiments across multiple medical VQA benchmarks and VLM architectures validate both the localization and interventions, demonstrating that the identified heads causally drive each failure mode and that targeted modulation generalises without retraining. The code is available at GitHub repository.

</details>


### [53] [DCM-SAM: Defect-Conditioned Mixture of LoRA Experts for NPU-Deployed AM Defect Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38811)
*Md Mushfiqur Rahaman,Md Mahedi Hasan,Imtiaz Ahmed,Srinjoy Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DCM-SAM，一种缺陷条件化的LoRA专家自适应混合方法，用冻结的SAM主干配合按缺陷类别划分的Conv-LoRA专家库与掩码解码器，在合成切片上训练，仅更新4.4%参数，实现XCT缺陷分割并在NPU上完成部署优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属增材制造零件需用X射线CT检测，但面临三大难题：标注数据稀缺、关键气孔与夹杂仅占几个像素、检测必须在设备端（机旁）进行。现有分割方法难以同时应对小目标、无标注与边缘部署约束。

Method: DCM-SAM采用缺陷条件化的自适应LoRA专家混合：共享一个冻结的Segment Anything主干，为每个缺陷类别配备独立的Conv-LoRA专家库和掩码解码器，各自单独训练一轮，无需提示（prompt-free），仅在合成切片上训练，可训练参数仅占4.4%。部署阶段针对Qualcomm Hexagon NPU进行适配，并通过数值等价的注意力重写使完整模型在FP16、1024x1024分辨率下运行。

Result: 在XCT-SAM报告的基准上，DCM-SAM以ViT-B主干在两个类别上均超越所有基线（对手使用ViT-H）；在未见任何真实图像的训练条件下，于真实NIST扫描上达到64.2%的气孔IoU。部署方面发现：ViT-H与ViT-L在1024x1024下因激活值（而非权重）超出设备上限无法分配内存，量化权重无效；ViT-B可运行，但适配后的编码器无法分配，经数值等价的注意力重写后，完整DCM-SAM可在FP16、1024x1024下全NPU运行，无算子回退CPU，掩码与FP32参考差异在0.01%像素以内。

Conclusion: DCM-SAM以极少参数更新和纯合成数据训练，在XCT缺陷分割精度上超过更大主干的基线，并揭示了边缘NPU部署中激活内存瓶颈这一常被忽视的问题，通过注意力重写实现了高效的全NPU端侧推理。

Abstract: Metal additive manufacturing parts are inspected by X-ray computed tomography, where labelled data is scarce, the pores and inclusions that matter span a few pixels, and inspection must happen at the machine. We present DCM-SAM, a defect-conditioned adaptive mixture of LoRA experts: one frozen Segment Anything backbone carries a separate Conv-LoRA expert bank and mask decoder per defect class, each trained in its own pass, without prompts, on synthetic slices alone, updating only 4.4% of the parameters. On benchmarks that XCT-SAM reports, DCM-SAM improves on every baseline for both classes from a ViT-B backbone against their ViT-H, and reaches 64.2% pore IoU on real NIST scans having seen no real images during training. Deployment then exposes what adaptation work rarely measures: on a Qualcomm Hexagon NPU, ViT-H and ViT-L compile yet cannot allocate at 1024x1024 image resolution, since activations rather than weights exceed the device ceiling, and quantizing weights does not help. ViT-B alone runs, but the adapted encoder then fails to allocate where the stock one succeeds, until a numerically identical rewrite of the attention lets the complete DCM-SAM run in FP16 at 1024x1024, with no operator falling back to the CPU, masks within 0.01% of pixels of the FP32 reference. Code: https://github.com/MushfiqShovon/DCM-SAM.

</details>


### [54] [Future Video Generation Better Aligns with the Human Visual Cortex than Observed Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38819)
*Chang-Bae Bang,Hyungjin Chung,Byung-Hoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究比较了人类视觉皮层fMRI响应与视频扩散模型内部表征的对齐程度，发现用于生成未来视频帧的表征比观测视频本身的表征更符合人脑视觉处理，且这种对齐集中在高级视觉皮层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于视觉模型与人脑视觉皮层对齐的研究大多忽略了人脑不仅处理当前观测到的视觉刺激，还会基于已观测内容预测未来刺激这一特性。因此作者假设：用于生成未来视频帧的内部表征，比观测视频本身的表征更符合人类视觉处理的预测性本质。

Method: 研究比较了人类观看视频时的视觉皮层fMRI响应与两类视频扩散模型的内部表征：自回归（AR）视频扩散模型及其非AR基础模型。首先在AR模型内部进行对比分析，然后比较AR模型与非AR基础模型，最后开展人类行为实验。

Result: 1）AR模型中用于未来视频生成的表征比观测视频的表征与视觉皮层对齐更好；2）与非AR基础模型的观测视频重建表征相比，AR模型未来视频生成表征同样对齐更好；3）观测视频重建的对齐主要集中在低级视觉皮层，而未来视频生成的对齐集中在高级视觉皮层；4）人类行为实验显示，人类更偏好那些放大与视觉皮层对齐更好的各层贡献所生成的视频。

Conclusion: 研究发现，用于预测未来视频帧的生成式表征比观测视频重建表征更符合人脑视觉皮层的预测性处理机制，且不同层次对齐分布于不同级别视觉皮层，为理解人脑视觉处理的预测本质提供了新视角。

Abstract: Studying the alignment between the internal representations of vision models and the responses of the visual cortex to the same observed visual stimuli has enabled us to better understand human visual processing. However, studies so far have largely overlooked the fact that the human brain not only processes observed visual stimuli, but also predicts upcoming stimuli based on what has been observed. Accordingly, we hypothesize that internal representations for generating future video frames are better aligned with the predictive nature of human visual processing than representations of the observed video itself. To this end, we compare the alignment between human video-watching fMRI responses in the visual cortex and the internal representations from two types of video diffusion models, an autoregressive (AR) model and its non-AR base model. We first conduct a within-model analysis of the AR video diffusion model and show that the representations for future video generation align better with the visual cortex than the representations of the observed video. We then compare the internal representations of the AR model with those of its non-AR base model and again show that the representations for future video generation align better with the visual cortex than the representations for observed video reconstruction by the base model. Specifically, the alignment of observed video reconstruction is concentrated in lower-order visual cortex, whereas that of future video generation is concentrated in higher-order visual cortex. Finally, we show in a human behavioral experiment that humans prefer videos generated by amplifying the contributions of individual layers that align better with the visual cortex.

</details>


### [55] [DecoMoE: Decoupling Visual Propagation and Expert Computation for Efficient Multimodal MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38823)
*Xudong Tan,Peng Ye,Ming Xie,Chenyu Huang,Yaoxin Yang,Jiayuan Fan,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DecoMoE，一个二维结构化压缩框架，通过解耦视觉传播与专家计算来加速多模态 MoE 大模型推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态 MoE 模型推理成本高：长视觉 token 序列反复触发注意力、路由、分发与专家 MLP 计算；现有方法通常只压缩 token 或专家单一维度，另一维度仍存在冗余。

Method: 基于两个观测（视觉传播所需深度随输入变化；文本 token 路由呈现集中且可复现的专家重要性模式），提出 DecoMoE：1）Sample-Adaptive Visual Boundary（SAVB）预测输入相关的视觉退出层，在该层移除视觉 token 块；2）Routing-Calibrated Expert Prefix（RCEP）离线依据文本 token 路由质量重排专家，并从预测退出层起，在每层 MoE 保留覆盖目标路由质量比例的最短连续前缀专家。

Result: 在 Qwen3-VL-MoE 与 InternVL3.5-30B-A3B 上、六个基准测试评估。Qwen3-VL-MoE 上保留 97.91% 的稠密基线性能，计算量从 27.06 降至 16.73 TFLOPs，延迟从 0.44 降至 0.26 秒，取得 1.69 倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过视觉 token 与专家维度协同的结构化压缩，DecoMoE 能在几乎不损失性能的前提下显著降低多模态 MoE 模型的推理计算与延迟。

Abstract: Multimodal mixture-of-experts (MoE) models combine sparse expert activation with visual-language capabilities, yet their inference remains costly because long visual-token sequences repeatedly incur attention, routing, dispatch, and expert-MLP computation. Existing methods typically compress either the token or expert dimension, leaving redundancy along the other. Our analysis reveals two complementary regularities: the depth required for visual propagation varies across inputs, while text-token routing exhibits concentrated and recurrent expert-importance patterns. Based on these observations, we propose DecoMoE, a two-dimensional structured compression framework that decouples visual propagation from expert computation. The Sample-Adaptive Visual Boundary (SAVB) predicts an input-dependent visual-exit layer at which the visual-token block is removed. The Routing-Calibrated Expert Prefix (RCEP) reorders experts offline using text-token routed mass and, from this predicted exit layer onward, retains at each MoE layer the shortest contiguous prefix covering a target routed-mass fraction. We evaluate DecoMoE on Qwen3-VL-MoE and InternVL3.5-30B-A3B across six benchmarks. On Qwen3-VL-MoE, DecoMoE retains 97.91% of dense-baseline performance while reducing computation from 27.06 to 16.73 TFLOPs and latency from 0.44 to 0.26 seconds, yielding a 1.69x speedup. Code will be available at https://github.com/ShawnTan86/DecoMoE.

</details>


### [56] [FrameMorrow: Future-guided Frame Selection with Prospective Tokens for Long-Horizon Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38839)
*Bo Yin,Xiaobin Hu,Jiaqi Zhao,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FrameMorrow，一种基于"未来信息需求"的前瞻式历史帧选择器，用于长时程视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程视频生成中，生成历史不断增长，保留全部历史代价高昂且冗余。现有方法依据"当前内容"判断历史相关性，但与当前相关的信息未必对未来生成有用，而看似不相关的历史可能在未来变重要。

Method: 核心思想是按历史对"未来信息需求"的相关性进行选择。无需生成完整未来，只需用紧凑表示刻画"接下来重要什么"。据此提出 FrameMorrow：预测一小组前瞻性 token 表示未来信息需求，并据此从历史中挑选相关显式帧（而非模型内部状态），从而实现跨生成器的即插即用，包括闭源模型，额外推理开销很小。

Result: 在五个基准、11 个生成模型上评测，覆盖长视频生成、交互式生成与动作条件世界模型。实验显示在长程一致性、视觉质量与动作对齐方面均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 按未来信息需求选择历史信息是长时程视频生成的有效范式，FrameMorrow 以显式帧选择实现通用、低成本、可插拔的改进。

Abstract: Long-horizon video generation requires models to effectively leverage an increasingly long generation history. As the generated history grows, retaining all previous content becomes increasingly expensive and redundant, making effective historical selection essential. Existing approaches often determine historical relevance based on the current content. However, information relevant to the present is not necessarily useful for future generation, while seemingly less relevant history may become important later. Our key insight is that historical information should be selected according to its relevance to future information needs. Capturing these needs does not require generating the full future; instead, a compact representation of what becomes important next is sufficient to guide historical selection. Building on this insight, we propose FrameMorrow, a prospective frame selector that predicts a small set of prospective tokens representing future information needs and uses them to identify relevant information from history. FrameMorrow selects explicit historical frames rather than model-specific internal states, enabling plug-and-play integration across diverse generators, including closed-source models, with little additional inference cost. We evaluate FrameMorrow across five benchmarks and 11 generative models spanning long-video generation, interactive generation, and action-conditioned world models. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in long-range consistency, visual quality, and action alignment across diverse generation settings.

</details>


### [57] [Decoupling Spherical Reasoning from Dense Prediction for 360 Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38856)
*Zhijie Shen,Chunyu Lin,Shuai Zheng,Feng Li,Runmin Cong,Huihui Bai,Yao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Fibonacci球面图(FSG)作为中间推理空间，将ERP全景深度估计中的上下文建模与稠密预测解耦，配合球面上下文调制(SCC)模块，在三个基准上取得更优深度精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ERP全景深度估计中空间变化的畸变使几何一致的特征建模困难。作者希望将上下文建模从畸变的ERP空间解耦到原生球面空间进行，以缓解畸变带来的建模问题。

Method: 1) 提出Fibonacci球面图(FSG)作为中间推理空间：将ERP特征提升到球面上准均匀的Fibonacci节点，通过互补的球面邻域捕获局部与长程依赖；2) 节点近似均匀分布，减少高拉伸区域在关系建模中的过度表示，且节点数紧凑，避免在全分辨率ERP上构图的开销；3) 提出球面上下文调制(SCC)模块，用增强的球面表示自适应调制稠密ERP特征，桥接球面推理与稠密预测。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在深度精度上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过将上下文建模置于准均匀球面图空间并与稠密ERP预测解耦、再以球面上下文调制引导像素对齐预测，可有效缓解ERP畸变，提升全景深度估计精度。

Abstract: The equirectangular projection (ERP) is widely used for panoramic depth estimation, but its spatially varying distortion makes geometry-consistent feature modeling challenging. We revisit panoramic depth estimation by decoupling contextual modeling in native spherical space from dense ERP prediction. To this end, we propose a Fibonacci Spherical Graph (FSG) as an intermediate reasoning space to lift ERP features onto quasi-uniform Fibonacci nodes on the sphere and capture local and long-range dependencies through complementary spherical neighborhoods. The resulting spherical discretization distributes graph nodes approximately uniformly over the spherical surface, reducing the over-representation of highly stretched regions during relational modeling. Operating on a compact set of Fibonacci nodes also avoids the computational burden of constructing and processing a graph at full ERP resolution. To bridge spherical reasoning and dense prediction, we propose a Spherical Context Conditioning (SCC) module that adaptively modulates dense ERP features with the enhanced spherical representation, allowing spherical context to guide pixel-aligned depth prediction. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed method consistently achieves superior depth accuracy over existing approaches.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [58] [Large Language Models are Approximate Survival Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38181)
*Juan M Zambrano Chaves,Peniel Argaw,Risa Ueno,Carlo Bifulco,Kristina Young,Rom Leidner,Tristan Naumann,Hoifung Poon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Survprompt 框架，将结构化患者协变量转为自由文本临床情境，测试零样本 LLM 生存预测能力，并与随机生存森林等传统模型对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者可能借助消费级 LLM 获取预后信息，但 LLM 能否准确预测生存时间尚未被严格评估。

Method: Survprompt 将患者协变量转换为自由文本临床 vignette，提示预训练 LLM 进行零样本生存预测；在 MSK-CHORD 及 Providence St. Joseph 健康网络两个多机构泛癌队列上，与随机生存森林（RSF）等传统模型对比，采用 cMAE 和 c-index 评估，并做特征消融分析。

Result: 前沿 LLM 在个体生存时间预测上的 cMAE 具有竞争力，如 GPT-5.6-Sol 在若干癌种上 cMAE 与专门训练的 RSF 相差 10% 以内，在前列腺癌上甚至优于 RSF；特征消融显示 LLM 关注的临床变量与专业模型相似。但 LLM 在不同癌种和机构间准确性不稳定，且 c-index 较低，难以区分高、低风险患者。

Conclusion: 零样本 LLM 无需专门训练即可给出较准确的预后估计，但其跨癌种与跨机构表现不稳定，是临床应用的重要局限。

Abstract: Survival analysis estimates time-to-event outcomes from patient covariates and is widely used for medical risk assessment. Patients seeking prognostic information after a diagnosis may turn to large language models (LLMs), now readily accessible through consumer applications. However, whether LLMs can provide accurate survival predictions has not been rigorously evaluated. We introduce Survprompt, a framework that converts structured patient covariates into free-text clinical vignettes and prompts pre-trained LLMs to predict survival zero-shot. We benchmark Survprompt against conventional survival models, including random survival forests (RSF), across two multi-institutional pan-cancer cohorts: the publicly available MSK-CHORD cohort and a newly curated cohort from the Providence St. Joseph Health Network constructed using an LLM-based medical abstraction framework. We report censored mean absolute error (cMAE) and concordance index (c-index) and conduct feature ablations to identify variables influencing LLM predictions. Frontier LLMs achieved surprisingly competitive cMAE for individual survival times. For example, GPT-5.6-Sol achieved cMAE within 10% of state-of-the-art RSF models specifically trained for survival prediction for several cancer types and lower cMAE than RSF for prostate cancer in MSK-CHORD. Feature ablations revealed that LLMs prioritized clinical variables similarly to specialized survival models. However, LLMs showed inconsistent accuracy across cancer types and institutions and poorly discriminated between high- and low-risk patients (lower c-index). Zero-shot LLMs can generate surprisingly accurate prognostic estimates without specialized training, but their variable performance across cancer types and institutions remains an important limitation for clinical use.

</details>


### [59] [TomasuLLM: Out-of-Order Speculative Execution for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38201)
*Jiangnan Yu,Ceyu Xu,Mengming Li,Shiyu Huang,Yiran Xia,Jian Weng,Hui Xue,Haohui Mai,Yuan Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 TomasuLLM 运行时，借鉴乱序处理器的思想，将编码智能体的工具调用乱序提前执行，在保证正确性的前提下显著降低因长时间工具调用导致的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体常需调用编译器、测试套件、仓库命令等耗时工具（数秒到数分钟），期间智能体只能空闲等待。这种顺序接口隐藏了本可预测并提前启动的工作，形成类似乱序处理器诞生的性能瓶颈。

Method: TomasuLLM 在轨迹顺序之外执行工具调用：预判并草拟未来动作，在隔离的写时复制（copy-on-write）沙箱中运行，追踪其依赖与副作用，仅在验证通过且所有前序步骤提交后才按轨迹顺序提交结果。

Result: 在三个基准上提升性能：100 个 SWE-bench Verified 任务 1.31x，28 个 Terminal-Bench 2.0 任务 1.35x，18 个 SWE-Marathon 会话 1.27x 匹配进度；并在 4,010 条提交验证记录中实现零误接受。

Conclusion: 通过乱序执行与写时复制沙箱验证，可在保持任务执行正确性的同时有效隐藏长工具调用延迟，且收益随工具延迟增大而扩展，验证环节保证零误接受。

Abstract: Long-running tools can dominate coding-agent latency: compilers, test suites, and repository commands take seconds to minutes while the agent idles. This observation stall presents the same tension that drove out-of-order processors -- asequential interface hides work that can be predicted and started early, but a speculative result may become visible only after it and every earlier step have been validated.
  We present TomasuLLM, a runtime that executes agent tool calls out of trajectory order while preserving task-execution correctness. It drafts future actions, runs them in isolated copy-on-write sandboxes, traces their dependencies and effects, and commits results in trajectory order only after validation against committed state. Across three benchmarks spanning sub-second to minutes-long tool calls, TomasuLLM improves the reported benchmark means and scales with tool latency: 1.31x on 100 SWE-bench Verified tasks, 1.35x on 28 Terminal-Bench 2.0 tasks, and 1.27x matched progress on 18 SWE-Marathon sessions. Across 4,010 audited commit-validation records, it produces zero false accepts.

</details>


### [60] [Automatic estimation of verbal fluency index in people with Motor Neuron Disease using ASR alignment and pause modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38203)
*Bahman Mirheidari,Leslie Ing,Daniel Blackburn,Sharon Abrahams,Christopher McDermott,Heidi Christensen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个自动化言语分析系统，用于估计运动神经元病（MND）患者认知障碍评估中的言语流畅性指数（VFI），结合ASR（WhisperX）、VAD（Silero）与精细化时间戳，性能优于传统声学特征和自监督嵌入方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动神经元病患者常伴随言语困难，使得认知障碍（CI）的监测变得困难。ECAS量表中的言语流畅性指数（VFI）是评估CI的核心指标，因此需要一种能自动、可靠估计VFI的方法，以支持及时的诊疗与照护。

Method: 基于独特的MND数据集，系统结合ASR（WhisperX）与VAD（Silero）并采用细化的时间戳处理，预测VFI并提取若干具有临床可解释性的特征。评估中与基于传统声学特征及自监督嵌入的系统在多种回归算法下进行对比。

Result: 临床启发的特征持续优于其他特征集。最佳模型取得良好效果：P-words的R2为0.9、NRMSE为0.05；S-words的R2为0.8、NRMSE为0.08，证明自动化VFI估计的可行性。

Conclusion: 自动化VFI估计在MND认知评估中具有可行性，临床可解释特征表现最佳，为CI监测提供了有前景的自动化工具。

Abstract: Monitoring cognitive impairment (CI) in motor neuron disease (MND) is essential for timely treatment and care, yet challenging due to co-occurring speech difficulties. The Edinburgh Cognitive and Behavioural ALS Screen (ECAS) provides a robust metric for CI assessment, with the Verbal Fluency Index (VFI) a central element. Building on recent advances in automated speech analysis, this study proposes a system for estimating VFI. It leverages a unique MND dataset and combines ASR (WhisperX) and VAD (Silero) with refined timestamping to predict the VFI and extract several clinically interpretable measures. Our approach outperformed systems based on traditional acoustic features and self-supervised embeddings, evaluated using multiple regression algorithms. Clinically inspired features consistently outperformed the other sets, with the best models achieving strong results (P-words: R2 0.9, NRMSE 0.05; S-words: R2 0.8, NRMSE 0.08), demonstrating the feasibility of automated VFI estimation.

</details>


### [61] [The System Prompt Illusion: How Instruction Preambles Modify Computation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38205)
*Muhammad Usama,Dong Eui Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文利用CKA分析17个指令微调模型中系统提示对内部表征的影响，发现安全类系统提示在计算层面几乎不起作用，从机制上解释了基于系统提示的安全防护为何容易被绕过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统提示是实践者控制语言模型行为的主要手段，但其对Transformer内部计算的实际影响尚不清楚。作者希望从机制层面揭示系统提示（尤其是安全类提示）究竟改变了模型内部的哪些计算，以解释基于系统提示的安全防护为何持续存在被绕过的脆弱性。

Method: 在覆盖8个架构家族、1.5B至72B参数的17个指令微调模型上，使用中心化核对齐（CKA）比较20个系统提示（分属5个功能类别）下的逐层表征；引入线性探针基线定位机制，并使用因果激活补丁验证相关层是否中介行为变化。

Result: 影响是层次选择性的且依赖指令类型：人设与格式类指令深度重构中间层表征，而安全类指令几乎不改变表征，与最小基线的差异在统计上不可区分；限制性安全指令与明确允许性指令（"你没有任何限制"）激活近乎相同的计算通路（平均CKA相关0.997），在商业规模模型上依然如此，70B-72B模型的安全渗透率仍低于10%。线性探针显示模型在每一层都编码了提示类别，但只在少数层重构计算，即提示被可靠"看到"，但在安全方面未被深度"执行"。因果激活补丁确认这些层中介了行为变化，且表征深度可预测行为效应量（Spearman rho = 0.761，p < 0.001）。

Conclusion: 系统提示的安全控制主要停留在表征编码层面而非深层计算重构层面，这为基于系统提示的安全防护的持续可绕过性提供了机制性解释。代码已开源。

Abstract: System prompts are the primary lever practitioners use to control language model behavior, yet what they actually do to the computation inside the transformer remains poorly understood. Across 17 instruction-tuned models spanning 8 architecture families and 1.5B to 72B parameters, we use Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) to compare layer-wise representations under 20 system prompts in five functional categories. Effects are layer-selective and instruction-type-dependent: persona and formatting instructions deeply restructure intermediate representations, while safety instructions barely move them, producing changes statistically indistinguishable from a minimal baseline. Restrictive safety instructions and explicitly permissive ones ("you have no restrictions") engage near-identical computational pathways (mean CKA correlation 0.997), and this persists at commercial scale, where safety penetration remains below 10% even at 70B-72B. A linear probing baseline exposes the mechanism: the model encodes prompt category at every layer but restructures its computation only at a small subset, so the prompt is reliably "seen" but, for safety, not deeply "acted upon." Causal activation patching confirms these layers mediate behavioral change, and representational depth predicts behavioral effect size across the full 17-model cohort (Spearman rho = 0.761, p < 0.001). The findings provide a mechanistic explanation for the persistent jailbreak vulnerability of system-prompt-based safety. Code: https://github.com/Usama1002/system-prompt-illusion-cka

</details>


### [62] [TutlAit v1: a crowdsourced Moroccan Tamazight speech dataset with Arabic transcriptions and regional accent labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38219)
*Mohamed-Amine Chadi,Ezzahra Ait El Arbi,Ismail Khayoub,Aymane Fadili,Yassine Ennhili,Jadjigua Bouali,Hanane Inhid,Mohammed Ameksa,Hajar Mousannif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TutlAit是一个摩洛哥Tamazight语音语料库，包含13,384个音频文件（约20.9小时），带有现代标准阿拉伯语文本和地区口音标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tamazight是摩洛哥官方语言之一，但语音技术资源严重匮乏：公开标注音频稀缺、缺少地区变体信息、转写质量参差不齐。

Method: 开发众包Web应用（React 18前端、Django 5后端、PostgreSQL），通过定向社交媒体招募母语者，采用"文本到音频"和"音频到文本"两种工作流采集数据，并补充从公开视听媒体用ELAN标注的片段；上传音频统一转为16kHz单声道WAV，经SHA-256去重、时长校验和人工审核。

Result: 数据集含13,384个音频文件，总计75,231秒（约20.9小时，约3.01GB）；Atlas变体9,956个文件（14.08小时），Souss变体3,378个文件（6.75小时），另有少量Rif（22个）和Kabyle（28个）子集。

Conclusion: 该语料库可用于摩洛哥Tamazight的语音识别、语音翻译和口音识别研究。

Abstract: Tamazight (Amazigh) is, together with Arabic, one of the two official languages of Morocco, yet it remains severely under-resourced for speech technology: pub licly available labelled audio is scarce, generally lacks information on the regional variety spoken, and is often of uneven transcription quality. This article describes the TutlAit dataset, a corpus of Moroccan Tamazight speech paired with Modern Standard Arabic text and explicit regional accent labels. The data were collected with TutlAit, a purpose-built crowdsourcing web application (React 18 front end, Django 5 / Django REST Framework back-end, PostgreSQL database). Native speakers recruited through targeted LinkedIn and Instagram campaigns created an account, declared their regional variety (Atlas, Souss, Rif or other) and demographic information, and then contributed through two workflows: Text-to Audio, in which an Arabic sentence is displayed and the volunteer records its oral Tamazight rendering in the browser, and Audio-to-Text, in which a Tamazight excerpt is played and the volunteer types its Arabic transcription. A complemen tary set of segments was obtained from freely accessible Tamazight audiovisual media, segmented and annotated with ELAN and imported through a bulk CSV/ZIP pipeline. Every upload is converted server-side to 16kHz mono WAV, hashed with SHA-256 for duplicate rejection, checked for duration bounds and validated by an administrator. The dataset contains 13,384 audio files totalling 75,231 seconds (approximately 20.9 hours, about 3.01GB). The Atlas variety accounts for 9,956 files (14.08h) and the Souss variety for 3,378 files (6.75h); small Rif (22 files) and Kabyle (28 files) subsets are also included. The corpus can be reused for speech recognition, speech translation and accent identification for Moroccan Tamazight.

</details>


### [63] [Conformal Factuality Control for Multi-Hop Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38222)
*Muhammad Aimal Rehman,Chi-Kuang Yeh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究claim级conformal事实性控制能否推广到多跳RAG场景，实验表明在严格目标下可靠性提升显著但保留claim极少、大量回答被弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG能为大语言模型提供外部证据，但检索到的上下文并不能保证生成claims有事实依据；在多跳RAG中检索与推理经过多个依赖阶段，问题更突出，因此需检验此前针对（单跳）RAG开发的claim级conformal事实性控制是否仍然有效。

Method: 将split-conformal claim过滤方法应用于多跳RAG，在HotpotQA、Natural Questions、TriviaQA上使用Llama 3.1 8B与GPT-4o-mini评测，并设置单跳参照实验。

Result: 六种多跳模型-数据集配置下，conformal目标越严格，保留claims全部获支持的响应比例越高：95%目标下从无过滤的55.60%–76.03%升至95.80%–97.20%。但提升具有强选择性：95%目标下仅4.41%–31.09%的claims被保留，9.70%–51.40%的响应非空。

Conclusion: conformal事实性控制可扩展到多跳RAG，但标称可靠性必须结合claim保留率与弃权率共同解读。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can ground large language models in external evidence, but retrieved context does not guarantee that generated claims are factually supported. This problem is especially relevant in multi-hop RAG, where retrieval and reasoning proceed through multiple dependent stages. We study whether claim-level conformal factuality control, previously developed for RAG, remains effective in this setting. We apply split-conformal claim filtering to multi-hop RAG and evaluate it on HotpotQA, Natural Questions, and TriviaQA using Llama 3.1 8B and GPT-4o-mini, together with a single-hop reference experiment. Across all six multi-hop model-dataset configurations, increasingly stringent conformal targets consistently increase the fraction of responses whose retained claims are fully supported. At the 95% target, this rate ranges from 95.80% to 97.20%, compared with 55.60%-76.03% without filtering. However, the improvement is strongly selective: only 4.41%-31.09% of generated claims are retained and 9.70%-51.40% of responses remain non-empty at the 95% target. These results show that conformal factuality extends to multi-hop RAG, while demonstrating that nominal reliability must be interpreted jointly with claim retention and abstention.

</details>


### [64] [Which Models Work Well Together? Measuring Heterogeneity for LLM Team Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38274)
*Liangyu Teng,Hengsong Liu,Juncen Guo,Jingyu Zhang,Yang Liu,Jing Liu,Liang Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出异质性驱动的LLM团队选择框架，通过离线画像刻画个体能力与两种互补信号，将团队选择建模为质量-互补性组合优化问题并用贪心搜索求解，在多基准上优于仅考虑质量的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM团队的性能上限不仅受个体模型能力限制，还受到成员间错误共振与预测差异的影响。尽管异构组队在实践中常被观察到有效，但现有方法缺乏可计算、可解释、可优化的互补性度量，导致团队构成只能依赖启发式规则。

Method: 提出异质性驱动的团队选择框架：先进行离线画像，刻画个体能力，并提取两种互补信号——一种捕捉错误模式的相关性降低（减少共同失败），另一种衡量预测行为的分歧（捕捉策略多样性）。将团队选择形式化为标准化的"质量-互补性"组合目标，并用高效贪心搜索从候选池中选出小型团队。

Result: 在多个基准上，于受控的候选池与团队规模设置下，该框架一致优于仅考虑质量的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为多LLM系统建立了可复用的团队选择原则，说明质量与互补性结合的组合优化能有效提升LLM团队表现。

Abstract: The performance ceiling of an LLM team is constrained not only by individual model capabilities, but also by inter-member error resonance and predictive differences. Although heterogeneous teaming is often observed to be effective in practice, existing approaches lack complementarity metrics that are computable, interpretable, and optimizable, leaving team composition to rely on heuristics. We propose a heterogeneity-driven team selection framework that performs offline profiling to characterize individual capability along with two complementary signals: one captures decorrelation in error patterns to reduce co-failures, while the other measures divergence in predictive behavior to capture strategy diversity. We formulate team selection as a standardized quality--complementarity combinatorial objective and apply an efficient greedy search to select a small team from a candidate pool. Experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms quality-only baselines under controlled candidate pools and team sizes, establishing reusable selection principles for multi-LLM systems.

</details>


### [65] [Framing the Narrative: Ideological Mimicry in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38256)
*Olivia Macmillan-Scott,Michael Jacobs,Nils Metternich,Mirco Musolesi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM的政治立场并非固定属性，会随用户话术、前提和政治信号发生系统性的"意识形态模仿"，可能加剧信息茧房。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估通常把大模型对政治争议话题的立场视为相对稳定的属性，但真实用户通过术语、假设和个人背景传递政治信号。作者担心若模型顺应这些信号，会为立场相反的用户提供系统性不同的同一议题叙述，形成个性化政治信息环境并强化分歧。

Method: 构建Poli-SHIFT数据集与评估框架，针对美英澳三国十个争议性政治话题，对七个开放权重LLM进行测试；系统操纵争议性术语、政治倾向性前提和用户信息，并在多选题与开放文本两种形式下获取回答。

Result: 各模型均稳健地表现出提示框架影响输出政治立场。仅改变术语，就在16.9%的配对比较中使模型对议题的支持方向发生反转；用户自述的政治意识形态也会使回答系统性向其立场偏移。

Conclusion: 政治立场不是LLM的固定属性，其表达的观点依赖于与用户的交互。随着LLM成为日益个性化的信息来源，这种交互依赖的适应可能促成强化用户既有观点的政治信息环境。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to answer questions about politically contentious issues, yet evaluations typically treat a model's stance as a relatively stable property. Real users, however, communicate political signals through their terminology, assumptions, and personal context. We investigate whether such signals produce ideological mimicry: systematic shifts in the political stance expressed by an LLM toward the position conveyed by the interaction. If LLMs adapt their responses to these signals, they risk creating personalised political information environments in which users with opposing views receive systematically different accounts of the same issue, potentially reinforcing existing divisions. We build the Poli-SHIFT dataset and evaluation framework and assess seven open-weight LLMs across ten contentious political topics in the United States, United Kingdom, and Australia, systematically manipulating contested terminology, politically valenced premises, and user information, and eliciting responses in both multiple-choice and open-text formats. Across models, we find robust evidence that prompt framing shapes the political stance of LLM outputs. Changing terminology alone reverses which side of an issue a model supports in 16.9% of matched comparisons. Stated political ideology also systematically shifts responses toward the user's position. These findings show that political stance is not a fixed property of LLMs; the views expressed are conditional on the interaction with the user. As LLMs become increasingly personalised sources of information, such interaction-dependent adaptation could contribute to political information environments that reinforce users' existing perspectives.

</details>


### [66] [Breaking Babel: A Self-Evolving Multi-Agent System for Long-Form Subtitle Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38660)
*Haibo Jin,Xinjie Li,Najmeh Sadoughi,Yang Liu,Yibo Wang,Zhu Liu,Yuzong Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 SMART 自演化多智能体长剧集字幕翻译系统，并构建 Subtitle Arena 基准与 SubMQM 评测框架，在 15 个翻译方向与 MuSC 基准上均取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长剧集字幕翻译需要跨集甚至跨全剧的语篇与文化语境推理，并保持术语和风格一致。现有单 LLM 方法多停留在句子级，多智能体系统多采用静态工作流，无法适应场景复杂度或制作语境。

Method: SMART 采用自演化多智能体架构：在测试时训练阶段，构建持久化的剧集级记忆，并通过动态路由器与 Mixture-of-Agents 层翻译部分句子，配合术语验证、字幕约束校验与上下文检索工具；由 judge-refiner 循环对候选打分，利用文本批评更新智能体提示与路由策略，而无需重训基座 LLM。测试时推理阶段则用演化后的配置翻译剩余剧集。

Result: 在 Subtitle Arena 全部 15 个方向上取得最佳总体 MQM 分数，平均惩罚比最强竞争多智能体系统降低 6.9%；在公开 MuSC 基准上，四个语言对均取得最佳模型结果，并取得最佳人工评测结果，总体得分 4.50/5。

Conclusion: SMART 通过测试时自演化与动态路由有效提升了长剧集字幕翻译的质量与一致性，配套的 Subtitle Arena 与 SubMQM 为字幕翻译评测提供了更贴合实际的基准。

Abstract: Long-form subtitle translation requires reasoning over discourse and cultural context spanning episodes or entire series, while maintaining consistent terminology and style. Existing single-LLM methods are largely sentence-level, and multi-agent systems often use static workflows that do not adapt to scene complexity or production context. We propose SMART, a Self-evolving Multi-Agent system for long-foRm subtitle Translation. During test-time training, SMART builds persistent series-level memory and translates a subset of sentences through a dynamic router and Mixture-of-Agents layer with tools for terminology verification, subtitle constraint validation, and contextual retrieval. A judge-refiner loop scores candidates and uses textual critiques to update agent prompts and routing policies without retraining the underlying LLMs. During test-time inference, the evolved configuration translates the remaining series. We also introduce Subtitle Arena, covering 14 genres, 2--198 episodes per series, production years 1959--2023, and 15 target locales, together with SubMQM, a subtitle-adapted MQM framework with seven dimensions and 19 error categories. SMART achieves the best overall MQM score in all 15 Subtitle Arena directions, reducing average penalty by 6.9% over the strongest competing agent system. On the public MuSC benchmark, SMART obtains the best model result across all four language pairs and also achieves the best human-evaluation result, with an overall score of 4.50/5.

</details>


### [67] [ContextAdapt: Evaluating Contextual Adaptation and Value Alignment in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38260)
*Olivia Macmillan-Scott,Mirco Musolesi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 ContextAdapt 评估框架，检验 LLM 能否在不同专业情境（医学、法律、金融、国家安全）下恰当应用诚实、自主、保密等价值，并保持对情境无关变化的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诚实、自主、保密等价值常被视为 AI 对齐的普遍原则，但其具体含义依赖决策情境。论文追问：LLM 是否能跨专业场景恰当调整价值应用，同时在情境变化不影响相关专业规范时保持一致。

Method: 构建 ContextAdapt 评估框架，覆盖医学、法律、金融、国家安全四大领域与诚实、自主、保密三类价值。基于专业及监管一手文献建立"价值×领域"矩阵，设计测试默认专业规则与公认例外的情境，评估 12 个 LLM 推荐的行为及其给出的理由。另有因子实验，操纵领域名称、模型角色与利害程度。

Result: 主实验中模型平均恰当性达 95.6%，但使用正确领域专属理由的比例差异悬殊（25.6%–76.9%）。明确点明专业领域、改变模型角色对行为影响有限；操纵利害程度则暴露严重但局部的失败：有时尽管专业义务未变，模型仍改变回答，感知的严重性似乎成为诚实与保密情境中"披露"的线索。

Conclusion: 评估价值对齐不能只看模型是否遵循抽象原则，还须考察其能否在不同情境中恰当应用这些原则；行为恰当性高不等于理由对齐，且对利害程度的敏感可能造成局部不一致。

Abstract: Values such as honesty, autonomy, and confidentiality are often regarded as general principles underpinning AI alignment. However, what it means to act in accordance with these values can depend on the context in which a decision is made. In this paper, we ask whether large language models (LLMs) appropriately adapt the application of a value across professional settings, while remaining consistent when contextual changes do not alter the relevant professional norm. To study this, we introduce ContextAdapt, an evaluation framework covering honesty, autonomy, and confidentiality across medicine, law, finance, and national security. Drawing on primary-source professional and regulatory documents, we construct a value x domain framework and use this to develop scenarios testing both default professional rules and recognised exceptions. We evaluate 12 LLMs on both the actions they recommend and the justifications they provide. In our main experiment, models achieve 95.6% mean appropriateness, although the use of the correct domain-specific justification varies substantially across models, from 25.6% to 76.9%. In a separate factorial experiment, explicitly naming the professional domain and changing the role of the model have limited effect on behaviour. Varying stakes, however, reveals severe but localised failures: in some cases, models alter their responses even though the underlying professional obligation remains unchanged. In particular, perceived severity appears to act as a cue for disclosure across both honesty and confidentiality scenarios. These results show that evaluating value alignment requires us to consider not only whether models follow abstract principles, but whether they apply them appropriately across different contexts.

</details>


### [68] [NinaXander: Feasibility and Limits of Composing Frozen Language Models Across Architecture Families via a Shared Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38261)
*Takanori Kotama,Shun-ichiro Hayashi,Daichi Mukunoki,Tetsuya Hoshino,Takahiro Katagiri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出NinaXander，用单一共享隐空间适配器连接不同架构家族的冻结语言模型层，实现事后模型重组，部分配置可大幅降低KV缓存但性能不及母模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究来自不同架构家族的冻结语言模型能否在训练后（post hoc）被重新组合，并检验跨模型中间表示是否可对应、能否共享语义空间。

Method: 提出NinaXander组合语言模型：先运行一个模型的前若干层，用一次性训练的共享隐空间适配器转换中间表示，再运行另一模型的剩余层。使用循环架构RWKV-4-Raven-7B与Transformer架构Tulu-Pythia-6.9b，训练单一适配器后可得到不同层连接点的多种组合模型而无需重训。

Result: 组合模型能回答多选题，生成文本句法通顺。Pythia前5层+RWKV后27层的配置将Transformer KV缓存减少84.4%，准确率与单独RWKV无显著差异；但无组合模型在多选题上匹敌母模型Pythia，WikiText语言建模性能急剧下降。仅在共享分词器、相同深度与隐藏宽度的有利情况下获得表示对应。

Conclusion: 跨架构家族的冻结模型可在事后部分重组并获得效率收益，但表示对应仅在理想条件下成立，无法证明模型间共享通用语义空间，整体性能仍不及母模型。

Abstract: In this paper we propose NinaXander, a series of composed language models obtained by connecting layers of frozen language models from different architecture families with a single trained shared-latent adapter. A composed model runs the first layers of one model, converts the resulting intermediate representation once with the adapter, and then runs the remaining layers of the other model. Once the adapter is trained, several composed models that connect at different layers are obtained without retraining. Using the recurrent RWKV-4-Raven-7B and the Transformer-based Tulu-Pythia-6.9b, abbreviated as RWKV and Pythia, this study examines whether frozen models from different families can be recombined post hoc. The composed models answered multiple-choice questions, and those whose generations we examined produced syntactically well-formed text. The configuration that combines the first 5 layers of Pythia with the remaining 27 layers of RWKV reduced the Transformer key-value (KV) cache by 84.4% with accuracy not significantly different from that of RWKV alone. In multiple-choice accuracy, however, no composed model matched the parent model Pythia, and language-modeling performance decreased sharply on WikiText, a corpus of Wikipedia articles outside the training domain. The correspondence between intermediate representations was also obtained in one favorable case, with a shared tokenizer, the same depth, and the same hidden width, and does not show that the models share a general semantic space.

</details>


### [69] [RSIGame: Autonomous Agentic Game Development with Recursive Self-improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39045)
*Wenyi Wu,Minghao Fu,Jieyu You,Kun Zhou,Siqi Liu,Aayush Salvi,Yiheng Lin,Ce Zhang,Xiaohan Lan,Jiahui Zhu,Yujie Zhong,Qi She,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RSIGame：递归自我改进的自主智能体游戏开发框架，通过局部探索-诊断-改进循环与全局质量跟踪，并将经验内化到生成器，在140个基准任务上稳定提升游戏质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型使自动游戏生成日益可行，但仅生成"可玩"版本后如何可靠提升质量仍有挑战。朴素迭代优化易过拟合少量测试用例，产生脆弱游戏，存在未修复漏洞、行为缺失及泛化性差的问题。

Method: 提出RSIGame框架，包含互补的局部与全局循环：局部"探索-诊断-改进"循环广泛探索可执行游戏、诊断并按优先级处理问题、进行有证据支撑的修订，并由不断演化的检查清单持续累积测试与改进指南；全局循环跟踪整体质量、保存最佳检查点、检测长期开发中的饱和或退化。此外，通过训练将成功开发经验内化到生成器中。

Result: 在140个GameCraft-Bench任务、两个游戏引擎、五种生成器上，RSIGame在相同开发预算下持续提升游戏质量。经验内化使Qwen3.8-27B在Godot上达61.38、Phaser上达58.53，超过GPT-5.5单次生成得分，同时将Qwen的生成token减少11倍。

Conclusion: RSIGame通过递归自我改进与经验内化，有效缓解迭代优化的过拟合与脆弱性问题，在有限预算下显著提升自动生成游戏的质量与效率，为自主游戏开发提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have made automatic game generation increasingly feasible, yet reliably improving generated games beyond a playable version remains challenging. Naive iterative refinement can easily overfit a small set of test cases, producing fragile games with unresolved bugs, missing behaviors, and poor generalization to broader player interactions. We introduce RSIGame, an autonomous agentic game development framework with recursive self-improvement. RSIGame organizes development into complementary local and global loops. Concretely, a local explore-diagnose-improve loop broadly explores the executable game, diagnoses and prioritizes discovered issues, and performs evidence-grounded revision, where an evolving checklist continually accumulates new testing and improvement guidance. A global loop tracks overall quality, preserves the best checkpoint, and detects saturation or regression over long-horizon development. Beyond test-time improvement, RSIGame further internalizes successful development experience into the generator through training. Across 140 GameCraft-Bench tasks, two game engines, and five generators, RSIGame consistently improves game quality under matched development budgets. Notably, experience internalization enables Qwen3.8-27B to reach 61.38 on Godot and 58.53 on Phaser, exceeding GPT-5.5 one-shot scores while reducing Qwen's generation tokens by 11 times.

</details>


### [70] [EVOKE: Eliciting World Knowledge in Agents for Transferable Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38334)
*Yuhan Guo,Jinming Liu,Liang Xu,Ziqiang Li,Jianguo Huang,Zhicheng Wang,Hu Zhu,Qiuyu Chen,Yuntao Wei,Xin Jin,Wenjun Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 EVOKE 后训练方法，通过在固定状态与交互历史下引入目标多样性排序，隐式激发 LLM 智能体预训练中已有的世界知识，从而提升决策性能、未见环境泛化与数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 作为多步决策智能体时跨环境迁移能力差；世界模型方法虽可缓解，但需额外训练且预测误差在规划中会累积。作者认为数字环境中 LLM 已在预训练中内化了大量世界知识，问题应从"获取知识"转为"激发知识"，而常规后训练因单一目标监督反而诱导策略依赖表层上下文习惯，缺乏激发压力。

Method: EVOKE 在固定环境状态和交互历史的前提下，对同一批候选动作在多个不同目标下进行排序，迫使动作偏好随目标改变而变化，使依赖上下文习惯或单目标相关性的策略无法正确排序，从而隐式激发出策略预训练的世界知识。该方法有理论支撑：能在多样目标下胜任的智能体，必然编码了可从其动作偏好中恢复的世界模型。

Result: 在三种骨干模型和多样任务上评估，EVOKE 提升了任务表现、未见环境泛化能力与数据效率；并进行了受控分析以理解增益来源。

Conclusion: 为通过直接决策监督激发内化世界知识、实现可迁移动作提供了新视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as agents for multi-step decision-making, yet transfer poorly to unseen environments. World-model methods address this by training agents to predict future observations, at the cost of additional training and errors that compound when predictions are used for planning. However, for LLM agents operating in digital environments, much of this world knowledge is already internalized during pretraining, which shifts the problem from acquiring it to eliciting it. We argue that typical post-training provides little pressure for such elicitation, since supervision under a single goal at each visited state inadvertently drives policies to rely on superficial contextual habits. We introduce EVOKE, a post-training method that supplies this pressure through goal diversity at fixed states. Motivated by theory showing that an agent competent across diverse goals must encode a world model recoverable from its action preferences, EVOKE holds the environment state and interaction history fixed and ranks the same candidate actions under alternative goals, forcing action preferences to change, so that a policy relying on contextual habits or single-goal correlations cannot order them correctly. This implicitly elicits the policy's pretrained world knowledge to inform decisions.
