We evaluate EVOKE across diverse tasks in three backbones, demonstrating improved task performance, unseen environment generalization, and data efficiency. We further conduct controlled analyses to better understand what drives these gains. These findings offer a new perspective on eliciting internalized world knowledge for transferable action through direct decision supervision.

</details>


### [71] [Halluscoring 2026: The first shared task on llms hallucination detection and answer verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38355)
*Aisha Alansari,Abdessalam Bouchekif,Ahmed Hasanaath,Salah Eddine Bekhouche,Malak Alkhorasani,Mohammed-En-Nadhir Zighem,Saad Ezzini,Hichem Telli,Hend Al-Khalifa,Muhammad Abdul-Mageed,Hadid Abdenour,Hamzah Luqman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 介绍 HalluScoring 2026 阿拉伯语问答幻觉检测与事实核查共享任务，包含四项子任务，13 支队伍参赛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估阿拉伯语问答中幻觉检测与事实核查在难题泛化设置下的表现，尤其是对未见问题和未见 LLM 生成回复的泛化能力。

Method: 共享任务分两大部分共四个子任务：任务1为二元幻觉检测，含未见问题（1.1）与未见LLM生成回复（1.2）；任务2在检测基础上还需从六个候选中选出正确事实答案，涵盖伊斯兰知识（2.1）与通用知识（2.2）。基于 HalluScore 和 HalluTruthQA 两个阿拉伯语数据集。

Result: 共13支队伍参赛，10支提交系统描述论文。任务1获胜队伍在子任务1.1和1.2的AUC-ROC测试分数分别为0.772和0.767；任务2获胜队伍在辅助评估下，伊斯兰与通用知识子任务分别达到0.882和0.857。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在分布偏移下幻觉检测仍具挑战性，而任务2的事实答案识别在辅助评估下取得较高成绩。

Abstract: We present HalluScoring 2026, a shared task for evaluating hallucination detection and factual verification in Arabic question answering under challenging generalization settings. The shared task is organized into two main tasks, each comprising two subtasks, for a total of four subtasks. Task 1 evaluates binary hallucination detection, considering generalization to unseen questions (Subtask 1.1) and responses generated by unseen LLMs (Subtask 1.2). Task 2 extends the evaluation beyond detection by requiring the systems to additionally identify the correct factual answer from six related candidates, covering Islamic knowledge (Subtask 2.1) and general knowledge (Subtask 2.2). The shared task is based on two Arabic datasets: HalluScore and HalluTruthQA. A total of 13 teams participated in the shared task, 10 of which submitted system description papers. The results of Task 1 demonstrate that hallucination detection remains challenging under distribution shift, with the winning team achieving AUC-ROC test scores of 0.772 and 0.767 for Subtasks 1.1 and 1.2, respectively. For Task 2, the winning team achieved scores of 0.882 and 0.857 in the Islamic and general-knowledge subtasks, respectively, under assisted evaluation.

</details>


### [72] [Evaluating Language Model Safety Across Long Adversarial Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38357)
*Parisa Salmani,Peter R. Lewis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究多轮持续对抗对话下的语言模型安全性，发现单轮安全率虽高（85%-100%），但随着对话轮次增至101轮，安全率骤降至15%-44%，说明单轮安全无法保证长程对抗交互中的安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话安全评估多采用单一有害提示进行测试，而真实系统通过长程、自适应对话与用户交互，这种评估方式无法反映风险在多轮交互中累积的真实情况，因此需要考察模型在持续对抗用户下的安全表现。

Method: 研究选取三个开源、经指令微调的模型，针对两个有害提示，在不同对话长度和随机种子下进行评估。每轮由第二个语言模型充当持续对抗用户，并用安全分类器对每个回复标注安全或不安全。

Result: 在所有模型-提示组合中，首轮安全回复率为85%-100%；到第11轮降至38%-61%；到第101轮降至15%-44%。这种下降跨模型普遍存在，且远超现有多轮安全评估通常采用的短交互长度。

Conclusion: 强单轮安全性不一定能在持续对抗交互中保持，这证明了需要长时程评估以及能够应对跨轮风险累积的对话级安全防护措施。

Abstract: Conversational safety evaluations often test language models with a single harmful prompt, even though real-world systems interact with users through long, adaptive conversations. This study examines whether models continue to respond safely when an adversarial user persists across multiple turns. We evaluate three open-weight, instruction-tuned models on two harmful prompts across different conversation lengths and random seeds. In each setting, a second language model acts as a persistent adversarial user, while a safety classifier labels every response as safe or unsafe. Across all model-prompt combinations, first-turn safe-response rates ranged from 85% to 100%. By depth 11, they dropped to 38-61%, and by depth 101, to 15-44%. This decline appeared across models and continued well beyond the short interactions typically used in multi-turn safety evaluations. These results provide proof-of-concept evidence that strong single-turn safety does not necessarily persist during sustained adversarial interaction. They highlight the need for long-horizon evaluations and conversation-level safeguards that account for risk accumulating across turns.

</details>


### [73] [Doc2LoRA Provides Decodable Representations of Scientific Ideas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38374)
*Chand Sahil Mansuri,Joel Zachariah,Sadamori Kojaku*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出用 Doc-to-LoRA 超网络为每篇论文生成 LoRA 适配器，使论文空间的每个点（含混合点）都对应一个可对话、可指令的 LLM，从而同时支持检索与生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统论文向量表示只能用于相似检索，混合向量产生的新点往往没有对应论文，无法解读其含义。作者希望让空间中的每个点都能被"提问"，把抽象的向量混合转化为可交互、可生成的新想法起点。

Method: 用 Doc-to-LoRA 超网络为每篇论文生成一个 LoRA 适配器，以适配器作为论文的表示；对适配器参数做向量混合即可得到新点。训练目标为生成任务，同时引入一个小的可逆变换使嵌入适用于检索。在 APS 论文数据上，让子领域平均点对应的 LLM 用几个词命名该领域，并让两篇论文之间的混合点生成摘要。

Result: 在 APS 数据上，子领域平均点生成的领域标签在词重叠度和五位 LLM 评审评判下都优于五个基线，更接近官方名称。混合点生成的摘要随混合权重从一篇论文平滑过渡到另一篇。经可逆变换后，嵌入的检索性能与 SPECTER2、EmbeddingGemma 相当，接近 SBERT，且能映射回 LLM。

Conclusion: 论文表示既可检索又可生成：由于变换可逆，空间中的任意点（包括混合点）都对应一个可用自然语言提问的 LLM，让研究者能就任意位置的想法发问，作为生成新思路的起点。

Abstract: Representing scientific papers as points in a space lets us search for similar papers and inquire about how fields relate to one another and drive innovation. Beyond search, the vector space of papers invites generation: mixing papers through simple vector operations creates new points, mirroring combinatorial novelty, the recombination of existing ideas into new ones. However, a mixed point often represents an idea no paper has yet realized, with no papers nearby to identify the idea. We propose representing each paper by a LoRA adapter generated by the Doc-to-LoRA hypernetwork. Every point in the space, including mixtures, thus represents a large language model (LLM) open to questions and instructions in natural language. On papers from the American Physical Society (APS), we instruct the LLM at the average of each subfield to name the field in a few words and obtain labels closer to the official names than the labels of five baselines, as judged by word overlap and a panel of five LLM judges. We also ask the LLMs at points between two APS papers to write an abstract and obtain descriptions shifting from one paper to the other in step with the mixing weight. While Doc-to-LoRA is trained for generation, a small invertible transform makes the embeddings competitive for search, on par with SPECTER2 and EmbeddingGemma and close to SBERT. Because the transform is invertible, every point in the transformed space still maps back to an LLM. The embeddings thus serve both search and generation, enabling researchers to question the idea at any point in the space as a starting point for generating new ideas.

</details>


### [74] [Mid-Harness: Scaling Actions Between Model and Harness for Terminal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39982)
*Minki Kang,Ryo Hachiuma,Shaokun Zhang,Subhashree Radhakrishnan,Yonggan Fu,Jindong Jiang,Mingjie Liu,Ehsan Hosseini-Asl,Yi Dong,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Byung-Kwan Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究终端智能体的动作可靠性问题，提出在模型与执行框架（harness）边界分配测试时算力的 Mid-Harness 方法：采样并验证候选动作后再执行，从而显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端智能体通过随机生成模型产生动作，但"能生成有用动作"不等于"能可靠执行"。一个糟糕的命令（如错误的软件包安装）会改变环境，阻碍后续进展，即便模型本可生成更好的替代方案。因此论文关注：在模型-框架边界分配测试时算力能否提升动作可靠性与轨迹成功率，以及何种分配方式有效。

Method: 提出 Mid-Harness：在不改变生成器与执行框架的前提下，对候选动作进行采样并验证，再将选定的一个动作转发执行，即在执行前插入动作级验证环节。实验使用 TMAX-9B 生成器，并对比不同验证器（弱验证器、GPT-5.6 Sol 验证器、TMAX-9B 自身作为验证器、成对验证机制等），同时研究将强验证器响应蒸馏回 TMAX-9B，以及动作扩展与轨迹扩展的组合。

Result: 在 TerminalBench-Lite 上，TMAX-9B 生成器下弱验证时增加采样收益很小，而强验证器可有效利用同一生成器的替代动作；使用 GPT-5.6 Sol 作为验证器、采样 8 个动作时，Pass@1 从基线 50.00% 提升到 68.03%。当 TMAX-9B 自身作验证器时，成对验证在评估的验证机制中表现最佳。将强验证器响应蒸馏进 TMAX-9B 可在不改变动作生成器的前提下进一步提升 Pass@1。结合动作扩展与轨迹扩展，比仅增加轨迹生成能以更低估计 token 成本取得更高成功率。Mid-Harness 在其他模型、基准与执行框架上也有一致提升。

Conclusion: 动作扩展（action scaling）是终端智能体测试时算力扩展的一个有前景的方向，在模型-框架边界进行动作级采样与验证能有效提升动作可靠性与整体任务成功率。

Abstract: Terminal agents act through stochastic model generations, yet the ability to generate a useful action does not ensure its reliable execution. A poor command (e.g., wrong package install) can change the environment in ways that hinder subsequent progress, even when the model could generate a better alternative. We investigate whether allocating test-time compute at the model-harness boundary can improve action reliability and trajectory success, and what makes this allocation effective. To study these questions, we introduce Mid-Harness, which samples and verifies candidate actions before forwarding one for execution, while keeping the generator and harness unchanged. With a TMAX-9B generator, more action sampling yields little benefit under weak verification, whereas a capable verifier can exploit useful alternatives from the same generator. On TerminalBench-Lite, a GPT-5.6 Sol verifier raises Pass@1 from 50.00% for the base agent to 68.03% with 8 sampled actions. When the same TMAX-9B model serves as the verifier, pairwise verification performs best among the evaluated verification mechanisms. Distilling responses from the stronger verifier into TMAX-9B further improves Pass@1, while leaving the action generator unchanged. With TMAX-9B on TerminalBench-Lite, combining action and trajectory scaling reaches higher success at lower estimated token cost than generating more trajectories alone. Mid-Harness also improves performance across additional models, benchmarks, and harnesses. These findings identify action scaling as a promising target for test-time compute scaling in terminal agents.

</details>


### [75] [Evaluating Whether LLMs Can Reliably Connect the DOTs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38406)
*Eftekhar Hossain,John Salvador,Santu Karmaker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个约9.2K实例的多领域叙事补全基准，评测20个开源指令微调LLM，发现模型规模并不能可靠预测补全质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实信息嘈杂且碎片化，从碎片中重构连贯叙事需要模型补全缺失片段（text infilling），同时保持局部上下文与全局故事线的一致性。尽管文本补全被广泛用作LLM预训练目标，但其在真实叙事补全任务上的表现仍缺乏深入探究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了一个多领域叙事补全基准，包含约9.2K个实例，通过遮盖一到三句话生成，覆盖四类叙事：百科文本、常识故事、新闻文章和视觉叙事。基于该基准，评测20个指令微调的开源LLM（1.5B至70B参数），并考察不同指令具体程度与推理引导水平。输出采用标准自动指标以及涵盖五个叙事维度的定性框架进行评估。

Result: 模型规模并不能可靠预测补全质量：Gemma-2-2B取得最高定性得分（4.02/5），超过参数量十倍以上的模型，包括DeepSeek-Qwen-32B（3.77/5，差6.6%）和LLaMA-3.3-70B（3.71/5，差8.3%）。显式推理收益有限，思维链推理仅带来0.6%的边际提升。此外，短叙事和领域特征比填充位置更能预测任务难度。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在真实叙事补全任务上表现与模型规模不呈正相关，推理引导收益有限，任务难度更多由叙事长度和领域特性决定。这表明需要更关注数据与任务特性，而非单纯扩大模型规模。

Abstract: Access to real-world information is often noisy and fragmented. Constructing a coherent narrative from such fragments requires models to reconstruct missing spans within a broader storyline, commonly referred to as text infilling, while preserving consistency with both the local context and the global storyline. Despite using text infilling as a pre-training objective in many Large Language Models (LLMs), their actual performance on real-world narrative infilling remains underexplored. In this paper, we address this gap by introducing a multi-domain benchmark of ~9.2K instances for narrative infilling, constructed by masking one to three sentences across four narrative types: encyclopedic text, commonsense stories, news articles, and visual narratives. Using this benchmark, we evaluate 20 instruction-tuned open-source LLMs ranging from 1.5B to 70B parameters across varying levels of instruction specificity and reasoning guidance. Outputs are assessed using standard automatic metrics and a qualitative framework covering five narrative dimensions. Results show that model scale does not reliably predict infilling quality: Gemma-2-2B achieves the highest qualitative score (4.02/5), outperforming models over ten times larger, including DeepSeek-Qwen-32B (3.77/5, 6.6%) and LLaMA-3.3-70B (3.71/5, 8.3%). We further find that explicit reasoning offers limited benefits as chain-of-thought reasoning yields only a marginal improvement (+0.6%). Additionally, short narratives and domain characteristics emerge as stronger predictors of task difficulty than infill position alone for narrative infilling in current LLMs.

</details>


### [76] [Policy-Conditioned AI-Use Detection: An Evidentiary Framework for Academic Publishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38427)
*Jairo Diaz-Rodriguez,Mumin Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"政策条件化的AI使用检测"框架，主张检测目标不应是"文本是否由AI撰写"，而应是"人机协作流程是否符合既定规则"，并将政策规则作为显式输入、以预先固定误报率下的真阳率进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各大会议与期刊已针对作者、审稿人、领域主席等不同角色发布差异化的AI使用规则，并规定披露义务。然而作为执行手段的AI检测工具，其估计目标是"文本是否由AI模型撰写"，与会议/期刊实际需要做出的决策（是否违规、是否合规）并不一致，存在目标错位。

Method: 作者提出"政策条件化的AI使用检测"证据性框架：将治理规则作为显式输入；推理输出假设、证据、校准机制与不确定性，而非"检测到AI"这类裁决式结论；评测则基于可复现流程构建基准，生成合规与非合规的工作流，并在会议事先固定的误报率下报告真阳率。同时将该框架应用于同行评审场景进行推演。

Result: 在同行评审场景中，即便检测器处于较强工作点，在合理的违规率下，被标记的合规作者数量仍多于违规作者。该框架还明确指出会议需要配置的要素：结构化披露、尊重审稿人保密性的已批准工具路由，以及允许对结论提出异议的申诉路径。

Conclusion: 在这一框架下，检测器不再是作者身份分类器，而是一套可审计的程序，其错误率由会议预先设定并可为其辩护。

Abstract: Major venues now publish detailed rules about how authors, reviewers, and area chairs may use AI, and those rules differ by role, by task, and by what must be disclosed. AI detection, the instrument usually proposed to enforce them, estimates something else: whether an AI model wrote the text. We argue that this target is misaligned with the decisions conferences and journals face, and propose policy-conditioned AI-use detection, an evidentiary framework for assessing whether a human--AI workflow complied with a stated rule. Policy makes the governing rule an explicit input. Inference reports hypotheses, evidence, calibration regime, and uncertainty in place of verdicts such as "AI detected". Evaluation builds benchmarks from reproducible pipelines that generate compliant and non-compliant workflows, and reports true positive rate at a false positive rate the venue fixes in advance. We work the framework through peer review, where at plausible violation rates a detector at a strong operating point still flags more compliant authors than violating ones. The framework therefore also names what a venue must instrument: structured disclosure, approved-tool routing that respects reviewer confidentiality, and a path by which a finding can be contested. Under this framing a detector is not an authorship classifier but an auditable procedure with an error rate the venue fixes in advance and can defend.

</details>


### [77] [The Backdrop Exposes What the World Around an Agent Costs It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38469)
*Nusrat Jahan Lia,Shubhashis Roy Dipta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 BACKDROP 基准，向智能体执行环境中植入四类"日常风险"（越权、注入、越界、故障），在保持指令与正确终态不变的前提下衡量智能体能力的"存活率"。3,678 个变体、16 个模型上，四类风险同时存在时平均通过率从 69.5% 降至 31.3%，最强模型降幅最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准多在"静止"的干净世界中评测，而真实部署环境会被他人改动（如收到要求转账的消息、订单确认索要门禁码）。因此需要一种方法衡量干净世界中的能力在受扰动世界中还剩多少，并揭示干净环境得分是真实世界表现的上限。

Method: BACKDROP 以"任务 + 执行环境"为输入，逐一及同时植入四类风险，指令与正确终态保持不变。每类风险对应一个问题：Authority（他人消息能否覆盖用户）、Injection（记录中植入文本能否重定向智能体）、Boundary（请求是否把智能体拉入未授权的应用）、Fault（写入失败且结果未知时是否先检查再重试）。共构造 3,678 个变体，评测 16 个模型。

Result: 四类风险同时存在时，平均通过率由 69.5% 降至 31.3%；最强模型下降最多（Claude Fable 5.1 由 96.6% 降至 56.0%）。在植入文本确实到达智能体的运行中，46.4% 的运行听从了他人消息，20.3% 听从了注入文本；该差距在全部 16 个模型中一致存在。

Conclusion: 智能体已学会抵抗注入文本，但常会顺从他人的其他未授权请求。BACKDROP 形式化了这些能力缺口，说明智能体在某一任务世界中的得分是其真实世界性能的上限。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks test agents in worlds that stay still. Deployed agents work in worlds that other people also change. Someone texts the agent to send the money elsewhere or an order confirmation asks it to reply with a door code. We present BACKDROP, which asks how much of an agent's capability in a clean world survives in such a world. BACKDROP takes a task along with the agents execution environment, and plants four everyday hazards in its world, one at a time and all together. The instruction and the correct end state stay the same. Each hazard asks one question. Authority: does a message from another person override the user? Injection: does text planted in a record redirect the agent? Boundary: does a request pull it into an app it was not given? Fault: after a write fails without saying whether it landed, does the agent check before it retries? Across 3,678 variants and 16 models, , the average pass rate falls from 69.5% to 31.3% once all four hazards are present; the strongest models fall furthest (Claude Fable 5.1 from 96.6% to 56.0%). Agents have learned to resist injected text but often follow other unauthorized requests of other people. With all four hazards present, and counting only runs where the planted text reached the agent, agents followed another person's message in 46.4% of runs and injected text in 20.3%. The gap is consistent throughout all 16 models. BACKDROP formalizes these gaps and shows how an agent's score in a task's world is a ceiling on real-world performance.

</details>


### [78] [KlinikeBench: Evaluating Language Models Beyond Diagnostic Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38480)
*Xueting Fang,Zehui Li,Yang Yang,Camilla Giovino,Shubh K. Patel,Shailly Prajapati,Vallijah Subasri,Caihua Shan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出KlinikeBench基准，包含333个由临床医生编写的模拟临床问诊任务，通过虚拟患者与临床工具评估语言模型在多轮交互中的完整临床诊疗能力，发现模型诊断准确率虽高但交互问诊表现明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床基准多以完整病例描述评估语言模型的诊断能力，但真实临床中患者以不同方式提供信息，医生需主动采集病史并决定所需检查，仅靠诊断准确率无法判断智能体是否收集了关键信息或进行了恰当的临床评估；同时现有基准缺乏专业临床医生的验证。

Method: 构建KlinikeBench基准，包含333个由临床医生编写的任务，每个任务提供隔离沙盒环境，内含虚拟患者、临床工具以及任务特定的成功标准，并有超35位临床医生参与病例编写与基准评估。在每个任务中，语言模型在固定轮数预算内与患者沟通、询问相关病史、请求检查、遵守行动约束并给出最终诊断，各步骤分别计分并综合计分。研究还邀请临床医生对模拟对话与真实对话改编的参考对话进行质量评分，并对31个模型、7个模型家族进行实证评估。

Result: 临床医生对模拟对话的平均质量评分高于参考对话。在31个模型和7个模型家族中，表现最好的模型（如GPT-6-astra和Claude Opus 5）任务成功率不足30%，尽管其诊断准确率可达90.7%。部分模型能从与患者对话中获益，另一些模型在完整病历上诊断表现良好，但在对话场景中表现显著下降。

Conclusion: KlinikeBench为评估完整临床问诊过程提供了测试平台，揭示了诊断准确率与交互式临床评估表现之间存在显著差距，表明仅依赖诊断准确率不足以衡量语言模型的临床诊疗能力。

Abstract: Most clinical benchmarks evaluate language models (LMs) on diagnosis using complete case descriptions. In clinical practice, however, patients present information in different ways, and clinicians must obtain relevant history and determine which examinations are needed before reaching a diagnosis. Diagnostic accuracy alone therefore cannot establish whether an agent gathered essential information or conducted an appropriate clinical assessment. Furthermore, existing benchmarks lack professional clinicians' verification. To address this gap, we introduce KlinikeBench, a benchmark of 333 clinician-authored tasks, each providing an isolated sandbox environment with a virtual patient, clinical tools, and task-specific success criteria. More than 35 clinicians contributed to case authoring and benchmark evaluation. In an empirical study, clinicians gave simulated dialogues higher mean quality ratings than reference conversations, which is adapted from real conversation. In each task, an LM has a fixed budget of turns to communicate with the patient, ask about relevant history, request examinations, follow action constraints, and record a final diagnosis. We score these steps separately as well as together. Across 31 models and seven model families, the best-performing models (e.g., GPT-6-astra and Claude Opus 5) succeed on less than 30% of tasks, even though their diagnosis accuracy reaches 90.7%. Some models benefit from talking with the patient; others diagnose well from a complete chart but perform much worse in conversation. Overall, KlinikeBench provides a testbed for evaluating the full clinical encounter and reveals a substantial gap between diagnostic accuracy and performance in interactive clinical assessment.

</details>


### [79] [Personalized State-Transition-Aware Memory for Clinical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38490)
*Maryam Haghifam,Zahra Rajabi,Yizhou Sun,Carlos Morato*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: STAM：一种状态转移感知的记忆框架，用于LLM临床智能体，在保留历史的同时维护患者当前状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体处理临床记录时需追踪患者状态变化，又需保留理解变化所需的历史。简单累积记忆会导致不清楚哪些信息仍有效，而覆盖早期记忆则会丢失重建治疗史和临床轨迹的证据。

Method: STAM在新临床条目到达时记录状态变化，结合语义检索与类型化临床关系识别受影响的记忆，将当前信息保存在Active中、被取代或已解决的信息保存在History中；读取时通过查询依赖的门控机制选择性地提供历史记忆。

Result: 在四个纵向临床基准上，通过下游问答、直接状态维护诊断以及在近似匹配上下文长度下的对比进行评估。

Conclusion: STAM能够兼顾当前状态的准确维护与临床历史证据的保留，为临床LLM智能体的记忆管理提供有效方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents that reason over clinical records must track changes in a patient's state while preserving the history needed to understand them. Simply accumulating memories leaves it unclear which information still applies, whereas overwriting earlier memories can erase evidence needed to reconstruct treatment history and clinical trajectories. We introduce STAM, a state-transition-aware memory framework that records state changes as new clinical entries arrive. STAM combines semantic retrieval with typed clinical relations to identify affected memories, maintaining current information in Active and superseded or resolved information in History. At read time, a query-dependent gate selectively serves historical memory. Across four longitudinal clinical benchmarks, we evaluate STAM with downstream question answering, direct state-maintenance diagnostics, and comparisons at approximately matched context lengths.

</details>


### [80] [DEdit: Iterative Draft Editing for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38510)
*Longxuan Yu,Bingsen Chen,Peng Shi,Dongkyu Lee,Yi Xiang,Hideo Kobayashi,Sheng Zhang,Shuaichen Chang,Xing Niu,Zhuoyan Xu,Greg Ver Steeg,Jiarong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DEdit，一种基于扩散的草稿模型，可通过迭代编辑修复早期错误，在Qwen3-4B/8B上实现约5.7×–6.0×的宏平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式草稿模型虽能并行预测多token以降低草拟延迟，但各token独立预测，一旦前缀出现早期错误，前缀验证就会丢弃后续所有草稿，即便其中包含有用的下游预测，导致接受率与加速受限。

Method: DEdit支持传统并行unmasking草拟，并新增token-to-token迭代编辑，使后续预测可作为双向上下文修复早期错误、延长被接受前缀。训练上提出ProposalMix，依据首轮置信度将草稿预测与ground-truth token混合，教会模型在保留正确预测的同时修复错误。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B与Qwen3-8B的七个基准上，DEdit在被评估的草稿模型中取得最高的宏平均token接受率与加速比，贪婪解码下分别达5.72×和5.97×。增加编辑轮数与草拟窗口宽度可提升接受率；ProposalMix使缩短接受前缀的有害编辑减半；将编辑器限制为因果注意力会降低接受率，尤其在高可预测输出上，说明未来上下文是主要增益来源。

Conclusion: 迭代编辑结合双向上下文能有效修复草稿早期错误、提升接受长度与推理加速，未来上下文对性能提升至关重要。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive LLMs by having a lightweight drafter propose tokens that the target model verifies in parallel. Diffusion-based drafters further reduce drafting latency by proposing multiple tokens at once. However, these tokens are predicted independently, so a single early error causes prefix verification to discard the rest of the draft, even when it contains useful downstream predictions. We introduce DEdit, a diffusion-based drafter that can not only draft by conventional parallel unmasking but also iteratively edit its draft through token-to-token predictions. Through editing, later predictions can serve as bidirectional context for repairing earlier errors and extending the accepted prefix. To teach the model to repair errors while preserving correct predictions, we propose ProposalMix, a training scheme that mixes draft predictions with ground-truth tokens based on first-pass confidence during training. Across seven benchmarks on Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B, DEdit achieves the highest macro-average token acceptance and speedup among the evaluated drafters, reaching macro-average speedups of $5.72\times$ and $5.97\times$ over autoregressive generation under greedy decoding, respectively. Further analysis shows that acceptance improves with more editing passes and wider drafting windows, and that ProposalMix halves harmful edits that shorten the accepted prefix. Moreover, restricting the editor to causal attention lowers acceptance, especially on highly predictable outputs, indicating that future context is a key source of these gains.

</details>


### [81] [Shifting Mechanisms: How Positional Encoding Choice Shapes In-Context Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38530)
*Eric Enouen,Sainyam Galhotra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文从机制角度研究不同位置编码(PE)选择(如RoPE+滑动窗口注意力与NoPE+全局注意力的混合架构)如何影响语言模型的上下文检索机制,发现PE混合模型从位置检索转向语义检索,并揭示了长上下文收益背后的检索权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多采用逐层变化注意力跨度和位置编码的架构(如SWA NoPE),但这些选择如何塑造上下文检索机制尚不清晰,作者希望以机制视角揭示PE选择对模型内部检索机制的影响。

Method: 作者采用机制可解释性方法,追踪位置编码选择如何塑造模型用于上下文检索的内部机制;在22个开源模型(涵盖8个家族)上进行实验,并开展受控预训练消融实验,将位置编码限制在局部层,观察其影响。

Result: 标准RoPE模型主要依赖位置检索,而PE混合模型转向语义检索;受控预训练消融表明,将位置编码限制在局部层会产生这种语义转变,并降低位置信息的表示质量;PE混合模型报告的长上下文收益掩盖了检索权衡:SWA NoPE在多目标检索和QA上优于RoPE,但在区分竞争键时表现下降。

Conclusion: 这些行为差异更好地反映了从位置机制向语义机制的转变,而非长上下文检索的统一改进;研究揭示了PE混合架构长上下文收益背后的机制性权衡。

Abstract: Language models increasingly use architectures that vary attention span and positional encoding across layers, such as applying RoPE with sliding-window attention and NoPE with global attention (SWA NoPE). However, how these choices shape in-context retrieval remains unclear. To study this question, we take a mechanistic view, tracing how positional encoding (PE) choice shapes the internal mechanisms models use for in-context retrieval. Across 22 open-weight models spanning eight families, we find that standard RoPE models rely primarily on positional retrieval, while PE hybrids shift toward semantic retrieval. We further show on a controlled pre-training ablation that confining positional encoding to local layers produces this semantic shift, degrading representations of positional information. Finally, we show that the reported long-context gains of PE hybrids mask a retrieval trade-off: SWA NoPE improves over RoPE on multiple-target retrieval and QA, but degrades when distinguishing competing keys. We show that these behavioral differences better track the mechanism shift from positional toward semantic mechanisms than a uniform improvement in long-context retrieval.

</details>


### [82] [MedKIT: Evaluating Knowledge Integration and Generalization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38543)
*Lukas Thede,Yash Kumar Atri,David Chen,Danielle Bitterman,Matthias Bethge,Tom Hartvigsen,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MedKIT是一个面向医学领域知识更新的细粒度评测基准，揭示了模型在"记忆"与"可用"新知识之间的持续差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界知识（尤其临床证据）不断演化，模型需持续更新；过时知识在医疗场景中可能带来安全风险。现有知识整合评测多聚焦事实召回，难以判断新知识是否真正可用。

Method: 提出MedKIT基准，每个样本对应一个源于临床证据的事实更新，并配套针对性探测任务，覆盖词汇变体、关系变换、组合推理与开放式操作化，另设局部性测试检验旧知识保持。基于该基准，对5个模型（通用与医学LLM）上的12种知识整合策略开展大规模实证研究。

Result: 多数方法在原始更新任务和词汇变体上增益明显，但关系泛化能力有限，且在组合推理和操作化任务上均无实质性提升，呈现"召回"与"可用知识"之间的持续差距。

Conclusion: 知识整合面临根本性挑战，MedKIT可作为测试平台，推动开发使新整合知识在跨任务与跨情境下更稳定可用的方法。

Abstract: Constantly evolving real-world knowledge necessitates models to be updated continuously. Especially in medicine, as clinical evidence changes over time, outdated knowledge can pose safety risks. Existing evaluations of knowledge integration focus on factual recall, offering limited insight into whether newly integrated knowledge is actually usable. Our benchmark MedKIT (Medical Knowledge Integration and Transfer) provides a granular evaluation of how models integrate and apply knowledge under realistic sequences of clinical updates. Each instance corresponds to a factual update derived from clinical evidence, paired with targeted probes that assess transfer across lexical variation, relational transformations, compositional reasoning, and open-ended operationalization, as well as locality tests for knowledge preservation. Using MedKIT, we conduct a large-scale empirical study of 12 knowledge integration strategies across 5 diverse models, including both general-purpose and medical LLMs. Our results reveal a consistent gap between recall and usable knowledge: while most methods achieve strong gains on the original update task and under lexical variation, relational generalization is limited, and no method yields meaningful improvements on compositional or operational tasks. These findings highlight a fundamental challenge in knowledge integration and position MedKIT as a testbed for developing methods that make newly integrated knowledge more consistently usable across tasks and contexts.

</details>


### [83] [Prompt2Skill: Unsupervised Skill Optimization From Natural Language Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38593)
*Bo Ni,Li Li,Ryan A. Rossi,Franck Dernoncourt,Tyler Derr*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Prompt2Skill 仅凭自然语言任务描述即可自动构建并迭代优化 LLM 技能，无需人工编写或已有训练数据，在四个领域平均提升 10.8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家编写的技能成本高且未针对具体消费模型优化，而现有基于反思的自动技能优化方法依赖人工整理的分布内训练集，用户往往不具备；新兴任务也可能超出已有技能库范围。

Method: 提出 Prompt2Skill 框架：从自然语言任务描述出发，推导任务规范，自动发现或合成数据集，并通过反射式编辑的闭环迭代精炼技能。

Result: 在问答、阅读理解、电子表格操作和数学推理四个领域，Prompt2Skill 在开源与前沿模型上均稳定优于直接提示基线，平均提升 10.8。

Conclusion: Prompt2Skill 证明了仅凭任务描述即可自动生成并优化技能，摆脱对专家编写技能与人工训练集的依赖，具有跨领域和跨模型的通用性。

Abstract: Skills are external artifacts that Large Language Models (LLMs) consume at inference time to improve their performance on specialized domains by incorporating relevant procedural and domain knowledge. Expert-authored skills are expensive to produce, and the resulting artifacts are not optimized for the specific model that consumes them, whose failure modes can vary with version, scale and training. In addition, emerging tasks may fall outside the scope of existing skill libraries, creating a need to develop new skills before curated training data become available. Recent works have explored automated skill optimization through reflection, but they require a curated, in-distribution training set, which users might not always have. To address these limitations, we present Prompt2Skill, a framework that builds skills from natural-language task description alone. From the prompt, the system derives a task specification, discovers or synthesizes datasets, and refines the skill in a closed loop of reflective editing. Across four domains spanning question answering, reading comprehension, spreadsheet manipulation, and mathematical reasoning, Prompt2Skill consistently outperforms the direct prompting baseline, achieving an average improvement of 10.8 across open-source and frontier models.

</details>


### [84] [Beyond Oracle Communication: Benchmarking Interactive Intent Alignment Under Miscommunication and Evolving User Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38604)
*Zheyuan Zhang,Mengyuan Chao,Ke Xiao,Ziyi Chen,Daoan Zhang,Yan Zhang,Yanfang Ye,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"交互式意图对齐"任务设定并构建Drift-Bench++基准与GRIP评估协议，揭示LLM智能体在真实沟通与意图演化下的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准普遍假设用户能准确、充分且固定地表达意图（oracle假设），但现实中用户会误沟通、改变目标、失去耐心，该假设难以成立，需要新的任务设定与可执行基准来评估智能体。

Method: 定义Interactive Intent Alignment任务；提出Drift-Bench++基准构建流程，生成可控错位与意图漂移的已验证可执行任务，配以有限耐心、多样模拟用户及静默交互条件漂移的交互协议；并提出GRIP评估协议，覆盖任务落地、用户真实性、询问有效性与对演化意图的适应。

Result: 在多种环境、模型和交互条件下，更强的交互一致性地带来提升，但距离oracle性能仍有明显差距；在部署的ProdAgent会话上的验证表明所建模的失败模式在实际部署中普遍且影响重大。

Conclusion: Drift-Bench++为交互式意图对齐提供了统一的可执行基准，为在真实沟通与意图演化条件下评估和推进智能体奠定基础。

Abstract: Modern LLM agents increasingly tackle complex tasks through interactive, long-horizon exchanges with users, while existing benchmarks generally assume that users always accurately and sufficiently communicate a fixed intent. However, this oracle communication assumption rarely holds in practice: users may miscommunicate, change their goals, and run out of patience. We define this task setting as Interactive Intent Alignment, where agents must recover and continuously track the user's current intent despite imperfect communication and evolving goals. To study this setting, we introduce Drift-Bench++, a principled benchmark construction pipeline for verified executable tasks with controlled misalignment and intent shifts, along with an interaction protocol featuring finite patience, diverse simulated users, and silent interaction-conditioned shifts. We further develop GRIP, a comprehensive evaluation protocol covering task grounding, user realism, inquiry effectiveness, and adaptation to evolving intent. Across diverse environments, models, and interaction conditions, stronger interaction consistently helps but remains far from oracle performance; Validation on deployed ProdAgent sessions further shows that the modeled failures are prevalent and consequential in deployment. By providing a unified, executable benchmark for interactive intent alignment, Drift-Bench++ offers a foundation for evaluating and advancing agents under realistic communication and evolving intent.

</details>


### [85] [StreamDecisionBench: Evaluating Decisions in Force on Evolving Language Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38612)
*Jhen-Ke Lin,Chung Chun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 StreamDecisionBench (SDB)，用于评估语言模型作为程序决策组件时"决策生效期间"的准确性，将误差归因于判断、延迟或二者兼有。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地作为决策组件嵌入程序：程序传入当前状态，模型返回决策并持续生效直到新决策到来。当推理过程中证据发生变化时，针对旧状态正确的决策可能在状态已过时仍然生效（如客户已开始念卡号但呼叫录音仍在继续），而传统的无时间约束（离线）准确率会把这类错误判为正确。现有评估缺少对"每个瞬间实际生效决策"的度量。

Method: 构建 StreamDecisionBench (SDB)：场景以四个应用族流式输入证据，参考决策由公开规则的可执行代码计算；在每个瞬间评估当前生效的决策，并将每个错误瞬间归因于判断错误、延迟或二者兼有；在 1-5 秒的更新间隔上用对数时间轴下的归一化曲线下面积汇总生效准确率，使等倍率区间获得相等权重。评估四个托管模型的六种设置。

Result: 该综合得分与"场景级无时间准确率 × 预言机积分时序得分"的乘积相差不超过 2.9 分。推理可提升判断，但在低推理强度下，延迟使 Luna 和 Terra 两个 GPT 模型相对于无时间准确率分别损失 38.8 和 42.8 分；一个判断较弱但更快的组件可达到与无推理的 Terra 相近的综合得分。

Conclusion: 聚合与分族曲线揭示了这些权衡在何处发生变化，使评估对时间尺度的依赖性变得可见，强调需要以"生效时刻"而非离线准确率来评价作为决策组件的语言模型。

Abstract: As natural language drives more applications, language models increasingly run inside programs as decision components: the program sends them the current state and acts on the returned decision until a newer one arrives. When evidence changes during inference, a decision correct for its own state can stay in force after that state has passed, as when a call recorder keeps running after a customer starts reading out a card number; untimed (offline) accuracy counts such an error as correct. We introduce StreamDecisionBench (SDB), which evaluates the decision in force at every instant and attributes every erroneous instant to judgment, latency or both. Its scenarios stream evidence in four application families, with reference decisions computed from public rules by executable code. We summarize in-force accuracy across update intervals of 1-5 s by its normalized area under the curve on a logarithmic time axis, giving equal weight to equal multiplicative ranges. Across six settings of four hosted models, this score stays within 2.9 points of the scenario-wise product of untimed accuracy and an oracle's integrated timing score. Reasoning improves judgment, but at low effort latency costs Luna and Terra, two GPT models we also evaluate without reasoning, 38.8 and 42.8 points relative to untimed accuracy; a faster component with weaker judgment attains a similar integrated score to Terra without reasoning. The aggregate and family curves show where these tradeoffs change, making the evaluation's time-scale dependence visible.

</details>


### [86] [Marking Contour Tones in Yorùbá](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38627)
*Kólá Túbòsún*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对约鲁巴语（Yorùbá）声调正字法难题，提出采用附加符号表示升降调，以解决人名拼写丢失甚至反转语义的问题，并已在键盘与语音合成器上实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约鲁巴语是声调语言，其升降曲线声调在书写中难以体现。在人名和部分词汇中，传统拼写为避免元音延长而无法为第二声调提供承载音节，导致声调信息不仅缺失，有时甚至完全颠倒名字原意。现有方案不足以解决该问题。

Method: 作者提出采用抑扬符（caron）与长音符/扬抑符（circumflex）两种符号，置于单元音之上，用以编码上升调和下降调。该做法在约鲁巴语音系研究中自Olmsted (1951) 起已有先例，并首次可通过标准键盘输入与计算文本处理实现。作者在WriteYoruba键盘和TTSYoruba语音合成器中进行实现（其架构与听测评估另文报告）。

Result: 该符号方案在WriteYoruba键盘与TTSYoruba语音合成器上成功实现，使曲线声调首次可通过标准键盘输入和计算文本处理被表达与访问。

Conclusion: 采用抑扬符与扬抑符作为单元音上的正字法符号，可有效编码约鲁巴语的升调与降调，解决传统拼写中声调缺失或语义反转的问题，且具备键盘输入与文本处理的可行性。

Abstract: Yorùbá is a tonal language in which contour tones pose persistent orthographic challenges. These are especially notable for personal names and lexical items whose conventional spellings avoid vowel lengthening that would otherwise provide a host syllable for the second tone. A particular concern is a class of names in which the conventional spelling does not just omit tonal information but inverts the meaning of said name, sometimes asserting the opposite of what the name intends. This paper describes the problem, illustrates the inadequacy of current solutions, and proposes the adoption of the caron and circumflex marks. These are symbols with precedent in Yorùbá phonological scholarship since Olmsted (1951), used as orthographic conventions on single vowels to encode rising and falling contour tones, making them accessible for the first time through standard keyboard input and computational text processing. The proposal is supported by an implementation in the WriteYoruba keyboard and the TTSYoruba speech synthesizer, whose architecture and listener evaluation are reported separately (Tubosun et al., 2026).

</details>


### [87] [Strong Multilingual Privacy Tagging at Encoder Speed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38630)
*Jonathan Graehl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向隐私脱敏的多语言细粒度命名实体标注器，通过前沿模型标注、人类金标回放和边界修复，在7种语言上显著超越GLiNER2、Presidio等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私脱敏需在移除个人信息的同时保留文本中的关系表达，这要求实体标注具备细粒度区分能力，以支持多样化的脱敏策略，但现有方法在类型覆盖、多语言泛化和训练成本上存在不足。

Method: 基于多语言编码器加仿射跨度标注头，在35种语言的前沿模型标注上微调；采用覆盖率感知掩码回放映射的人类金标，避免未标注类型被误认为负例；并用学习的±1字符调整修复子词边界。

Result: 在7种语言1283条人类金标测试片段上，最佳脱敏F1为88.8，远高于GLiNER2的69.1/67.8、Presidio的57.3和OpenAI Privacy Filter的35.8；增加约5万条标注句使Ont3精确类型跨度F1从74.5升至76.3，映射金标回放单独提升人类金标F1约10点，边界调整贡献1.7点；本地LLM标注与编码速度远慢于XLM-R，编码器架构CPU吞吐为GLiNER2的4.9倍。已发布代码、提示与训练配方。

Conclusion: 该编码器式多语言细粒度实体标注方案在隐私脱敏任务上兼具高精度与高效率，显著优于现有基线，且训练数据与流程可复现、可扩展。

Abstract: Privacy redaction must remove personal information while preserving relationships expressed in text. We develop a multilingual named-entity tagger with fine-grained distinctions supporting varied redaction policies and methods for cheaply learning additional distinctions. We fine-tune a multilingual encoder with an affine span-tagging head on frontier-model annotations in 35 languages, replay mapped human gold with coverage-aware masking so unannotated types are not treated as negatives, and repair subword boundaries with a learned +/-1-character adjustment. On 1,283 human-gold test segments in seven languages, best measured redaction F1 is 88.8, against 69.1 for published GLiNER2 with 11 unrepresentable types excluded from its task (68.8 without that exemption), 67.8 for GLiNER2 adapted to the new training data, 57.3 for Microsoft Presidio and 35.8 for the best published OpenAI Privacy Filter fine-tune. Adding about 50,000 annotated training sentences and increasing human-gold replay improves exact typed-span F1 from 74.5 to 76.3 on Ont3, our 31-type frontier-annotated NER evaluation of 1,201 development segments. Mapped-gold replay alone raises human-gold F1 by ten points without loss on frontier-annotated text; boundary adjustment adds 1.7 exact typed-span F1 points on Ont3. Local LLMs fitting on a single 96-GB GPU underperformed as prompted annotators and frozen encoders, with encoding 30-95 times slower than XLM-R inference and prompted annotation roughly 180-1,100 times slower in the evaluated configurations. The encoder architecture delivers 4.9 times GLiNER2's CPU throughput. We release code, prompts and training recipes, with data-acquisition scripts and source links.

</details>


### [88] [MetaSteer: Context-Conditioned, nonlinear Steering via Attention-Projection Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38718)
*Mehdi Jafari,Hao Xue,Flora Salim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 MetaSteer，一种学习非线性、上下文相关干预并作用于注意力投影矩阵的 LLM 引导方法，无需线性概念几何假设，一次训练后可零样本迁移到未见概念与分布外语境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对大型语言模型的引导多依赖激活空间中的线性、与上下文无关的干预，这一假设近期受到质疑；当固定表示需编码多种行为区分时，会产生信息瓶颈。因此需要一种非线性、上下文相关的引导方法。

Method: MetaSteer 学习具有上下文依赖效应的非线性干预，并将其施加于注意力投影矩阵，使激活效应天然随输入语境变化，且无需线性概念几何假设。方法被建模为基于偏好的优化，在一个汇聚的偏好语料上训练一次，即可零样本迁移到未见概念和分布外语境；实现上使用低秩适配器。

Result: 尽管使用低秩适配器，MetaSteer 仍在隐状态轨迹中诱发结构化、上下文相关的变化，同时部分保留局部轨迹动力学（如速度与曲率）。在三个受控文本生成基准和三个智能体设置、跨多个模型家族与规模上评估：零样本条件下，MetaSteer 在多数总体比较中达到或超过强任务特定引导基线；在所评估设置中，更强的文本生成引导与更强的智能体引导表现相关联。

Conclusion: MetaSteer 表明非线性、上下文相关的干预可用于引导 LLM 并具备零样本迁移能力，性能可比或优于任务特定基线；论文还讨论了其几何轨迹效应、能力保持以及可迁移引导带来的安全性考量。

Abstract: Steering large language models typically relies on linear, context-independent interventions in activation space, an assumption that recent work has challenged and that can induce an information bottleneck when a fixed representation must encode many behavioral distinctions. We introduce MetaSteer, a method that learns nonlinear interventions with context-dependent effects and applies them to attention projection matrices, producing activation effects that vary with the input context by construction and requiring no linear concept-geometry assumption. Framed as preference-based optimization, MetaSteer is trained once on a pooled preference corpus and transferred zero-shot to unseen concepts and out-of-distribution contexts. We find that, despite using low-rank adapters, MetaSteer induces structured, context-dependent changes in hidden-state trajectories while partially preserving aspects of their local trajectory dynamics, including velocity and curvature. We evaluate MetaSteer on three controlled text-generation benchmarks and three agentic settings across multiple model families and scales. MetaSteer matches or outperforms strong task-specific steering baselines on most aggregate comparisons in the zero-shot regime. Across the evaluated settings, stronger text-generation steering is associated with stronger agentic steering performance. We further discuss geometric trajectory effects, capability retention, and safety considerations raised by transferable steering.

</details>


### [89] [Training LLM Judges from Language Feedback via Position-Selective Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38792)
*Ilgee Hong,Changlong Yu,Zhenghao Xu,Xin Liu,Yuwei Zhang,Qin Lu,Bing Yin,Tuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过基于熵偏移的位置掩码改进自蒸馏，训练出泛化性更强的LLM评判器，在主观任务上超越结果监督RL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主观任务的评判高度依赖所采用的评估标准及其权重。主流的结果监督强化学习（如GRPO）只用最终判决准确率这一标量来为整个rollout的所有token分配信用，无法对"标准选择"位置的token单独分配信用，也忽略了偏好标签天然伴随的丰富语言反馈（如偏好理由）。自蒸馏（SD）可利用这些语言反馈提供密集的、位置级别的监督，但并非所有位置的信号都同等有用。

Method: 利用教师与学生之间的逐位置熵偏移，识别出两种模式：上下文锐化（教师将概率集中于某个与反馈一致的特定标准表述）和上下文扩散（教师在多个与反馈一致的备选表述间分配概率）。作者将前者解读为鼓励记忆特定表述，后者则通过保留备选促进语义理解。基于这种不对称性，提出基于熵偏移的位置掩码，保留熵偏移分布的下尾部分，掩掉高熵偏移位置。

Result: 掩蔽高熵偏移位置相比朴素自蒸馏提升了分布外泛化能力。所得的自蒸馏评判器在所评估的主观子类别上比结果监督RL训练的评判器高出2-9个百分点，同时在客观类别上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 逐位置的熵偏移可作为自蒸馏中有用监督信号的判别依据；保留低熵偏移（扩散）位置、掩蔽高熵偏移（锐化）位置，能够提升LLM评判器的泛化性和主观任务表现。

Abstract: We study training LLM judges from natural language feedback, especially for subjective tasks where the verdict depends strongly on which evaluation criteria the judge invokes and how it weighs them. The dominant approach, outcome-supervised RL (e.g., GRPO), credits every token in the rollout with a single scalar determined only by the accuracy of the final verdict, providing no separate credit at the criterion-choice tokens and ignoring the rich language feedback (e.g., preference rationales) that naturally accompanies preference labels. Self-Distillation (SD) is one natural way to use this language feedback: the same model, conditioned on this feedback, acts as a teacher providing dense, position-level supervision. However, not all positions carry equally useful signal. Using the per-position entropy shift between teacher and student, we identify two regimes: context sharpening, where the teacher concentrates probability on a particular feedback-aligned criterion expression, and context spreading, where the teacher distributes probability across multiple feedback-aligned alternatives. We interpret these patterns as follows: sharpening encourages memorization of a particular criterion expression, whereas spreading promotes semantic understanding by preserving these alternatives. Motivated by this asymmetry, we introduce position masking based on the entropy shift that retains the lower tail of the entropy-shift distribution. Experiments show that masking higher-entropy-shift positions improves out-of-distribution generalization over naive SD. The resulting self-distilled judges outperform judges trained with outcome-supervised RL by 2-9 percentage points on the evaluated subjective subcategories, while remaining competitive on objective ones.

</details>


### [90] [Recovering Off-Policy Supervision for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38795)
*Jungseob Lee,Chanjun Park,Sugyeong Eo,Hyeonseok Moon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于 rollout 的投机解码草稿模型训练框架，通过 ALR 与 IRA 两个组件恢复块内全部监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多块草稿模型常在外部模型生成的语料上训练，一旦块内出现一个 off-policy token，后续所有槽位的监督都会失效；已有方法直接丢弃这些发散槽位，造成严重监督损失。

Method: 框架包含两部分：(1) Anchor-Label Relabelling (ALR)，用贪心目标 rollout 的分布替换语料标签，恢复所有预测槽位的有效监督；(2) In-Rollout Anchors (IRA)，将草稿块直接置于这些 rollout 中，使草稿模型接触目标模型生成的上下文，并复用预计算的 rollout 特征，不增加额外目标开销。

Result: 在固定的视觉-语言与纯文本语料上，贪心接受长度相比 DFlash 最高提升 36.5%，并持续优于擦除类基线；单轮训练即超过最佳擦除调度，三轮后达到在目标模型再生响应上训练的效果。

Conclusion: 该框架为在固定语料上训练投机解码草稿模型提供了有效且计算高效的方法，且无需修改原始文本。

Abstract: Block drafters for speculative decoding are commonly trained on corpora written by external models, where a single off-policy token invalidates supervision for all subsequent slots in a block. Existing approaches discard these divergent slots, resulting in severe supervision loss. To resolve this problem while preserving the training corpus, we propose a rollout-based training framework that recovers full supervision through two complementary components. The first component, Anchor-Label Relabelling (ALR), replaces corpus labels with distributions from greedy target rollouts, restoring valid supervision across all predicted slots. The second component, In-Rollout Anchors (IRA), places draft blocks directly inside these rollouts to expose the drafter to target-generated context, reusing precomputed rollout features at no additional target cost. Across fixed vision-language and text corpora, our framework increases greedy accepted length by up to 36.5% over DFlash and consistently outperforms erasing baselines. Notably, a single epoch of our method surpasses the best erase schedules. After three epochs, it matches the acceptance length of training on target-regenerated responses. These results show that our framework provides an effective and compute-efficient approach for training speculative drafters on fixed corpora without modifying the original text. Code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/ALR-IRA.

</details>


### [91] [Overlap, Unique and Conflict: Can LLMs Extract What They Can Recognize?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38799)
*Eftekhar Hossain,Santu Karmaker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出跨叙事任务OUC抽取（重叠-独有-冲突），构建约2.2万叙事对、14万实例的基准，评测14个开源LLM，发现独有信息易抽取、重叠与冲突难抽取，瓶颈在于从句配对与抽取而非关系识别，任务特定微调可显著缩小差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多视角替代叙事需要识别不同来源信息之间的一致、冲突与差异。然而现有跨文本关系研究多聚焦于对预定义文本对进行关系分类（如蕴含、矛盾），而非从完整叙事中直接抽取此类信息，存在明显空白。

Method: 提出Overlap-Unique-Conflict（OUC）抽取任务，即从两段叙事中抽取所有重叠、冲突与独有的从句；构建约22K叙事对、140K OUC实例的基准，覆盖事实、论辩与政治话语；评测14个开源LLM（0.6B–35B），并进行诊断分析，以及任务特定监督的微调实验。

Result: 独有信息抽取远易于重叠与冲突：最强模型Gemma-4-31B在重叠上F1仅61.13%、冲突48.58%，而独有超过75%。诊断表明困难并非仅来自关系识别，而是源于从完整叙事中配对并抽取对应从句的失败，小模型尤甚。微调后Qwen-3-8B较基线提升15–28个百分点，并在多项任务上超越约四倍规模模型（如Qwen-3.6-35B）。

Conclusion: 任务特定监督能显著缩小差距，但重叠与冲突抽取仍远未令人满意，跨叙事从句抽取仍是开放挑战。

Abstract: Understanding multi-perspective alternative narratives requires identifying how their information agrees, conflicts, or differs across sources. Existing work on cross-text relations largely focuses on categorizing relations between predefined text pairs, such as entailment or contradiction, rather than directly extracting such information from full narratives. To address this gap, we introduce Overlap-Unique-Conflict (OUC) extraction, a cross-narrative task that extracts all overlapping, conflicting, and unique clauses from two narratives. To support this study, we construct a benchmark of approximately 22K narrative pairs and 140K OUC instances spanning factual, argumentative, and political discourse. Evaluating 14 open-source LLMs (0.6B-35B), we find that unique information is far easier to extract than overlap and conflict: the strongest model, Gemma-4-31B, reaches only 61.13% F1-score on overlap and 48.58% on conflict, against more than 75% on unique. Further diagnostic analysis reveals that this difficulty does not stem from relation recognition alone, but rather from a failure to pair and extract the corresponding clauses from full narratives, especially in smaller models. Nevertheless, learning these extractions with task-specific supervision narrows the gap considerably: a fine-tuned Qwen-3-8B gains 15-28% absolute over its baseline and surpasses models roughly four times its size (e.g., Qwen-3.6-35B) on several tasks. Even so, overlap and conflict remain well below satisfactory, leaving cross-narrative clause extraction an open challenge.

</details>


### [92] [Uncovering Uncontrolled Repetition through Residual Stream Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38802)
*Yuanhe Zhang,Xinyao Zhou,Haoran Gao,Yuyao Zhang,Zhenhong Zhou,Fanyu Meng,Li Sun,Sen Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Tokenwise Residual Comparison (TRC)方法，在残差动态层面定位并抑制LVLM/LLM的失控重复，平均降低57%循环率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM自回归生成中的失控重复会延长生成并导致资源消耗攻击。已有分析仅关注中后层强激活特征，但对重复活动如何在这些层之前产生和发展理解不足。

Method: 提出Tokenwise Residual Comparison (TRC)，通过比较生成token间注意力与MLP对残差流的写入来识别重复相关异常，定位对应层并选择性抑制残差流中的坐标。

Result: TRC有效缓解失控重复，平均降低57%循环率；分析显示重复语义在浅层出现并沿残差流传播，破坏正常表示；方法可推广至LLM和大型推理模型（LRM）。

Conclusion: 研究将重复生成的分析从显著内部表示扩展到更早的干预机会，为缓解资源消耗攻击提供新思路。

Abstract: Uncontrolled repetition can prolong autoregressive generation in large language models (LLMs) and enable resource consumption attacks. Prior analyses of repetitive generation have identified strongly activated features in intermediate and late layers. However, how uncontrolled repetition activity emerges and develops before becoming prominent in these layers remains insufficiently understood. In this paper, we investigate this question primarily in large vision-language models (LVLMs), which support a richer set of uncontrolled repetitions through both visual and textual inputs. We propose Tokenwise Residual Comparison (TRC), a method that identifies and localizes anomalies associated with repetition from residual dynamics during generation. TRC compares attention and multilayer perceptron writes to the residual stream across generated tokens to identify patterns associated with repetition. It then selectively suppresses coordinates in the residual stream at the identified layer. Experiments show that TRC effectively mitigates uncontrolled repetition, reducing loop rates by 57\% on average. Our analysis further shows that repetition semantics emerge in shallow layers and propagate through the residual stream, disrupting normal representations. TRC also generalizes to large language models (LLMs) and large reasoning models (LRMs), where it consistently captures analogous repetition dynamics and achieves effective mitigation. Our work broadens the study of repetitive generation from its prominent internal representations to earlier opportunities for intervention, providing insights for mitigating resource consumption attacks.

</details>


### [93] [StateTree: Enhancing Long-Term Dialogue Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38809)
*Naen Xu,Wanqing Cui,Yibo Hu,Shixin Hong,Hengyu An,Meiguang Jin,Junfeng Ma,Tianyu Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 StateTree，一种数据驱动的强化学习方法，通过树结构路径追踪辅助任务解决长时对话推理中的证据分散、偏好更新与长上下文训练成本问题，在 10K token 训练后泛化至 128K，并在 LongMemEval 上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人化助手类大模型需要在长期、演进的交互历史中进行推理，但相关证据分散于多个会话、用户偏好随时间被修改，而标准长上下文训练在数据稀缺和计算代价高昂的情况下难以应对这些问题。

Method: StateTree 从稀缺对话中构造可验证真值的挑战性辅助任务：在多会话对话中嵌入键值记录形成二叉树，模型需跨会话检索记录并比较时间戳来解算分支，从根遍历到叶并找出隐藏目标问题。采用课程式强化学习逐步增加树深度，并引入边携带步骤级推理片段的组合变体，训练模型将部分线索组合成连贯查询。

Result: 仅在 10K token 上下文上训练即可泛化到 128K token，且无需全长 RL 成本，具备跨会话检索、时间推理、知识更新与组合式多跳推理能力。性能超过 SFT 与 RL 基线，同时保持短上下文通用推理能力。StateTree-7B 在 LongMemEval（128k）上提升高达 +23.60%，StateTree-14B 达到 59.00% 准确率，超过 QwenLong-L1-32B 的 45.20%。

Conclusion: StateTree 通过构造可验证的树结构辅助任务与课程式 RL 训练，以低成本实现了长时对话推理能力的有效泛化与显著性能提升。

Abstract: Large language models deployed as personalized assistants must reason over long, evolving interaction histories. However, in long-term dialogue reasoning, relevant evidence is scattered across sessions, preferences may be revised over time, and standard long-context training fails to address these challenges under data scarcity and prohibitive computational costs. We propose StateTree, a data-driven RL method that constructs a challenging auxiliary task from scarce dialogues with verifiable ground truth. StateTree augments multi-session dialogues with a tree-structured path-tracing task: key-value records are embedded across sessions to form a binary tree. Solving the task requires the model to traverse from root to leaf by retrieving records across sessions and comparing timestamps to resolve branches, then recover the hidden target question among distractor leaves. We apply curriculum RL training progressively increasing tree depth and introduce a compositional variant whose edges carry step-level reasoning fragments, training the model to compose partial cues into coherent queries. Trained on 10K-token contexts, StateTree generalizes to 128K tokens without full-length RL costs and exhibits capabilities including cross-session retrieval, temporal reasoning, knowledge update, and compositional multi-hop reasoning. StateTree outperforms both SFT and RL-based baselines while preserving short-context general reasoning. StateTree-7B achieves gains up to +23.60% on LongMemEval (128k), and StateTree-14B reaches 59.00% accuracy on LongMemEval, surpassing QwenLong-L1-32B (45.20%).

</details>


### [94] [Can Terminal Agents Trust Their Own Verification? Diagnosing and Improving Self-Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38812)
*Yingfeng Luo,Shaowei Wei,Daixin Wang,Dingyang Lin,Kaiyan Chang,Weiqiao Shan,Tong Zheng,Zhiqiang Zhang,Jingbo Zhu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究终端智能体自我验证的可信度，发现错误检测与修复是薄弱环节，并提出学生条件验证蒸馏（SCVD）方法显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端智能体依赖自我验证来评估和纠正解，但其可信度尚不清楚，尤其缺乏对验证与恢复行为的系统量化研究。

Method: 提出诊断框架：识别轨迹中首个完整解，判定其是否客观正确，并以该真值量化智能体后续的验证与恢复行为。在TerminalBench2.1上评估十个终端智能体。基于发现提出SCVD：让学生先产生候选解，再蒸馏更强教师在相同交互上下文下的验证与恢复过程。

Result: 验证几乎普遍发生，但仅61.43%的错误候选被检测到，检测到的错误中仅49.36%被成功修复。SCVD在三个Qwen3.5骨干上使TerminalBench2.1的Pass@1提升9.74–16.85个百分点（相比基座模型），比标准全轨迹蒸馏高4.49–8.61个百分点，同时避免了全轨迹蒸馏在SWE-bench Verified上明显的分布外退化。

Conclusion: 自我验证的主要弱点不在启动验证，而在错误检测与修复；SCVD通过聚焦学生候选后的验证与恢复蒸馏，有效提升终端智能体性能并保持分布外稳健性。

Abstract: Terminal agents rely on self-verification to assess and correct their solutions as they solve tasks through interaction with command-line environments. Yet how trustworthy such self-verification is remains poorly understood. To investigate this question systematically, we introduce a diagnostic framework that identifies the first complete solution in each trajectory, determines whether it is objectively correct, and uses this ground truth to quantify the agent's subsequent verification and recovery behavior. Applying it to ten terminal agents on TerminalBench2.1, we find that verification is nearly universal after a complete candidate is formed, yet only 61.43\% of incorrect candidates are detected and only 49.36\% of detected errors are successfully repaired. These results show that the main weakness in self-verification lies not in initiating verification, but in detecting and repairing errors. Motivated by these findings, we propose Student-Conditioned Verification Distillation (SCVD), which lets the student first produce a candidate solution and distills a stronger teacher's subsequent verification and recovery from the same interaction context. Across three Qwen3.5 backbones, SCVD improves \textsc{Pass@1} on TerminalBench2.1 by 9.74--16.85 percentage points over the corresponding base models and by 4.49--8.61 points over the standard full-trajectory distillation, while avoiding the pronounced out-of-distribution degradation of full-trajectory distillation on SWE-bench Verified.

</details>


### [95] [You're Hired: Strategic Model Selection for LLM Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38816)
*Zongwan Cao,Ziyuan Yang,Shangbin Feng,Michael Duan,Skyler Hallinan,Bingbing Wen,Lucy Lu Wang,Yulia Tsvetkov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究多LLM系统中的模型选择问题，提出并系统评估了9种选择算法分类，实验表明能力与训练相关的选择策略最优，可显著超越随机或启发式团队组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体与模型协作算法虽能结合不同LLM的优势，但受限于预定义、人工构建的模型池，缺乏对模型选择这一关键问题的系统研究。

Method: 提出并系统评估9种选择算法的分类体系，涵盖模型描述多样性、能力感知的行为多样性以及基于LLM的招募器；在两个规模为10和32的候选模型池上，结合四种模型协作算法，跨数学、编码、问答与推理任务进行大量实验。

Result: 有效的选择算法比随机或仅挑选个体表现最优模型的启发式团队最多提升36.1%；能力与训练相关的选择策略可降低选择方差并取得最佳表现；更大的候选池对浅层启发式方法挑战更大，而基于与候选模型交互、理解模型能力的算法能稳健过滤不一致、不安全的模型，并泛化到新奇的分布外任务。

Conclusion: 有原则且有依据的团队选择对构建高效多LLM系统至关重要，论文给出了推荐采用的强模型选择算法。

Abstract: While multi-agent and model collaboration algorithms gain traction to combine the strengths of diverse Large Language Models (LLMs), existing systems remain bottlenecked on pre-defined and hand-crafted model pools. In this work, we investigate the problem of model selection in multi-LLM systems. We propose and systematically evaluate a taxonomy of 9 selection algorithms ranging from diversity of model descriptions, capability-aware behavioral diversity, and LLM-based recruiters. We conduct extensive experiments across two candidate pools of 10 and 32 models, deployed in four model collaboration algorithms, and evaluated across tasks spanning math, coding, QA, and reasoning. Results demonstrate that successful selection algorithms greatly outperform random or heuristics-based teams such as merely selecting the models with top individual performance, by up to 36.1% across settings. Specifically, capability- and training-based selection strategies alleviate selection variance and achieve the best performance, which we recommend to employ before deploying real-world multi-LLM systems. Further analysis reveals that larger candidate pools pose greater challenges to shallow selection heuristics, while algorithms grounded in interacting with candidate models and understanding model capability robustly filter out misaligned, unsafe models, as well as generalizing to novel, out-of-distribution tasks. Together, we establish that principled and informed team selection is critical and present strong model selection algorithms for assembling effective multi-LLM systems.

</details>


### [96] [BARRAC: Adaptation of an English Aspect-based Sentiment Analysis Approach for Classification Tasks in Arabic Dialects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38820)
*Ali Almutairi,Gelareh Mohammadi,Imran Razzak,Aditya Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 BARRAC 框架，将英语方面的情感分析框架适配到阿拉伯语分类任务，在五个阿拉伯语方言数据集上取得优于少样本 SOTA 和 GPT-4o 的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语 NLP 发展迅速，已有诸多模型、数据集和基准，但本文关注一个核心问题：为英语等主流语言开发的方法能否迁移到阿拉伯语任务上。作者希望探索任务特定方法的适配，作为模型、数据集适配之外的另一条路径。

Method: BARRAC（Brainstorming Alignment and Replaced Representation learning for ArabiC tasks）将英语方面的情感分析框架迁移到阿拉伯语分类任务。它用阿拉伯语语言手段与标记（针对方言情感、讽刺和方言识别）替换消费者评论属性池，并用两阶段训练替代带噪自训练。

Result: 在五个阿拉伯语方言数据集上评估，BARRAC 取得 63.93% 的平均 macro-F1，比最佳少标签 SOTA 高出 3%，并在五分之四的任务上超过 GPT-4o。误差分析揭示了仍存在的挑战。

Conclusion: 结果表明，除了适配模型、数据集和基准之外，适配任务特定方法也是阿拉伯语 NLP 的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: With the rapid growth of Arabic NLP, several models, datasets and benchmarks have been reported. This paper asks whether approaches developed for majority languages like English can be adapted to Arabic tasks. We adapt an English aspect-based sentiment analysis framework to Arabic classification tasks and present the adaptation as BARRAC: Brainstorming Alignment and Replaced Representation learning for ArabiC tasks. BARRAC replaces consumer-review attribute pools with Arabic linguistic devices and markers for dialectal sentiment, sarcasm, and dialect identification, and replaces noisy self-training with two-stage training. Evaluated on five Arabic dialect datasets, BARRAC achieves a mean macro-F1 of 63.93\%, outperforming the best few-label SOTA by 3\%, and outperforming GPT-4o on four out of five tasks. Error analysis provides insights into remaining challenges. These results demonstrate that adapting task-specific approaches is a promising direction for Arabic NLP alongside adapting models, datasets and benchmarks.

</details>


### [97] [Forging LLM Authorship Fingerprints with Targeted Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38831)
*Haohan Yuan,Simin Chen,Xi Niu,Hanqing Guo,Depeng Xu,Haopeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究"目标指纹转移"问题：通过改写一个模型的输出文本，使模型归属分类器将其误判为指定的目标模型，并提出 ForgePrint 框架实现该攻击，证明文本归属检测的准确率不等于来源真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型归属分类器常被用来判断文本由哪个语言模型生成，被视为来源证据。但仅在未修改文本上准确，并不能说明在文本被刻意改写后仍能识别原始来源，因此需要研究归属信号在定向改写下的鲁棒性问题。

Method: 将问题形式化为"目标指纹转移"，选择摘要任务作为受控的条件生成场景（不同模型接收同一文档、表达相同内容）。提出 ForgePrint 的"先搜索后蒸馏"框架：先搜索能将归属推向目标指纹的改写，再将筛选出的改写蒸馏为一个单次前向的 4B Student 模型。

Result: 在 CNN/DM 上，Student 达到 70.2% 的目标成功率，优于其 Teacher（54.1%）以及六个公开改写基线中最强者（39.3%），且攻击从未查询过这些留出分类器。在将开源模型摘要转向指定商业模型时，成功率也达 68.3%。

Conclusion: 指纹可检测性不应与来源真实性混为一谈；即使文本归属检测在未修改文本上准确，在定向改写下也可能给出误导性的模型身份证据。

Abstract: Model-attribution classifiers can often identify which language model produced a text, making model-specific writing patterns a signal of provenance. Accurate attribution on unmodified text, however, does not show whether the prediction still identifies the original source after deliberate rewriting. We formulate this problem as targeted fingerprint transfer: rewriting one model's output so that attribution classifiers assign it to a chosen target model. We study summarization, where different models receive the same document and express the same underlying content, providing a controlled setting for conditional generation. We introduce ForgePrint, a search-then-distil framework that first searches for rewrites that move attribution toward a target fingerprint, then distils the selected rewrites into a one-pass 4B Student model. On CNN/DM, the Student reaches 70.2% target success rate, outperforming both its Teacher (54.1%) and the strongest of six published rewriting baselines (39.3%), against held-out classifiers that are never queried by the attack. It also reaches 68.3% target success when transferring summaries from an open model toward chosen commercial models. These results show that fingerprint detectability should not be conflated with source authenticity, and that text-only attribution can provide misleading evidence of model identity under targeted rewriting, even when it is accurate on unmodified text.

</details>


### [98] [Scaling Parameter and Context in Attention: Native Sparse Attention from Mixture-of-Head](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38832)
*Zizhuo Fu,Runsheng Wang,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出NAMOH架构原生稀疏注意力机制，通过每token激活部分注意力头实现参数缩放与上下文缩放协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缩放注意力参数可提升语言模型质量，但保留完整token历史使额外注意力头在长上下文下代价高昂；同时注意力用于检索与组合上下文信息，参数缩放也应支持更长上下文，因此探索注意力参数缩放能否直接实现高效有效的上下文缩放。

Method: NAMOH是一种架构原生稀疏注意力机制：每个token激活H个头中的K个，每个头仅保留其分配到的token并在该子序列内做因果注意力；头选择同时决定激活参数与可用上下文，无需扫描完整历史。在均衡分配下，固定K增大H可缩短头历史并减少每token的KV访问而不增加总KV存储；并支持头相对旋转位置编码以缩短路由子序列内的位置跨度，缓解位置引起的注意力噪声。

Result: 实验表明NAMOH可优于同等总参数的完全激活模型，并在长上下文推理中比具有相同激活参数量的更小稠密模型更高效；与GQA及现有稀疏注意力机制兼容。

Conclusion: 该工作为注意力缩放提供新路径，使参数缩放可直接驱动上下文缩放。

Abstract: Scaling attention parameters can improve language model quality, but retaining full token histories makes additional heads costly at long contexts. Furthermore, since attention retrieves and combines contextual information, parameter scaling should also support longer contexts. We therefore ask whether attention parameter scaling can directly enable efficient and effective context scaling. We introduce NAMOH, an architecture-native sparse attention mechanism that activates $K$ of $H$ heads per token. Each head retains only its assigned tokens and performs causal attention within this subsequence. Head selection thus jointly determines active parameters and available context without scanning the full history. Under balanced assignments, increasing $H$ at fixed $K$ shortens head histories and reduces per-token key-value (KV) access without increasing total KV storage. We further support head-relative rotary position embeddings to shorten positional spans within routed subsequences, aiming to mitigate position-induced attention noise. Experiments show that NAMOH can outperform fully activated models with the same total parameters, while enabling more efficient long-context inference than smaller dense models with matched active parameter counts. It remains compatible with GQA and existing sparse attention mechanisms. We hope this work offers a new path for scaling attention, with parameter scaling directly enabling context scaling.

</details>


### [99] [Where MLLMs Fail and Why: Causal Task Decomposition for Capability Failure Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38851)
*Xia Hu,Brian Potetz,Chun-Ta Lu,Huanfen Yao,Leonidas Guibas,Zhicheng Wang,Howard Zhou,Pengfei Xing,Andrew Gallagher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出因果分解框架CADET，通过干预前置依赖来区分MLLM组合任务失败源于内在能力缺陷还是上游级联错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端准确率只能记录MLLM在组合任务上失败的频率，无法区分失败是目标能力的内在缺陷，还是来自上游前置能力的级联错误，因此需要一种能隔离两种失败模式的诊断方法。

Method: 提出因果分解框架，对每个任务的前置依赖做受控干预：用能力指标（NC、IC、RC）在无辅助、前置正确、前置错误三种条件下评分任务；用贡献指标（N-Score、S-Score，源自因果概率）量化各前置条件的必要性与充分性。据此构建CADET诊断基准，含10个组合任务、分解为46个单位任务、超33,000道人工标注问题，覆盖感知、空间、时间与认知类别。

Result: 诊断前沿MLLM发现端到端准确率掩盖的系统性规律：提供正确前置条件可消除认知任务54%的错误，使其从最弱提升至超过空间与时间任务；因果贡献集中于少数关键前置条件，仅提供最关键的一项即可获得提供全部前置条件84%的收益。

Conclusion: 该因果分解框架与CADET基准能有效定位MLLM组合失败的来源，揭示能力层面的短板与关键前置依赖，为更有针对性的模型诊断与改进提供依据。

Abstract: End-to-end accuracy on compositional tasks records how often MLLMs fail, but cannot distinguish whether a failure reflects an intrinsic deficit in the targeted capability or a cascading error from an upstream prerequisite. We propose a causal decomposition framework that isolates these two failure modes through controlled interventions on the prerequisite dependencies of each task. Our capability metrics (NC, IC, RC) score each task under unassisted, correct, or incorrect prerequisites to diagnose where failures arise; contribution metrics (N-Score, S-Score), adapted from probabilities of causation, quantify each prerequisite's necessity and sufficiency to determine why. We instantiate the framework in CADET, a diagnostic benchmark of 10 composite tasks decomposed into 46 unit tasks with over 33,000 human-annotated questions spanning perception, spatial, temporal, and cognitive categories. Diagnosing frontier MLLMs with our framework uncovers systematic patterns that end-to-end accuracy obscures. Capability-wise, supplying correct prerequisites eliminates 54\% of errors on cognitive tasks, lifting them from weakest to above spatial and temporal. Prerequisite-wise, causal contributions are concentrated in a few critical prerequisites, and supplying the single most important one alone captures 84\% of the gain from supplying all prerequisites.

</details>


### [100] [TRACE: Target-Aware Retrieval, Attributed Evidence, and Contract-Constrained Extraction for LitTraceQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38861)
*Sachin Gupta,Divya Godara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TRACE 系统针对"可验证问答"中的接地契约缺口，通过目标分组检索、类型化证据定位与多模态表格抽取，在 LitTraceQA 基准上取得 0.760613 的官方测试成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 找到相关论文并不等于能从中产生可验证的答案。LitTraceQA 要求规范论文标识符、页面或对象级别的精确证据、以及与评测器匹配的类型化答案。论文将"源访问"与"评分器可见正确性"之间的分离称为 grounding contract gap（接地契约缺口），本文正是为填补这一缺口而提出。

Method: 提出 TRACE（Target-Aware Retrieval, Attributed Evidence, and Contract-Constrained Extraction），包含五项组件：目标分组检索、独立类型化证据定位、多模态表格抽取、模式驱动表格构建、失败即拒绝（fail-closed）验证。系统通过段落、对象、别名、引用和稠密表示索引 27,487 篇论文，并在每个信号中保留问题目标。表格处理上，先预测观察单元再抽取数值，并用与评测器兼容的键归一化组装行。

Result: 经审计的干净赛道产物在官方 71 题测试集上得分 0.760613，其中论文 F1 0.9728、证据 F1 0.6847、多选题准确率 0.9800、表行 F1 0.5423、宏单元准确率 0.3508。在 11 条公开开发集表格记录上，干净基线与坐标感知视觉填充的表行 F1 分别为 0.291 和 0.411（该诊断含回退输出，非官方测试结论）。

Conclusion: 剩余错误主要集中在定位符、观察单元、行键与源值身份四类问题上，说明证据级与表格单元格级的精确对齐仍是主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Finding a relevant paper is not the same as producing a verifiable answer from it. LitTraceQA requires canonical paper identifiers, exact evidence at the page or object level, and typed answers that match the evaluator. We call the separation between source access and scorer-visible correctness the grounding contract gap. TRACE - Target-Aware Retrieval, Attributed Evidence, and Contract-Constrained Extraction - addresses this gap with target-grouped retrieval, independent typed evidence localization, multimodal table extraction, schema-driven table construction, and fail-closed validation. It indexes 27,487 papers through passage, object, alias, citation, and dense representations while retaining the question target behind each signal. For tables, TRACE predicts the observation unit before extracting values and assembles rows with evaluator-compatible key normalization. Our audited selected clean-track artifact scores 0.760613 on the official 71-question test set, including 0.9728 paper F1, 0.6847 evidence F1, 0.9800 multiple-choice accuracy, 0.5423 table-row F1, and 0.3508 macro cell accuracy. On 11 public-development table records, a clean baseline and coordinate-aware visual fill obtain row F1 of 0.291 and 0.411, respectively; this diagnostic comparison includes fallback outputs and is not an official-test claim. Remaining errors chiefly concern locator, observation-unit, row-key, and source-value identity.

</details>


### [101] [GraphForge: Training Working Agents with Graph-Anchored Workspace Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38923)
*Qisheng Su,Hanchen Wang,Guanru Zhu,Huicheng Jiang,Qiuyinzhe Zhang,Kou Shi,Zhen Fang,Ziao Zhang,Qingnan Ren,Zehui Chen,Tao Gui,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 GraphForge：基于证据图、扎根真实文件的任务与验证数据合成框架，用于训练文件处理类智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练能读写多样文件、协调工具并产出交付物的智能体，需要大量基于真实文件且结果可验证的任务，但现有流水线要么用模型生成文件（缺乏真实性与多样性），要么在真实文件上建任务却缺少任务专属验证器，导致结果质量无法保证。

Method: GraphForge 以职业驱动的种子保证多样性，为每个种子组装真实文件工作区，并在文件关系上构建证据图；任务陈述与评分细则均由该图派生，使任务要求有工作区文件支撑、每条标准锚定到可验证所需文件。经初始 rollout 检验可执行性，并由修订智能体对照原始文件修复任务与评分细则，之后再收集轨迹。基于 2,169 条轨迹对 Qwen3.6-27B 进行微调，并用证据锚定评分细则筛选候选，对 SFT 模型自身 rollout 做拒绝微调。

Result: 在 OpenHands 下 GDPVal 达 1445.7（+65.7）；在 Claude Code 下 Workspace-Bench-Lite 与 SpreadsheetBench II 分别达 63.7（+7.7）和 24.0（+13.7）。基于评分细则筛选的拒绝微调在三个基准上均有进一步提升，表明评分细则可作为有效的选择信号。

Conclusion: GraphForge 通过证据图将任务生成与结果验证同时扎根于真实文件，可规模化合成高质量、可验证的智能体训练数据，且评分细则兼具验证与数据筛选价值；数据与模型已公开。

Abstract: Working agents need to read diverse files, coordinate tools, and produce deliverables. Training such agents requires tasks built on many real files with verifiable results, but few pipelines exist to synthesize this kind of data. Existing pipelines either generate files with models, which lack realism and diversity, or build tasks on real files without task-specific verifiers, leaving result quality unchecked. We introduce GraphForge, an evidence-graph based framework that grounds both the task and its verification in real files. Starting from occupation-grounded seeds for controlled diversity, GraphForge assembles a workspace of real files for each seed and builds an evidence graph over their relations. Since the task statement and rubrics are both derived from this graph, task requirements are backed by the workspace files and each criterion is anchored to the files needed to verify it. An initial rollout further tests executability, and a revision agent repairs the task and rubrics against the original files before trajectories are collected. Fine-tuning Qwen3.6-27B on 2,169 GraphForge trajectories brings GDPVal to 1445.7 (+65.7) under OpenHands, and Workspace-Bench-Lite and SpreadsheetBench II to 63.7 (+7.7) and 24.0 (+13.7) under Claude Code. Rejection fine-tuning on the SFT model's own rollouts, with candidates selected by the evidence-anchored rubrics, yields further improvements on all three benchmarks, suggesting that the rubrics provide a useful selection signal. The data and models are available.

</details>


### [102] [Making LLMs Say What They Think: Measuring and Improving CoT-Interpretability Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38972)
*Yihuai Hong,Shauli Ravfogel,Chen Zhao,Eunsol Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出CIA指标衡量LLM的思维链（CoT）与内部推理策略的一致性，发现现有模型对齐度有限（44.8-75.9%），并通过后训练同时以任务准确率和参数化忠实度信号为奖励来显著提升忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT常被视为LLM得出答案过程的代理，但越来越多证据表明CoT往往无法反映模型内部真实计算，甚至可以在不改变最终答案的情况下被篡改，因此需要衡量并提升CoT与内部计算之间的对齐程度。

Method: 提出CoT-Interpretability Alignment（CIA）指标，通过可解释性工具检测模型的内部推理策略，衡量其与CoT痕迹的一致程度；在三个任务（两跳问答、暗示干预、整数乘法）和三个LLM上评估CIA；随后通过后训练，将任务准确率和参数化忠实度信号同时设为奖励来改进CIA。

Result: LLM在三个任务上的CIA对齐度有限，范围仅为44.8%–75.9%；后训练实验显示可以大幅提升CoT参数化忠实度，同时保持或提升任务准确率，并进一步分析了泛化模式。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个审计CoT参数化忠实度的框架，以及使模型显式推理更可信的路径，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) traces often serve as a proxy for how Large Language Models (LLMs) arrive at their answers. However, growing evidence shows that models' CoT often fails to reflect their internal computations and can be changed without affecting their final answers. In this work, we measure and improve the alignment between the reasoning described in an LLM's CoT and what it computes internally. We propose CoT-Interpretability Alignment (CIA), a metric that measures the agreement between a model's CoT traces and its internal reasoning strategies as detected by interpretability tools. We evaluate CIA on three tasks (two-hop question answering, hint intervention, and integer multiplication) across three LLMs, finding that LLMs exhibit limited alignment across all tasks (44.8-75.9%). We then experiment with improving CIA via post-training, setting both the task accuracy and parametric faithfulness signals as a reward. Experiments show that we can substantially improve CoT parametric faithfulness while maintaining or improving the task accuracy. We provide rich analysis, such as their generalization patterns. Our work provides both a framework for auditing CoT parametric faithfulness and a pathway toward making models' explicit reasoning more trustworthy. Code and data are available at https://github.com/yihuaihong/CIA-minimal-repro.

</details>


### [103] [Fairness Beyond a Single Run: Training-Seed Variability in Speech LLM Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38976)
*Srishti Ginjala,Eric Fosler-Lussier,Srinivasan Parthasarathy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 语音LLM的人口统计公平性差距主要由随机种子而非音频压缩率决定，单次训练运行的公平性报告不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动语音识别中的人口统计公平性差距几乎总是基于单次训练运行来报告，但单次结果可能受到随机因素的强烈影响，导致结论不可复现或误导。作者希望厘清随机种子与音频压缩这两个因素对公平性指标的相对贡献。

Method: 在5种音频压缩因子和6个随机种子下，对语音LLM的Q-former投影器和LoRA适配器进行微调，固定编码器、基础解码器、数据和解码过程，并在Common Voice与Fair-Speech上评估每次运行。使用平衡的3×3分解来量化种子与压缩对公平性指标变异的贡献。

Result: 在460小时干净LibriSpeech上，种子对多数人口统计轴上的公平性指标影响大于压缩。对Fair-Speech种族的归一化差距，种子贡献了85.3%的变异，压缩仅8.3%（p=0.009），但压缩在年龄和性别上解释更多。种子间LibriSpeech留出词错误率仅差0.04点，而Common Voice差达8.57点，说明并非失败运行，且该效应在控制准确率和dropout后仍存在。将适配集扩展到960小时可减弱但未消除该效应。

Conclusion: 在Fair-Speech种族维度上，两个单次运行系统需在归一化差距上相差超过0.30，才能超出种子本身带来的变异性。因此单次训练的公平性报告不足以支撑可靠结论。

Abstract: Demographic fairness gaps in automatic speech recognition are almost always reported from a single training run. We fine-tune the Q-former projector and LoRA adapters of a speech LLM at five audio compression factors and six random seeds, holding the encoder, base decoder, data and decoding fixed, and evaluate every run on Common Voice and Fair-Speech. At 460 h of clean LibriSpeech, the seed moves fairness metrics more than compression does on most demographic axes. A balanced 3x3 decomposition attributes 85.3% of the variation in Fair-Speech ethnicity normalized gap to the seed against 8.3% to compression (p = 0.009), though compression explains more on age and gender. Held-out LibriSpeech word error rate spreads by 0.04 points across those seeds while Common Voice spreads by 8.57, so these are not failed runs, and the effect survives controlling for accuracy and dropout. Scaling and diversifying the adaptation set to 960 h damps the effect but does not remove it. On Fair-Speech ethnicity, two single-run systems must differ by more than 0.30 in normalized gap to exceed seed variability.

</details>


### [104] [When Clipping Reverses Correction: Failure Dynamics of Pointwise Forward-KL On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38995)
*Di Huang,Hao Li,Yixin Chen,Fuhai Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了on-policy self-distillation中前向KL目标逐点裁剪(clipping)的副作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后续研究沿用了OPSD中的逐点裁剪技巧，但其对训练的实际影响并未被直接检验，作者希望探究该裁剪是否真的有益。

Method: 设计仅在是否应用裁剪上不同的匹配训练实验，并给出理论证明：裁剪后的目标可能无法把学生拉向教师，反而会把被裁剪与未被裁剪的token概率都推离教师。

Result: 裁剪组产生的、能持续到回答末尾的重复(repetition)明显多于未裁剪组；训练中裁剪学生在重复内离开重复的概率低于教师、继续重复的概率高于教师，而未裁剪组则贴近教师。理论与实验一致。

Conclusion: 逐点裁剪会诱发并加固重复生成，实务上应谨慎使用该裁剪；未来的OPSD训练应以未裁剪或更合理的稳定化手段替代。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own generated responses using feedback from the same model conditioned on privileged information. On mathematical reasoning, the original OPSD study finds that stylistic tokens can dominate the training signal over math-related tokens, and that pointwise clipping of the forward KL objective stabilizes training. Pointwise clipping caps each vocabulary-wise forward KL term at a fixed threshold before summing over the vocabulary. Follow-up studies have adopted this clipping, but its effect on training has not been directly examined. In matched training runs differing only in whether clipping is applied, we observe that clipped runs produce substantially more repetitions that persist to the end of the response than their unclipped counterparts. We trace this failure to the clipped objective. We prove that the clipped objective can fail to correct the student toward the teacher and can instead push clipped and unclipped token probabilities away from its teacher. Our training runs agree with this analysis: inside repetitions, the clipped student places less probability than its teacher on leaving the repetition, and more on continuing it, whereas the unclipped runs stay close to their teachers.

</details>


### [105] [Settle: Learning When to Stop Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38997)
*Ryan Brown,Zihao Fu,Chris Russell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Settle 通过已完成推理轨迹中的答案稳定性学习何时停止，减少推理模型冗余生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型在答案已确定后仍继续生成，造成 token 浪费，需要一种能在保证准确率的前提下及时停止的方法。

Method: 在已完成的推理轨迹中依据答案稳定性学习停止时机；训练已有的推理结束 token，同时保持其他预测接近基础模型；推理时仅需普通解码。

Result: 在 Qwen3-4B 的 MATH-500 上，Settle 减少 40% token 数，准确率仅下降 0.5 个百分点；相比在首个稳定答案处截断轨迹的监督微调，在几乎相同 token 数下提升 6.16 个百分点；其停止分数可预测正确答案是否保持正确。

Conclusion: Settle 拓展了所评估停止方法的准确率-token 数帕累托前沿，是一种有效的推理停止方法。

Abstract: Reasoning models often continue generating after their answers have settled. Settle learns when to stop from answer stability in completed traces. It trains the existing end-of-reasoning token while keeping other predictions close to the base model, and requires only ordinary decoding at inference. On MATH-500 with Qwen3-4B, Settle reduces token count by 40% with a 0.5-percentage-point decrease in accuracy. It gains 6.16 percentage points over supervised fine-tuning on the same traces shortened at their first stable answer, at nearly identical token counts. Its stopping score predicts whether a correct answer will remain correct. Settle extends the accuracy-token-count Pareto frontier of the evaluated stopping methods.

</details>


### [106] [The Invisible Language Tax: Token Premiums of French and Regional Languages in 2026 LLM Tokenizers, and a French-Optimized Prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39001)
*Thomas Serval*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文测量不同语言在主流LLM分词器下的"token溢价"，并提出一个针对法语优化的字节级BPE原型Baracoda FR v1.2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务按token计费、上下文窗口以token衡量，但相同内容在不同语言中所需token数存在差异，这导致非英语用户承担额外成本，尤其在agentic场景中历史重发、分层定价和固定上下文窗口会放大绝对差距。作者希望量化这一"token溢价"并探索缓解方案。
