Method: 在七个2026年主流模型分词器（OpenAI o200k、Llama 3、Qwen3、DeepSeek V3/V4、Gemma 3、Mistral Tekken、Claude gen-5）上，使用NTREX-128（124种非英语参考译文）及《世界人权宣言》的地区语言版本测量token溢价。另做受控实验（BPE、Europarl、50k词表）考察向训练数据加入法语的影响，并开发字节级BPE原型Baracoda FR v1.2。

Result: 法语比英语多需31%–58%的token；简体中文范围从少5%到多40%，在七个分词器中的六个上比法语更便宜。法国的地区及海外语言约为英语的1.6–3.3倍。受控实验显示加入法语可快速降低溢价，但存在收益递减且英语成本上升。在预先固定协议的六语料测试中，Baracoda FR v1.2比Tekken在法语上少用11.5%的token、英语少用3.7%；去除重叠句和等预算条件下结果依然成立；但在其他语言上更差，同等词表规模下未超过CroissantLLM。

Conclusion: 跨语言token溢价显著且在不同分词器间差异明显，针对特定语言优化分词器可降低成本，但可能牺牲其他语言表现。作者强调本研究仅为分词结果，对模型质量和任务成本的影响仍有待验证。

Abstract: LLM services are billed per token and context windows are measured in tokens, yet the number of tokens needed for the same content varies across languages. We measure this token premium on seven tokenizers of widely used 2026 models (OpenAI o200k, Llama 3, Qwen3, DeepSeek V3/V4, Gemma 3, Mistral Tekken, and the Claude generation-5 tokenizer via Anthropic's counting API) on NTREX-128 (124 non-English reference translations) and on the Universal Declaration of Human Rights for regional languages. French requires 31% to 58% more tokens than English, whereas Simplified Chinese ranges from 5% fewer to 40% more and is cheaper than French on six of the seven tokenizers. Regional and overseas languages of France pay roughly 1.6 to 3.3 times the English count. We discuss how history re-sending, tiered pricing and fixed context windows amplify the absolute gap in agentic use. In a controlled experiment (BPE, Europarl, 50k vocabulary), adding French to tokenizer training data quickly reduces the premium, with diminishing returns and a growing cost for English. Finally, we present Baracoda FR v1.2, a byte-level BPE prototype with Tekken's vocabulary size. On a final test of six corpora never consulted during design, with a protocol declared fixed beforehand, it uses 11.5% fewer tokens than Tekken on French and 3.7% fewer on English; results hold after removing test sentences overlapping the training data and with an equal ordinary-token budget. It is worse on other languages and, at comparable vocabulary size, does not outperform CroissantLLM. These are segmentation results only; effects on model quality and task cost remain to be shown.

</details>


### [107] [Evidence First, Arithmetic Second: A System Report and Failure Analysis for DocSem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39013)
*Divya Godara,Sachin Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EVICALC 系统参加 DocSem 共享任务，在官方最终测试的 1,730 个任务上获得 8.61% 联合准确率；公开验证集上答案准确率 92.17%、证据 F1 为 1.00，但两者配置不同不可直接比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向文档语义（DocSem）共享任务，需要从 PDF 文档中定位证据段落并完成需要算术推理的问答，作者构建 EVICALC 系统以探索"读取 PDF—选段—生成算式—本地求值"这一流程的可行性，并通过保存中间结果和事后分析定位失败原因。

Method: 系统流程为：读取 PDF、选择相关段落、提示语言模型生成算术表达式、在本地代码中求值该表达式；同时保存各阶段中间结果以便检查失败案例。此外还开展了一项探索性研究，对 100 份文档直接读取页面图像。

Result: 官方最终测试在 1,730 个任务上联合准确率为 8.61%；独立运行的公开验证集上答案准确率 92.17%、证据 F1 为 1.00，但两组配置与指标不一致，非受控对比。事后人工分析的一个案例中，OCR 与块分组把相关段落并入其他块，系统据无关文本作答；读取页面图像的探索性研究中，100 份文档仅 22 份返回了证据标识。

Conclusion: 这些描述性发现只能作为进一步评估的动机，并不能确立总体得分高低的因果原因；系统流程可运行且中间结果可追溯，但证据定位环节（如 OCR 与版式分块）是明显的薄弱点。

Abstract: EVICALC, our system for the DocSem shared task, achieved 8.61% joint accuracy on 1,730 tasks in the official final test evaluation. It reads a PDF, selects a passage, asks a language model to write an arithmetic expression, and evaluates that expression in local code. Saved intermediate results support inspection of failures. A separate public-validation run achieved 92.17% answer accuracy and 1.00 evidence F1. The configurations and metrics differ, so these scores are not a controlled comparison. Our manual, post-hoc analysis is descriptive: in one inspected case, optical character recognition (OCR) and block grouping merged the relevant passage into another block, and the system answered from unrelated text. An exploratory study of reading page images on 100 documents returned evidence identifiers for only 22 documents. These descriptive findings motivate further evaluation; they do not establish the causes of the overall score.

</details>


### [108] [A Missing Piece for Trustworthy AI Reviewers: From Benchmarking Rhetorical Robustness to SciCore Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39027)
*Chenguang Wang,Ming Li,Chengrui Fan,Jianpeng Chen,Han Chen,Tianyi Zhou,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"修辞鲁棒性"概念与RobustReview基准，发现AI审稿人存在"虚假鲁棒性"，并设计双分支审稿模型SciCore以兼顾稳定性与区分度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI审稿人可能对同一科学内容的不同措辞给出不同评分，从而奖励修辞优化而非科学改进。因此需要一个能同时衡量"内容保持改写下的稳定性"与"跨论文区分度"的评估目标。

Method: 形式化定义"修辞鲁棒性"（内容保持改写稳定性 + 跨论文区分度）；构建受控全稿件基准RobustReview，含1,260个稿件版本，评测30种审稿人配置；提出SciCore——双分支审稿模型，将全稿件判断与基于结构化"科学核心"提取的判断取平均。

Result: 基准揭示"虚假鲁棒性"现象：低改写敏感性伴随跨论文评分崩塌；人类对齐度与修辞鲁棒性对审稿人的排序不一致；以内容为中心的提示协议未能稳定提升鲁棒性。在GPT-5.5对比中，SciCore取得领先的稳定性-区分度联合表现，并保持有竞争力的人类对齐度。

Conclusion: 修辞鲁棒性是一个独立的评测目标，科学核心式审稿有望提升该性能。

Abstract: AI reviewers can assign different judgments to manuscripts that report the same science in different wording, potentially rewarding rhetorical optimization over scientific improvement. We formulate Rhetorical Robustness as the joint requirement of stability across content-preserving rewrites and discrimination across papers. We introduce RobustReview, a controlled full-manuscript benchmark with 1,260 manuscript versions, and evaluate 30 reviewer configurations. The benchmark reveals false robustness, where low rewrite sensitivity coincides with score collapse across papers, and shows that human alignment and rhetorical robustness rank reviewers differently. Moreover, the evaluated content-focused prompting protocol does not consistently improve robustness across backbones. Motivated by these findings, we introduce SciCore, a dual-branch reviewer that averages a full-manuscript judgment with a judgment based on an extracted, structured science core. This design combines manuscript-level assessment with a content-normalized view intended to reduce rhetorical sensitivity. In our primary GPT-5.5 comparison, SciCore achieves a leading joint stability-discrimination profile among the benchmarked reviewers while maintaining competitive human alignment. These results identify rhetorical robustness as a distinct evaluation target and demonstrate the potential of science-core review to improve it.

</details>


### [109] [Structure vs. Chain-of-Thought: Evaluating LLM Criteria Extraction for Depression Severity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39049)
*Xinkai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文比较了两种用大语言模型评估抑郁严重程度的方法：直接打分与"临床标准抽取+编码计数"，发现标准抽取方法的优势依赖于阈值调参，且序数一致性提高并不代表更能识别重症案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接让LLM对社交媒体帖子给出抑郁严重程度评分虽简便，但可审计性差；先标注帖子符合哪些临床标准、再由代码把计数转为标签的方式更便于临床医生逐条核查。论文旨在系统检验"标准抽取"是否真的优于直接提示与思维链提示，以及其收益来源是否为真正的推理能力或仅仅是阈值校准。

Method: 在两个Reddit语料（其中一个为重新标注的压力数据集）上，使用三个不同规模的LLM（9B至前沿规模）和两份问卷（PHQ-9、BDI-II），比较三种提示策略：直接提示、思维链、临床标准抽取。采用二次加权kappa衡量与标签的一致性，并分别在"阈值在标注数据上拟合"与"阈值依PHQ-9标准先验固定"两种设定下评估，同时对思维链在同一批标签上重新校准以作对照。

Result: 对两个前沿模型而言，标准抽取仅在阈值由标注数据拟合时于一个语料上超过思维链，且增益不显著；即便对思维链做同样校准，差距仍不显著。阈值先验固定时，标准抽取在两个语料上均无增益，即使模型平均每帖标注超过两个标准。9B模型在抑郁社区语料上倾向将多数帖子判为重度，先验规则在无标签条件下优于直接提示和思维链；重新校准思维链后差距消失，说明存在校准效应。此外，更高的序数一致性并不保证更好识别重症：PHQ-9标准抽取漏掉大多数重症帖子，从直接提示到思维链再到标准抽取，漏检率在几乎所有比较中递增。在主要语料上，一个使用该数据集自身特征（含词数）的模型与先验规则下的前沿标准抽取无显著差异。

Conclusion: 标准抽取的可解释性优势并不能自动转化为抑郁严重程度评估的性能优势，其表面增益主要来自阈值校准而非更强的推理；序数一致性指标可能掩盖对重症案例的漏检，因此在临床应用前需以重症识别等更贴近实际需求的指标进行评估，并对阈值设定方式保持审慎。

Abstract: A large language model (LLM) can rate depression severity directly from a social media post or mark which clinical criteria the post shows and let code turn the count into a label. The latter is easier to audit because a clinician can check each marked criterion. We compare these approaches on two Reddit corpora using three LLMs (from 9B to frontier scale) and two questionnaires (PHQ-9, BDI-II), and measure agreement with quadratic weighted kappa. For the two frontier models, criteria extraction scores above chain-of-thought on one corpus only when its decision thresholds are fitted on labeled data. Neither model's gain is significant, with or without recalibrating chain-of-thought on the same labels. With thresholds fixed a priori from PHQ-9's criteria, extraction shows no gain on either corpus, even where models mark over two criteria per post. The 9B model behaves differently on a corpus from depression communities. It labels most posts severe, whether prompted directly or with chain-of-thought, while the a priori rule beats both without labels. After chain-of-thought is recalibrated on the same labels, no significant gap remains, consistent with a calibration effect. Yet higher ordinal agreement does not ensure better detection of severe cases. PHQ-9 criteria extraction misses most severe posts, and moving from direct prompting to chain-of-thought and then to extraction increases misses in nearly all comparisons. On the primary corpus, a relabeled stress dataset, a model using that dataset's own features, including word counts from the text, is not significantly different from frontier criteria extraction under the a priori rule.

</details>


### [110] [CORE: Conflict-Oriented Reasoning Elimination for Verifiable Language-Model Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39069)
*Siyu Song,Rui Xu,Jia Lin,Kai Liu,Weifang Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CORE 是一种利用验证器提供的认证冲突核心来指导回溯的测试时推理搜索控制器，在图上着色和多种推理任务上均优于逐步修复和 Tree of Thoughts。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时推理解题系统在失败时通常直接重启或修改最近一步，但错误往往源于更早的决策，导致搜索低效且重复犯错。因此需要利用"失败解释"来定位真正导致冲突的早期决策。

Method: 提出 CORE 搜索控制器：向验证器请求认证冲突核心（certified conflict core），回溯到该核心中最近的决策（backjumping），并缓存冲突以避免重复。在可靠验证、有限分支与深度、穷尽提案条件下，无上限搜索是完备的，且不会剪掉有效解。

Result: 在 2,000 个匹配提案与精确验证器的图上着色实例上，CORE 相比时间序修复将验证器调用中位数降低 39.8%（30 变量）和 35.0%（36 变量），缓存进一步优于仅回溯。在五个推理任务上，CORE 平均成功率 75.9%（Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct）和 84.2%（Qwen3-8B），高于 Tree of Thoughts 的 72.5% 和 81.8%，且验证器调用与生成 token 更少。

Conclusion: 结果表明，使用经过认证的失败解释来指导语言模型搜索具有显著价值，可提升效率与成功率。

Abstract: Test-time reasoning systems often respond to failure by restarting or revising the latest step, even when an earlier decision caused the error. We introduce CORE, a search controller that requests a certified conflict core from a verifier, backjumps to the latest decision in that core, and caches the conflict to avoid repeating it. Under sound verification, finite branching and depth, and exhaustive proposals, the uncapped search is complete and never prunes a valid solution. On 2,000 planted graph-coloring instances with matched proposals and an exact verifier, CORE reduces median verifier calls by 39.8% at 30 variables and 35.0% at 36 variables relative to chronological repair; caching further improves on backjumping alone. Across five reasoning tasks, CORE achieves 75.9% mean success with Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and 84.2% with Qwen3-8B, compared with 72.5% and 81.8% for Tree of Thoughts. It also uses fewer verifier calls and generated tokens on both backbones. These results show the value of using certified failure explanations to direct language-model search.

</details>


### [111] [LexReward: A Taxonomy-Driven Reward Framework for Legal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39071)
*Yida Cai,Xin Dai,Bingxiang He,Huiyuan Xie,Yuxiao Ye,Zhenghao Liu,Yang Bai,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LexReward，一种基于分类法（taxonomy）驱动的法律奖励建模框架，从Style、Element、Chain三个维度对法律回答质量进行细粒度评估，并用于构建偏好数据训练DPO及奖励模型LexRM，实验证明其能有效区分回答质量并提升下游优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律语言模型需要不仅捕捉答案正确性、还能反映法律回答多维质量的奖励信号。然而现有奖励方法多依赖粗粒度的整体判断，领域特异性与可解释性有限。

Method: 提出LexReward框架，沿三个互补维度刻画法律回答质量：Style（词汇与句法质量）、Element（法律主体、事实、法条与裁判）、Chain（法律推理的顺序、完整性、正确性与非冗余）。为每个维度制定评分标准（rubrics）以明确评价准则与质量等级，并据此构建成对偏好数据，用于DPO和奖励模型训练，得到LexRM。

Result: 实验表明，基于评分标准的奖励能可靠区分不同质量的法律回答；在偏好数据上进行DPO训练可在三个维度上均提升性能。学习到的奖励模型LexRM也支持有效的下游优化：各维度专用奖励模型通过强化学习提升对应维度上的策略性能，且奖励时无需参考答案。维度级分析进一步验证了所提分类法与奖励构建的有效性。

Conclusion: LexReward通过taxonomy驱动的多维奖励建模，为法律领域提供了更具领域特异性和可解释性的奖励信号，可有效提升法律语言模型的回答质量与下游优化效果。

Abstract: Legal language models require reward signals that capture not only answer correctness but also the multidimensional quality of legal responses. Existing reward methods, however, often rely on coarse-grained holistic judgments, providing limited domain specificity and interpretability. We introduce LexReward, a taxonomy-driven framework for legal reward modeling. LexReward characterizes legal response quality along three complementary dimensions: Style, covering lexical and syntactic quality; Element, assessing legal subjects, facts, statutes, and decisions; and Chain, evaluating the order, completeness, correctness, and non-redundancy of legal reasoning. For each dimension, we develop rubrics that specify evaluation criteria and quality levels. The resulting rewards are used to construct pairwise preference data for Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and reward-model training. Experiments show that the rubric-based rewards reliably distinguish legal responses of different quality and that DPO training on the preference data improves performance across all three dimensions. The learned reward models, LexRM, also support effective downstream optimization: each dimension-specific reward model improves policy performance in its corresponding dimension through reinforcement learning, without requiring reference answers at reward time. Dimension-wise analyses further support the effectiveness of the proposed taxonomy and reward construction.

</details>


### [112] [Beyond Text: LLM-Based Dimensional Emotion Evaluation in Multimodal Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39072)
*Yutong Hu,Jinho Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于LLM的多模态对话情感识别框架，在IEMOCAP上同时进行离散情感识别与VAD维度评估，通过语音线索文本化、零样本/少样本提示与LoRA微调对比六个模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话情感识别已被广泛研究，但将LLM用于多模态对话中的连续维度情感评估仍鲜有探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用SpeechCueLLM思路，将声学线索转为自然语言描述；在IEMOCAP上对LLaMA、GPT、Qwen三个家族的六个模型，分别进行零样本提示、少样本提示和LoRA微调。

Result: LoRA微调的LLaMA显著优于提示工程的GPT模型（归因于领域适应而非模型容量）；最佳模型Valence CCC达0.7822，创IEMOCAP新SOTA；消融显示文本化音频描述对较小模型提升明显（加权F1 +3.5~3.6），对最大模型贡献有限；VAD各维度性能差异与IEMOCAP标注者一致性层级高度吻合。

Conclusion: 领域适应比模型规模更关键；音频线索在语言能力受限时价值最大；VAD维度的性能不对称反映标注一致性差异。该文属于MLLM。

Abstract: Emotion recognition in conversation has been widely studied, but applying Large Language Models (LLMs) to continuous dimensional emotion evaluation in multimodal dialogue remains largely unexplored. We propose an LLM-based framework that performs discrete emotion recognition and Valence-Arousal-Dominance (VAD) dimensional evaluation on IEMOCAP, incorporating acoustic cues as natural language descriptions following the SpeechCueLLM approach. We evaluate six models spanning the LLaMA, GPT, and Qwen families under zero-shot prompting, few-shot prompting, and LoRA fine-tuning. LoRA fine-tuned LLaMA models substantially outperform prompt-engineered GPT models on both tasks despite GPT's larger scale, a gap we attribute to domain adaptation rather than model capacity. Our best model achieves a Valence CCC of 0.7822, a new state-of-the-art on IEMOCAP. Ablation studies confirm that textual audio descriptions meaningfully improve smaller models (+3.5 to 3.6 weighted F1) while contributing little for the largest model, suggesting audio cues are most valuable when linguistic capacity is limited. The performance asymmetry across VAD dimensions closely mirrors the annotator agreement hierarchy in IEMOCAP's own annotations.

</details>


### [113] [False Frontiers: Diagnosing and Mitigating Co-Cheating in Self-Evolving Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39102)
*Meijia Chen,Hao Li,Zheng Lu,Hongshan Lin,Junbai Tian,Yichen Liu,Zijun Tian,Yufan Zou,Shuhan Sun,Hanxin Chen,Zeyu Zhang,Weizhi Du,Yueting Li,Tianyu Shi,Alaa Khamis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 自演化搜索智能体存在"共同作弊"（co-cheating）问题：提议者与求解者共享同一错误却内部奖励上升。作者提出 CrossFit，通过交叉拟合（A 组文档生成的问题由仅用 B 组训练的辅助求解者评分，反之亦然）切断反馈血缘，将假一致率从 6.1%/8.8% 降至 3.0%/3.7%，并在七个搜索基准上平均提升 8.8 和 8.4 点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自演化搜索智能体通过联合优化"出题者（proposer）"和"解题者（solver）"来构建训练课程，形成闭环。该闭环引入一种失效模式——co-cheating：两者逐渐在共享错误上达成一致，内部奖励提升但外部正确性并无相应增长。对源证据的事后审计显示，随着自演化轮次增加，co-cheating 愈发严重，伪标签正确率停滞甚至下降。因此需要一种机制在训练前或训练中打破这种共谋。

Method: 1) 多采样验证（MSV）：对同一模型分别带源文档查询三次、不带源文档查询三次，据此决定任务是否准入并替换不可靠伪标签；2) CrossFit（主方法）：将提议者的源文档划分为 A、B 两组，A 生成的问题由仅在 B 上训练的辅助求解者打分，B 生成的问题由仅在 A 上训练的辅助求解者打分，交叉拟合的一致性决定提议者奖励，使同源伪标签无法通过反馈求解者复现，而原求解者的更新规则保持不变。实验在 Qwen3.5-4B 与 Qwen3.5-9B 上重跑该闭环，并做了"用排除源的反馈重放相同提议"的隔离实验。

Result: MSV 将假一致质量从 6.1% 降至 5.7%（4B）、从 8.8% 降至 7.2%（9B），但仍有大量残余 co-cheating，且每个候选需多 6 次标注生成。CrossFit 进一步降至 3.0% 和 3.7%。用排除源的反馈重放相同提议后，假一致率进一步降至 0.4% 和 0.1%，从而将反馈血缘与课程变化的影响分离。在七个下游搜索基准上，CrossFit 相较标准耦合自演化平均提升 8.8 与 8.4 点，相较 Search-R1 提升 8.7 与 7.8 点（4B/9B）。

Conclusion: co-cheating 是自演化搜索智能体闭环中的真实现象，随轮次累积而加重；简单的事前验证（MSV）只能部分缓解且代价高。CrossFit 通过交叉拟合切断提议者与求解者之间的反馈血缘，在几乎不增加成本的前提下显著抑制假一致，并带来稳定的下游搜索性能提升，说明从机制上打破提议—求解共谋比事后过滤更有效。

Abstract: Self-evolving search agents build their own training curricula by jointly optimizing a proposer that generates questions and a solver that answers them. This closed loop introduces a failure mode we call co-cheating: the proposer and solver increasingly agree on shared errors, so internal reward improves without a matching gain in external correctness. A post-hoc audit against source evidence shows co-cheating growing more severe over successive rounds of self-evolution, with pseudo-label correctness stagnating or declining even as the in-loop training signal improves. The most direct mitigation is to verify proposals before training: we introduce multi-sample verification (MSV), which queries the same model three times with the source and three times without it to decide task admission and replace unreliable pseudo-labels. MSV partially reduces false agreement but leaves substantial residual co-cheating and costs six extra labeler generations per candidate. These limitations motivate CrossFit, our main method: it partitions the proposer's source documents into groups A and B; questions generated from A are scored by an auxiliary solver trained only on B, and vice versa. The cross-fitted agreement determines proposer reward, so a same-source pseudo-label cannot be reproduced through the feedback solver, while the original solver's update rule is unchanged. Rerunning the loop with Qwen3.5-4B and Qwen3.5-9B, MSV reduces false-agreement mass from 6.1% to 5.7% and from 8.8% to 7.2%, whereas CrossFit reduces it to 3.0% and 3.7%. Replaying identical proposals with source-excluded feedback further reduces false agreement to 0.4% and 0.1%, isolating feedback ancestry from curriculum changes. Across seven downstream search benchmarks, CrossFit improves average performance over standard coupled self-evolution by 8.8 and 8.4 points and over Search-R1 by 8.7 and 7.8 points at 4B and 9B.

</details>


### [114] [Bongard: Training Machine Intuition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39111)
*Li Ding,Haidi Jin,Chen Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出开源权重的"系统1"直觉模型Bongard，基于T5Gemma 2 4B-4B编码器-解码器，通过三阶段训练与联合嵌入、沙盒后训练，在高吞吐决策任务上实现高效高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类智能高度依赖习得的直觉（模式识别与快速判断，无需显式展开中间步骤），作者希望将"机器直觉"视为一种可独立设计与训练的能力，并提供开源、高效、适合高吞吐决策负载的替代方案。

Method: 采用T5Gemma 2 4B-4B编码器-解码器结构，将"读证据"与"做判断"分离：编码器双向读取状态与问题指令，多个解码分支共享该编码，从而对同一状态的多个判断只需一次读取；由训练好的head在候选上输出概率而不生成文本。训练分三阶段（监督判断→语义关系→动作结果），每阶段在单张Blackwell GPU上更新全部70.9亿可训练参数；随后进行联合嵌入后训练与沙盒阶段（从动作rollout和精确oracle中学习结果分布）。

Result: 联合嵌入后训练将留出改写集准确率从75.7%提升至85.9%；沙盒阶段将冻结沙盒面板准确率从50.6%提升至64.8%。在DecisionBench上，最终模型在23,900个决策上达78.05%准确率，在61个系统的公开比较中排名第四。单张RTX PRO 6000上，短请求中位延迟36 ms，关于同一状态的32个问题耗时221 ms。

Conclusion: Bongard表明机器直觉可以通过表示学习和结果反馈被系统化训练，为高吞吐决策工作负载提供了一种开源且高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Human intelligence relies heavily on learned intuition: recognising patterns and judging situations without explicitly unfolding every intermediate step. We introduce Bongard, an open-weight System One model that treats machine intuition as an independent capability to design and train. A T5Gemma 2 4B-4B encoder-decoder separates reading the evidence from making judgments. The encoder reads the state bidirectionally together with the question instructions, and separate decoder branches share this encoding, so many judgments about the same situation require only one reading of the state. A trained head returns probabilities over the supplied candidates without generating text. Training proceeds in three stages, from supervised judgments to semantic relationships to action outcomes, and each stage updates all 7.09 billion trainable parameters on one Blackwell GPU. Joint-embedding post-training raises accuracy on held-out rephrasings from 75.7% to 85.9%. A sandbox stage then learns outcome distributions from action rollouts and exact oracles, raising accuracy on a frozen sandbox panel from 50.6% to 64.8%. On DecisionBench, the final model reaches 78.05% accuracy over 23,900 decisions and ranks fourth of 61 systems in the public comparison. On one RTX PRO 6000, its median latency is 36 ms for short requests, and 32 questions about one state take 221 ms. Bongard demonstrates that machine intuition can be systematically trained via representation learning and outcome feedback, providing an open, efficient alternative for high-throughput decision workloads.

</details>


### [115] [Diagnosing On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39118)
*Yang Li,Gongle Xue,Yuheng Yuan,Yijia Guo,Shizhe Zhang,Liwen Hu,Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统诊断了语言模型数学推理中的on-policy self-distillation (OPSD)，发现OPSD仅在狭窄的兼容区间内有效，整体上是敏感而非通用可靠的后训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD作为无需外部奖励或独立强教师的推理增强方法受到关注，但其在语言推理中的行为尚不明确，已有报告结果从轻微提升到行为崩溃差异极大，因此需要系统诊断。

Method: 在0.6B–8B参数范围的多个模型上对数学推理任务进行OPSD诊断，采用受控实验与token级分析，考察教师信号来源及有效条件。

Result: 教师信号由推理模式对齐与完整教师前缀决定，而非仅由特权语义决定；OPSD仅在狭窄兼容区间提升推理，否则导致无效长度增长、稳定退化或行为崩溃；token级分析显示教师信号不稳定且无法预测下游性能。

Conclusion: OPSD是一种敏感算法，而非普遍可靠的推理改进后训练方法。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has attracted growing interest as a promising approach to improve the reasoning ability of language models. Without external rewards nor a separate stronger teacher, the self-teacher with privileged information could provide dense signals on student's trajectories. However, its behavior in language reasoning remains unclear, with reported outcomes ranging from modest gains to behavioral collapse. In this work, we diagnose OPSD for mathematical reasoning across models spanning 0.6B--8B parameters. We conduct controlled experiments and token-level analyses to fully delve into OPSD. We point out that teacher's signal is shaped by reasoning-mode alignment and the complete teacher prefix, rather than by privileged semantics alone. OPSD improves reasoning only in narrow compatibility regimes. Otherwise, it produces ineffective length growth, stable degradation, or behavioral collapse. Token-level analysis shows that teacher's signal is not stable and does not predict downstream performance. Based on these results, we argue that OPSD is a sensitive algorithm rather than a generally reliable reasoning-improvement post-training method.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Absorbing State Phase Transitions in Multi-Agent Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38327)
*Wenwen Zheng,Yuzhe Yang,Helen Qu,Xin Eric Wang,Haewon Jeong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文用吸收态相变理论预测基于LLM的多智能体搜索任务的成功率，推导出临界通信度d_c，并在真实任务中验证理论的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统会出现非平凡动力学，已有初步证据表明统计力学形式化方法可有效建模和预测这些行为；同时，为最优任务求解设计多智能体通信拓扑是活跃的研究问题。

Method: 将搜索任务按组合搜索经典结果分类为四种类型；理论推导临界通信度d_c，即每个智能体最少可通信的智能体数量阈值；在真实搜索与发现任务（软件配置调试、物理机制发现）上评估前沿LLM多智能体系统。

Result: 与理论的一致性结果不一：在d_c之上错误假设不再失控增殖、搜索进入已解决状态的理论预测仅部分得到验证；LLM智能体可能不与邻居通信，并会发展出对个体有利但限制协作收益的策略。

Conclusion: 吸收态相变形式化方法为预测LLM多智能体搜索成功提供了理论框架，但LLM智能体的实际通信行为与个体理性策略会使理论与现实出现偏差，需进一步考虑智能体行为因素。

Abstract: Nontrivial dynamics can emerge in large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems, and preliminary evidence exists that formalisms from statistical mechanics can be effective at modeling and predicting such behaviors. In parallel, designing multi-agent communication topology for optimal task-solving is an active research question. In this paper, we focus on predicting the success of multi-agent search tasks using the formalism of absorbing state phase transitions. We first taxonomize search tasks into four types, informed by classical results in combinatorial search. We then theoretically derive a critical communication degree $d_c$, the minimum number of agents each agent can communicate with, above which incorrect hypotheses do not proliferate uncontrollably and the search enters the solved state. Finally, we evaluate frontier LLM-based multi-agent systems on real-world search and discovery tasks, software configuration debugging and physical mechanism discovery, and find that agreement with theory is mixed. LLM agents may not communicate with their neighbors and can develop strategies that are individually beneficial but limits the benefits of collaboration.

</details>


### [117] [PANDA: A Decentralized Architecture with Flexible Orchestration for Scalable, Fault-Tolerant Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38482)
*Matthew D. Laws,Cristina Nita-Rotaru*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: PANDA是一种去中心化的LLM多智能体系统架构，支持大规模异构智能体自组织成专业小队，提升可扩展性、容错性与治理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体架构难以可靠高效地解决大规模多步任务：无法支撑大量智能体与并发任务，容错性差，难以治理智能体交互，且无法适应不同任务所需的多样规划与执行模式。

Method: PANDA采用去中心化架构，连接大量异构且独立管理的智能体，使其相互发现能力并按任务自组织成小型专业团队。它将集体通信与团队通信解耦以提升扩展性，支持智能体同时参与多个团队、负载均衡与并发调度；将底层架构与编排策略分离，支持星型、链型、网状三种模式；通过动态重规划应对故障；并采用信任网络模型在不依赖中心化服务的前提下约束交互。

Result: 在HotPotQA基准上，PANDA可扩展至数千智能体，毫秒级组队，在最高8倍效率下达到与最先进方法相当的准确率，并在现有系统失败的情况下实现100%任务完成率。

Conclusion: PANDA通过去中心化、通信解耦、编排策略分离与信任网络治理，有效解决LLM多智能体系统在规模、效率、容错与治理方面的瓶颈。

Abstract: Existing architectures for LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) cannot reliably and efficiently solve multi-step tasks at scale: they struggle to support large numbers of agents and concurrent tasks, tolerate failures, govern agent interactions, and accommodate the diverse planning and execution patterns different tasks require. We present PANDA, a decentralized architecture that connects a large collective of heterogeneous, independently administered agents, letting them discover each other's capabilities and self-organize into small specialized teams per task. PANDA scales by decoupling collective communication from team communication, allowing agents to participate in multiple teams simultaneously, load-balancing tasks across the collective, and scheduling concurrent work within each agent. PANDA further separates the underlying architecture from the orchestration strategy, supporting three planning and execution patterns (star, chain, and mesh) that can be selected according to the structure and requirements of each task. PANDA detects infrastructure and orchestration failures and recovers affected tasks by dynamically replanning around failed components. Finally, to provide governance without a centralized service that would limit scalability, PANDA uses a web-of-trust model to constrain agent interactions to established trust relationships. We evaluate PANDA on the HotPotQA benchmark, demonstrating that it scales to thousands of agents, assembles teams in milliseconds, matches state-of-the-art accuracy at up to 8x the efficiency, and sustains 100% task completion under faults where existing systems fail.

</details>


### [118] [From Solo to Social Learning: Characterizing Recursive Social Improvement in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38516)
*Kunal Jha,Max Kleiman-Weiner,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究提出"递归社会性改进"概念，考察多个自改进LLM智能体在各自追求自身奖励时能否相互学习、提升整个群体；结果显示复制同伴可使学习更高效，但尚不能更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM自改进方法通常一次只优化一个系统，多智能体框架又常让所有模型追求同一共享目标。作者想探究一个不同的问题：当每个智能体追求自身奖励时，自改进的LLM能否彼此学习，进而提升整个群体的表现。

Method: 提出"递归社会性改进"能力设定，研究智能体种群修改技能文件，并决定是否、何时以及从谁那里复制技能。独立搜索、向同伴学习和行动共享同一token预算。先在受控环境中用既有社会学习算法验证，再让模型自行编写和修订技能，观察同伴对改进方式的影响。

Result: 在受控环境中，既有社会学习算法能从同伴获益，但三个LLM都不能：它们每token获得的奖励低于独立学习者，要么探索过窄，要么在行动前耗尽token。让模型自行编写技能后，观察同伴改变了其改进方式，帮助一个模型更快找到有用技能、另一个减少私有搜索开销，但两者在同成本下都未超过独立学习者。技能被复制、修订和传递，一次发现可催生更多搜索，但这些交换使种群集中在更少的独立发现上。

Conclusion: LLM可以通过复制同伴使学习更高效，但尚不能因此更有效；社会性学习在自改进LLM群体中的收益仍有限且可能降低多样性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can now improve themselves by revising the instructions they follow, and LLM agents are increasingly orchestrated to work together on complex problems. However, self-improvement methods typically optimize one system at a time, and multi-agent frameworks often have every model work toward a shared goal. We ask a different question. When each agent pursues its own reward, can self-improving LLMs learn from one another well enough to improve the whole population? We call this capability recursive social improvement. We study populations that revise skill files and choose whether, when, and whom to copy from. Independent search, learning from peers, and acting all share one token budget. In controlled environments, established social-learning algorithms benefit from peers, but three LLMs do not. They earn less reward per token than solo learners, and explore too narrowly or run out of tokens before acting. We then let the models write and revise their own skills. Observing peers changes how they improve, helping one model find useful skills sooner and another spend less on private search. Neither, however, outperforms independent learners at the same cost. Skills are copied, revised, and passed on, so one discovery can seed further search. Yet these exchanges concentrate the population around fewer independent discoveries. Together, these results show that LLMs can make learning more efficient by copying from peers, but not yet more effective.

</details>


### [119] [Recursive Organization Improvement: A Modeling Specification for Human--Agent Organizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38643)
*Zilong Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文提出递归组织改进的建模规范，并通过可执行检查器、公开记录映射与受控仿真进行验证，考察证据获取、复用与及时更新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 更强的AI智能体不会自动带来更好的组织，团队需要学习保留哪些工作安排以及何时重新考虑它们，因此需要一套可执行、可检验的组织改进建模规范。

Method: 提出连接行为者可见历史、组织记忆、决策权与携带证据的变更契约的建模规范；通过可执行检查器、公开记录映射和受控仿真评估；机制研究在固定资源上限下交叉六种决策规则、三种记忆条件与三种任务环境。

Result: 稳态环境中，累积证据将平衡评估的归一化净价值每任务从0.45224提升至0.48007；重复重评相对该比较基准的劣势从重置证据下的0.01702降至累积证据下的0.00007。最佳工作流反转时出现相反代价：无限期保留延迟适应，有限窗口以过渡成本恢复最终性能。探索性控制中，匹配试验获取与标签复用将表观重评收益从0.00607降至0.00191；程序替换在测试的反转时间下无稳定收益。

Conclusion: 在评估组织改进时，必须将证据获取、复用与及时更新等机制同评估器替换区分开来。

Abstract: Stronger AI agents do not automatically produce better organizations: teams must also learn which work arrangements to retain and when to reconsider them. We propose a modeling specification for recursive organization improvement and evaluate it through an executable checker, a public-record mapping, and controlled simulation. The specification connects actor-visible histories, organizational memory, decision rights, and evidence-carrying change contracts. The mechanism study crosses six decision rules, three memory conditions, and three task environments under fixed resource ceilings. In a stationary environment, cumulative evidence raises balanced evaluation's normalized net value per task from 0.45224 to 0.48007. Repeated reassessment's disadvantage relative to this comparator falls from 0.01702 with reset evidence to 0.00007 with cumulative evidence. A reversal of the best workflow reveals the opposite cost: indefinite retention delays adaptation, while a finite window restores eventual performance at a transition cost. In exploratory controls, matching trial acquisition and label reuse reduces the apparent reassessment gain from 0.00607 to 0.00191. Program replacement adds no stable benefit across the tested reversal times. The study identifies evidence acquisition, reuse, and timely updating as mechanisms that must be separated from evaluator replacement when assessing organizational improvement.

</details>


### [120] [CollabFlow: Recursive Self-Improvement of Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38662)
*Xiao Huang,Mingda Zhang,Junming Zhang,Qiang Huang,Hanwen Zhang,Yue Dai,Zijia Wang,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出 CollabFlow，一种基于递归自我改进（RSI）的 LLM 多智能体协作系统：可训练的 Collab-Director 组建智能体团队，冻结执行器运行，利用每轮结果反向训练导演，并结合证据条件通信协议与协作轨迹平衡（CTB）目标，实现持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 多智能体协作存在三大问题：协作在操作者层面预先定义、循环未被闭合；仅学习拓扑结构会保留逐字交换从而传播错误；基于系统自身结果做奖励最大化会导致只集中在少数团队，忽视多样性。

Method: 1）构建可训练的 Collab-Director 来组建完整智能体团队，由冻结的执行器运行，每轮结果用于重新训练导演；2）在协作图边上引入证据条件通信（Evidence-Conditioned Communication）协议，接收方仅在发送方证据以一定边际更强时才采纳不同答案，从而学习“谁与谁通信、如何通信”；3）跨轮提出协作轨迹平衡（CTB）这一基于流的目标，使每个团队仅按其构建顺序被记一次贡献，并瞄准与奖励成比例的团队分布，保持多个优秀团队同时在场；4）对自生成目标在轮次间的漂移范围给出界，该界随记录累积而收缩。

Result: 在十二个数据集上，CollabFlow 优于所有基线，并且在多轮迭代中持续提升。

Conclusion: CollabFlow 通过闭合“协作—结果—再训练”的递归自我改进闭环，将协作协议学习与团队分布目标结合，有效缓解错误传播与团队集中化问题，并展现出跨轮次持续改进的能力。代码已开源。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) lets a system improve from its own outcomes; in LLM-based multi-agent systems, Agents refine one another within a task, and outcomes improve how they collaborate across tasks. However, existing multi-agent collaboration leaves this loop open: collaboration is pre-defined at the operator level, topology-only learning keeps verbatim exchange that propagates errors, and reward maximization on a system's own outcomes concentrates on a few teams. To address these challenges, we propose CollabFlow, an RSI system of Learned Agent Collaboration: a trainable Collab-Director constructs teams of complete Agents, a frozen executor runs them, and each round's outcomes retrain the director. Within each round, the edges of a collaboration graph carry protocols of Evidence-Conditioned Communication: a receiver adopts a differing answer only when the sender's evidence is stronger by a margin, so the director learns who communicates and how. Across rounds, we further propose Collaborative Trajectory Balance (CTB), a flow-based objective that credits each team once across its construction orders and targets a reward-proportional distribution over teams, so several good teams stay in play. We also bound how far this self-generated target moves between rounds, which shrinks as records accumulate. On twelve datasets, CollabFlow outperforms all baselines and keeps improving across rounds. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CollabFlow-631E.

</details>


### [121] [Where Do Multi-Agent Systems Fail? Evidence-Grounded Diagnosis of Collective Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38761)
*Zhengye Han*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文提出"诊断契约"框架，用于判定多智能体系统中某集体机制是否被违反，强调正确结果不能替代对机制运行记录的检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统即使正确回答，也可能违反了共享信息的路由、准入、存储与执行机制；而错误答案又难以揭示具体哪个机制失效。因此需要明确：什么样的执行证据足以断定某机制被违反。

Method: 提出诊断契约，将"何为违反"与"哪些执行记录能证明违反"分离，结论分为支持、排除或未知（删除记录只能使结论变为未知，不能反转）。对四种机制设计契约，并从机制起作用的步骤重放执行：一次不变、一次破坏机制、一次恢复机制。同时用LLM诊断器比较内部记录与公开输出，并测试通用提示与说明契约的提示。

Result: 机制被破坏时答案常常仍然正确；LLM诊断器从内部记录中检出的违反远多于从公开输出中检出的；在相同记录下，通用提示常宣称记录并不支持的确定性，而说明契约的提示基本避免了这一点。契约在简化形式下也适用于独立开发系统的机制，但在独立工作流上，此前近乎完美的诊断行为出现退化。

Conclusion: 正确结果不能替代对集体机制运行方式的记录；在单一基准上的良好一致性并不能证明诊断器可迁移到其他系统。

Abstract: When a multi-agent system answers correctly, it is tempting to conclude that its agents shared, checked, and used information as intended. Yet a system can break one of its collective mechanisms, the rules that govern how agents route, admit, store, and act on shared information, and still return the right answer, while a wrong answer rarely reveals which mechanism failed. We ask what evidence from an execution is sufficient to conclude that a particular mechanism was violated. Our answer is a diagnostic contract, which separates what counts as a violation from which execution records can establish one, and concludes that a violation is supported, ruled out, or unknown; removing records can make this conclusion unknown but never reverse it. We test contracts for four mechanisms by replaying executions from the step where a mechanism acts, once unchanged, once with the mechanism broken, and once with it restored. Broken mechanisms often left the answer correct. An LLM diagnoser detected many more violations from internal records than from public outputs, yet with identical records a generic prompt often claimed certainty the records did not support, which prompts stating the contracts largely avoided. The contracts also applied, in narrow form, to mechanisms in independently developed systems, but a diagnostic behavior that was nearly perfect on our benchmark degraded on an independently developed workflow. A correct outcome is therefore no substitute for records of how collective mechanisms operated, and agreement on one benchmark does not show that a diagnoser transfers to another system.

</details>


### [122] [Fast and Scalable Multi-Agent Distribution Matching via Partitioned Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38960)
*Kooktae Lee,Ruchika Singh*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出基于分块的近似最优传输框架，实现多智能体系统可扩展的末端分布匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局离散最优传输用于多智能体末端分布匹配时计算代价高昂，难以扩展到大规模系统。

Method: 将智能体与目标样本划分为空间对应的块，求解小型局部传输问题；在质量平衡条件下构造受限耦合，并交替进行局部指派与有限时域控制（适用于线性与非线性动力学）。

Result: 受限耦合保持全局可行性并给出 Wasserstein 代价的上界；交替方案具备逐周期下降保证；仿真验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持与 Wasserstein 目标严格联系的同时，实现了可扩展的末端分布匹配。

Abstract: This paper presents a scalable optimal-transport-based framework for terminal distribution matching in multi-agent systems. While optimal transport provides a natural way to measure distributional mismatch and assign agents to a desired spatial distribution, global discrete transport can become computationally expensive for large-scale systems. We address this bottleneck by partitioning agents and target samples into spatially corresponding blocks and solving smaller local transport problems. Under a mass-balance condition, the resulting restricted coupling remains feasible for the global problem and provides an upper bound on the Wasserstein cost. The local assignments generate target locations for finite-horizon agent control, applicable to both linear and nonlinear dynamics. By alternating local assignment and control, we establish a cycle-to-cycle descent guarantee for the resulting transport surrogate. The proposed framework therefore enables scalable terminal distribution matching while retaining a rigorous connection to the Wasserstein objective. The technical soundness of the proposed results is validated through simulations.

</details>


### [123] [Consensus and Factual Dynamics in Large Populations of Interacting Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39211)
*Emanuele Ricco,Elia Onofri,Vincenzo Sammartino,Roberto Di Pietro*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文提出RHEON框架，将单个冻结LLM采样出的智能体群体建模为可演化的O(n)自旋系统，研究交互结构、拓扑与采样温度如何影响LLM群体的共识与事实正确性，并构建了470万条回复的演化语料Eraclitus-4.7M。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM共识研究通常固定单一交互结构，无法回答"共识如何依赖于智能体之间的交互方式"这一问题；同时普遍把"一致同意"当作"正确性"的代理指标，其可靠性未被系统检验。

Method: 提出RHEON，一个受统计物理启发的框架：把从单一冻结模型采样出的智能体群体视为阶梯式交互几何（有效维度递增，从1D环到全耦合平均场图）上的O(n)自旋系统，以采样温度T作为热无序来源，用类Glauber异步动力学演化；在提示词、群体规模、通信拓扑、采样温度共432种配置上扫描，生成带标签的演化语料Eraclitus-4.7M。

Result: 智能体在最初几次更新扫描内即达到最强共识增益；每个智能体的邻居数增加平均上加速收敛。某配置最终落在事实正确还是幻觉共识，无法仅由初始状态预测；使幻觉最小化的温度取决于智能体间的耦合方式，因此常见的近贪心默认设置并不自动最安全。语义一致性与事实收敛正相关，交互会强化这一关联，但一致性始终不足以证明正确性。

Conclusion: LLM群体的共识是依赖交互几何与温度的集体行为，不可简化地等同于正确性；设计多智能体系统时需把拓扑与采样温度作为关键变量联合调优，而非默认采用近贪心解码和固定交互结构。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed as populations of interacting entities, in which consensus --agreement on a shared answer-- emerges as a collective, unengineered behaviour. Prior work on LLM consensus shows that agents can cross-verify their answers and converge towards more factual responses, treating agreement as a proxy for correctness. However, these studies usually fix a single interaction structure, leaving open how consensus depends on how agents interact. We address this gap by introducing RHEON, a physics-inspired framework that recasts a population drawn from a single frozen model as an evolving $O(n)$ spin system on a ladder of interaction geometries of increasing effective dimension --from a 1D ring to a full-coupling mean-field graph-- with the sampling temperature $T$ as the tunable source of thermal disorder, evolved through a Glauber-like asynchronous dynamics. Sweeping RHEON across $432$ configurations of prompt, population size, communication topology, and sampling temperature yields Eraclitus-4.7M, a tagged evolutionary corpus of $4.7$ million responses. We find that agents reach their strongest consensus gain within the first few update sweeps and that increasing the number of neighbours per agent accelerates convergence on average. We further show that whether a configuration settles on factually correct or hallucinated consensus is not predictable from its initial state alone, and that the hallucination-minimising temperature depends on how the agents are coupled, so the common near-greedy default is not automatically the safest. Finally, semantic agreement correlates positively with factual convergence, and interaction strengthens the association, yet never enough for unanimity to certify correctness.

</details>


### [124] [Solving Multi-Agent Sokoban via LaCAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39889)
*Keisuke Okumura*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Sokoban-LaCAM，利用多智能体路径规划（MAPF）技术实现可扩展的多智能体推箱子规划器，可高效求解涉及数十个智能体和箱子的实例，并保持完备性与最终最优性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推箱子是多智能体规划领域长期存在的经典基准问题，与仓储物流等实际应用相关。但多智能体版本因分支因子随智能体数量急剧增长、需同时处理任务分配与无碰撞路径规划等难点而发展不足，因此作者希望借助MAPF的最新进展来构建可扩展的求解方案。

Method: 作者基于多智能体路径规划（MAPF）的最新进展设计求解器Sokoban-LaCAM，将MAPF作为核心原语，整合任务分配与无碰撞路径规划，以应对多智能体推箱子中分支因子爆炸的问题。

Result: Sokoban-LaCAM能够高效求解涉及数十个智能体和箱子的实例，同时保持完备性（completeness）和最终最优性（eventual optimality）保证。

Conclusion: 研究表明MAPF可作为解决更广泛集体自动化问题的强大基础原语，为多智能体推箱子及相关仓储物流场景提供了可扩展的规划方案。

Abstract: Sokoban, a puzzle game in which an agent pushes boxes onto unlabelled target locations in a grid world, is a long-standing benchmark planning problem. While it is easy to see the connection to practical applications such as warehouse logistics with autonomous forklifts, its multi-agent counterpart has remained underdeveloped. This is because Multi-Agent Sokoban is substantially more difficult due to factors specific to multi-agent planning, such as the rapidly growing branching factor as the number of agents grows and the need to handle integrated task assignment and collision-free pathfinding. In this paper, we show that a scalable planner for Multi-Agent Sokoban can be designed by leveraging recent advances in multi-agent pathfinding (MAPF). Specifically, our Sokoban-LaCAM efficiently solves instances involving tens of agents and boxes while preserving both completeness and eventual optimality guarantees. This provides evidence that MAPF can serve as a powerful primitive for solving broader collective automation problems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Improving OCR Faithfulness via Gated and Attenuated On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38282)
*Baode Wang,Zuming Huang,Kexuan Ren,Jun Huang,Wei Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出GAD-RL，根据学生模型当前任务表现动态调节教师蒸馏强度，用于视觉语言模型的联合后训练，在多模态OCR基准上取得更优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型可能将图像中的异常文字改写为语义通顺的表达，损害OCR转录的忠实性；同时序列级任务奖励与局部教师指导虽有互补性，但固定教师的监督会随学生能力提升而逐渐失效。

Method: GAD-RL在联合后训练中自适应调节教师监督：冻结教师以参考转录和学生生成前缀为条件；当某响应组存在任务奖励≥0.95的输出时关闭蒸馏，并随组平均奖励升高持续衰减蒸馏强度；同时以前向KL按学生给出教师Top-1 token的概率加权，在学生支持度低时抑制局部辅助更新。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-2B上，GAD-RL在CHAOS-Bench取得59.92% Micro Recall，分别超越GRPO与GRPO+OPD（固定权重）8.45和4.43个百分点；在OmniDocBench v1.6上Overall得分91.18。

Conclusion: 自适应调节教师监督强度可缓解固定教师指导随学生提升而失效的问题，提升视觉语言模型OCR转录的忠实性与整体后训练效果。

Abstract: Vision-language models may rewrite anomalous text in images into linguistically plausible expressions, compromising OCR transcription faithfulness. Sequence-level task rewards and local teacher guidance are complementary, but guidance from the same teacher may not remain equally effective as the student improves. Offline analysis shows that supervision from a fixed teacher becomes progressively less favorable as the student improves, both across training checkpoints and across response groups with different task rewards. Motivated by this observation, we introduce GAD-RL, which adaptively regulates teacher supervision during joint post-training according to the student's current task performance and local distributions. A frozen teacher conditions on reference transcriptions and student-generated prefixes. GAD-RL disables distillation for response groups containing an output with task reward at least 0.95 and continuously attenuates distillation strength as group-mean reward increases. It also weights forward KL by the student's probability of the teacher's Top-1 token, moderating local auxiliary updates when student support for that candidate is low. On Qwen3.5-2B, GAD-RL achieves 59.92% Micro Recall on CHAOS-Bench, surpassing GRPO and GRPO+OPD (fixed-weight) by 8.45 and 4.43 percentage points, respectively, while achieving an Overall score of 91.18 on OmniDocBench v1.6.

</details>


### [126] [AREX-2: Advancing Self-Improving Agents through Long-Horizon Reflective Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38288)
*Hongjin Qian,Chaofan Li,Kun Luo,Wenqing Wei,Jianlyu Chen,Shuqi Lu,Yuyang Hu,Hongwang Xiao,Hui Wang,Chaozhuo Li,Qiwei Ye,Zhicheng Dou,Defu Lian,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AREX-2，通过在机器学习与算法编程任务上合成长期迭代的自我改进轨迹，训练基于 Qwen3.8-27B 的 LLM 智能体，使其在推理、编程与深度研究等多项基准上取得领先成绩，并随迭代轮数增加而持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望提升 LLM 智能体的"自我改进"能力，即在测试阶段迭代优化解的能力。该能力依赖两项互补技能：反思（生成优于当前解的新解）与长程执行（在多轮迭代中保持有效性）。作者假设这两项能力是领域无关的，因此可在易于监督的场景中习得。

Method: 从机器学习与算法编程任务中合成长期改进轨迹，因为这两类任务提供可验证反馈并奖励持续迭代；基于 Qwen3.8-27B 构建智能体并在该数据上训练。

Result: MLE-bench Lite 达 81.8，Frontier-CS 达 70.7；迁移至深度研究任务：BrowseComp 84.0、HLE 52.6、GAIA 92.2、DeepSearchQA 93.8；且随迭代轮数预算增加性能持续提升。

Conclusion: 长程反思性数据是通往自我改进智能体的有效路径。

Abstract: We present AREX-2, an effort to advance the self-improving capability of LLM agents, which we define as the ability to iteratively refine a solution at test time. This ability rests on two complementary capabilities: reflection, which produces a solution better than the current one, and long-horizon execution, which keeps the iteration effective over many rounds. We hypothesize that both capabilities are domain-agnostic, and can therefore be learned in scenarios that are well suited for supervision. Accordingly, we synthesize long-horizon improvement trajectories from machine learning and algorithmic programming tasks, two domains that offer verifiable feedback and reward sustained iteration. Trained on this data, our agent, built on Qwen3.8-27B, achieves strong results on MLE-bench Lite (81.8) and Frontier-CS (70.7), transfers to deep research with 84.0 on BrowseComp, 52.6 on HLE, 92.2 on GAIA, and 93.8 on DeepSearchQA, and keeps improving as its budget of rounds grows. These results show that long-horizon reflective data is an effective route toward self-improving agents.

</details>


### [127] [MoFlow: Multi-Objective Agentic Workflow Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38294)
*Yining Lu,Aurelie Lozano,Xi Yang,Naoki Abe,Yu Deng,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MoFlow 将智能体工作流生成建模为多目标马尔可夫决策过程，用凸包蒙特卡洛树搜索与乐观集合值回溯一次搜索近似覆盖帕累托前沿，从而无需重训即可按任意偏好返回工作流，并在六个基准上取得最高平均超体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作流生成方法通常只优化准确率或目标的加权和，导致每个训练好的生成器只固定一种权衡方案，当偏好变化时必须从头重新训练。

Method: MoFlow 将工作流生成形式化为多目标马尔可夫决策过程，并采用带乐观集合值回溯的凸包蒙特卡洛树搜索求解；每个节点存储一组可达权衡而非单一加权分数，一次搜索即可近似覆盖帕累托前沿，之后可通过查表针对任意偏好返回工作流。

Result: 在涵盖数学、代码和问答的六个基准上，与六个强基线对比。尽管评测设置偏向基线（每个测试偏好都重跑基线，而 MoFlow 未见这些偏好），MoFlow 仍取得最高的平均超体积。

Conclusion: MoFlow 能以单次搜索覆盖多目标权衡空间，实现无需重训的偏好自适应工作流生成，在多项任务上优于需要针对每个偏好重训的单标量优化基线。

Abstract: We study the generation of agentic workflows that jointly optimize multiple objectives, such as accuracy, cost, latency, robustness, and consistency. Existing methods for workflow generation typically optimize accuracy alone or a weighted sum of objectives, so each trained generator commits to one fixed trade-off and must be retrained from scratch when preferences change. To alleviate this, we propose MoFlow, which generates workflows optimized across varied preferences. Specifically, MoFlow formulates workflow generation as a multi-objective Markov decision process and solves it by leveraging Convex-Hull Monte Carlo Tree Search with optimistic set-valued backups, where every node stores a set of reachable trade-offs rather than one weighted score. A single search thus approximately covers the Pareto front, from which MoFlow can return a workflow for any preference by lookup without retraining. We evaluate MoFlow against six strong baselines on six benchmarks spanning mathematics, code, and question answering. Since the baselines are single-scalar optimizers by design, an apples-to-apples comparison is difficult. We instead adopt an evaluation setup that favors the baselines, in that they are rerun for each testing preference, which MoFlow never sees. Even under this stringent setup, MoFlow achieves the highest average hypervolume.

</details>


### [128] [AI Agents are Vulnerable to Radicalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38296)
*Ozgur Can Seckin,Shalmoli Ghosh,Alessandro Flammini,Kristina Lerman,Maria Elizabeth Grabe,Filippo Menczer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM智能体之间能否相互影响并走向激进化，发现"共鸣"（强化既有信念）比"说服"更易使目标LLM激进化，且效应可扩散至相关信念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能够影响人类信念，但其是否能以及如何相互操纵尚不清楚，尤其在个性化AI智能体与多智能体生态系统中存在潜在风险。

Method: 模拟两个LLM智能体的对话：一个作为目标智能体，根据人口统计与心理属性扮演人类人格；另一个作为影响者智能体，意图使目标信念更极端。考察两条激进路径：共鸣（强化目标已有信念）与说服（推广目标原本认为不重要的信念），并从情感与行为指标进行评估，同时比较不同影响策略（如谄媚、未经证实的说法）的效果。

Result: 两种机制都能使目标激进化，但共鸣的效应始终强于说服；不同影响策略产生的激进化程度不同，但在各指标间并不一致；共鸣还会扩散到相关信念，表明AI智能体内部存在相互关联的信念结构。

Conclusion: AI智能体易受激进化影响，尤其是当信息与其既有信念一致时，这为个性化AI智能体及多智能体AI生态系统的脆弱性敲响警钟。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can influence people's beliefs, yet little is known about whether and how they can manipulate each other. To investigate this, we simulate conversations between two agents: a target LLM that role-plays a human persona based on demographic and psychological attributes, and an influencer LLM that aims to make the target's beliefs more extreme. We examine radicalization along two pathways: resonance, where the influencer reinforces a target's pre-existing belief, and persuasion, where the influencer promotes a belief the target initially considers unimportant. Across affective and behavioral metrics, we find that both mechanisms radicalize the target. However, resonance produces consistently stronger effects than persuasion. Different influence tactics, such as using sycophancy and unverified claims, produce different levels of radicalization, but not consistently across metrics. We further show that resonance propagates to related beliefs, suggesting interconnected belief structures within AI agents. These findings indicate that AI agents are susceptible to radicalization, particularly when messages align with their existing beliefs, raising concerns about the vulnerability of personalized AI agents and multi-agent AI ecosystems.

</details>


### [129] [CARAT: Do Materials LLMs Reason or Recite?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38340)
*Jiajun Wu,Jian Yang,Zixiang Ni,Zhenzhu Li,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CARAT框架，用八种匹配视图区分材料大模型是基于晶体结构推理还是复制输入中的答案，并揭示基准评测中的捷径与陈述性表象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料领域大模型回答晶体结构问题时，准确率无法判别其究竟是从结构推理还是直接复制输入中已印出的答案字段；同时作者也担忧自身基准与表征方式可能夸大性能优势。

Method: 固定问题与金标准答案，构建八种匹配视图；在GraphSpace中逐一分名结构关系；加入匹配微调、答案遮蔽、证据注入、配对推理与可拒绝声明的规则；并对基准施加"捷径攻击"，通过迭代加固消除答案泄漏路径。

Result: 最难族上，接地视图比公式输入高17.3分；GraphSpace较普通周期图高19.3分，但拆分为2类效应：普通渲染已含所需信息时仅1.96分，完全缺失这些字段时达46.7分；捷径规则可解7族中的4族，加固后11条捷径接近随机水平；冻结模型在95.6%配对案例中引用关系却不加利用，匹配监督后升至99.8%，删除链接后降至23.4%（低于最佳捷径的27.0%）。

Conclusion: 准确率本身不足以证明结构性推理；性能优势往往反映基线信息缺失而非证据呈现方式；引用关系与实际使用关系需分别验证，且二者的可学习性已被证实。

Abstract: When a materials LLM answers a question about crystal structure, does it reason from the structure or copy an answer already printed in its input? Accuracy cannot tell: a structural description often prints the very field it is scored against. CARAT holds question and gold answer fixed across eight matched views, names each structural relation separately in GraphSpace, and adds matched fine-tuning, answer masking, evidence injection, paired inference, and a rule that can withhold claims. First, on the benchmark's hardest families the grounded view is worth 17.3 points over formula inputs. Second, we turn that scrutiny on ourselves. GraphSpace beats a plain periodic graph by 19.3 points, but that margin is two effects at once: where the plain rendering carries everything the question needs it is 1.96 points, and where it omits those fields entirely, 46.7 points. The headline mostly measures what the baseline lacked, not how evidence is presented. Third, we attack our own benchmark. A rule that skips the link and reads the list directly answers four of seven hardened families, so we rebuilt it until eleven such shortcuts sat near chance. The frozen model quotes that link yet answers the same when we redirect it, on 95.6% of paired cases: it repeats the relation without using it. After matched supervision it reaches 99.8%, and deleting the link drops it to 23.4%, below the 27.0% the best shortcut reaches: both steps are learnable.

</details>


### [130] [Examining Variation in How Guided AI Tutors Resolve Student Impasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38346)
*Bakhtawar Ahtisham,Kirk Vanacore,Alessandra Napoli,Josh Arens,Ksenia Ionova,Clayton Cohn,Shima Salehi,Rene Kizilcec*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文分析真实LLM化学辅导对话中的"僵局"（impasse），发现僵局深度与类型是可观测的对话信号，可据此实现分级、状态敏感的实时辅助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生卡住时导师面临"协助困境"：过早帮助会妨碍有效挣扎，过晚帮助则导致学生陷入挫败的持续僵局（轮子空转）。生成式AI导师越来越多使用限制直接给答案的护栏，但对其在僵局持续时的行为知之甚少。

Method: 分析1,260次真实会话中的20,462个学生回合，识别出6,630个僵局回合（三类：概念错误、表达不确定性、求助）。用这些僵局模拟三种辅导条件（基线、禁止直接给答案、引导式导师），并对150个僵局样本进行提示特异性实验；同时分析真实交互中的僵局轨迹。

Result: 每增加一个僵局回合，下一回合恢复的几率下降12.7%（AOR=0.873, p<.001）；早期辍学者在进入执行前陷入递归的概念诱导。提问的收益随僵局持续而衰减（脚本化提问×深度 AOR=0.78；追问×深度 AOR=0.83），而针对学生错误则收益递增（AOR=1.14）。脚本化提问失败后重复提问的恢复率为28.1%，而针对错误处理的恢复率为39.8%。

Conclusion: 僵局深度和类型是可观测的回合级对话信号，学习分析可据此实时触发分级、状态敏感的辅助。

Abstract: When a student is stuck, a tutor faces the assistance dilemma: help given too early can hinder productive struggle, while help withheld too long leaves the student in a frustrating, persistent impasse (i.e., wheel spinning). Generative AI tutors increasingly use guardrails restricting answer-giving, yet little is known about how such tutors behave once an impasse persists. We analyze 20,462 student turns from 1,260 authentic sessions with a guided LLM chemistry tutor, identifying 6,630 impasse turns of three major types: conceptual errors, expressed uncertainty, or help-seeking. We then used these impasses to simulate three tutoring conditions to study variation in AI tutor guidance through impasses: baseline, no-direct-answer, and guided tutor. For a sample of 150 impasses, prompt specificity changed pedagogy: a baseline tutor provided the answer directly in 50.7% of responses, a no-direct-answer tutor asked a follow-up question every time, and the guided tutor responded in a wide variety of ways depending on the context. We then analyzed impasse trajectories in authentic interactions, finding that each additional impasse turn lowered the odds of next-turn recovery by 12.7% (AOR = 0.873, p < .001), and early dropouts were caught in recursive concept elicitation before reaching execution. The benefit of questioning decayed as impasses persisted (scripted question x depth AOR = 0.78; follow-up x depth AOR = 0.83), whereas addressing the student's error grew more beneficial (AOR = 1.14); after a failed scripted question, repeating it was followed by recovery in 28.1% of cases, compared with 39.8% when the tutor addressed the error instead. For learning analytics, these findings identify impasse depth and type as observable, turn-level dialogue signals that analytics can use to trigger graduated, state-sensitive assistance in real time.

</details>


### [131] [Beyond Mode Collapse: Generating Diverse Synthetic Expert Conversations via Generative Flow Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38359)
*Sumit Asthana,Michael Ion,Kevyn Collins Thompson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出用GFlowNet生成多样化、高质量的合成对话数据，用于LLM后训练，并在辅导与情感支持两个领域验证其优于RL和端到端LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量合成数据对LLM后训练至关重要，需覆盖多样化专家策略。但直接用LLM提示或基于终端场景条件生成的数据多样性低，会塌缩到主导模式，难以代表真实对话中的多样专家策略。

Method: 提出基于生成流网络（GFlowNets）的合成数据生成方法。以对话的关键交互特征（如困惑片段动态、支架指令平衡）构建高斯混合密度，训练GFlowNet生成潜在对话结构，从而按训练数据中的流行度比例采样专家策略。

Result: 在辅导对话和情感支持对话两个结构不同的领域，该方法在保真度、模式覆盖和真实性之间取得更好平衡，优于强化学习与端到端LLM基线，且不复制训练数据。在三个下游结果预测任务上，用GFlowNet合成对话训练的分类器比竞争性合成基线提供更强的训练信号。

Conclusion: GFlowNet可用于生成高多样性、高质量的合成对话数据，兼顾保真度与模式覆盖，为LLM后训练提供更有效的训练信号，适用于多种对话领域。

Abstract: High quality synthetic data is central to post training LLMs for adaptive AI applications that represent the diverse expert strategies and decisions in conversations. Prompting LLMs directly or conditioning them on end use scenarios yields low diversity data that collapses onto dominant modes. We propose a method to generate diverse high quality synthetic data using Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets). We show that training GFlowNets to generate latent conversation structure using a Gaussian mixture density over key interaction features (e.g., confusion episode dynamics, scaffolding directive balance) enables sampling expert strategies in proportion to their prevalence in the training data. Across two structurally distinct domains, tutoring and emotional support dialogues, our GFlow based synthetic data generation approach offers a better balance of fidelity, mode coverage and authenticity than reinforcement-learning and end to end LLM baselines, without copying training data. Evaluated on three downstream outcome prediction tasks, classifiers trained on synthetic GFlowNet generated conversations provide a stronger training signal than competitive synthesis baselines.

</details>


### [132] [Can an AI Agent Rediscover a Blaschke-Curve Invariant?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38369)
*Yunus E. Zeytuncu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将广义Blaschke曲线作为受控环境，用AI代理从数值坐标中重新发现与六点共线相关的三次多项式定理，并提供完整研究日志、检验与局限说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望构建一个可控的数学再发现测试场景：目标定理对代理保密，只能依赖数值坐标与几何假设进行推断，从而考察AI在猜想、数值验证与证明分离方面的表现，并明确此类单例实验的可复现性与先验知识问题。

Method: 固定一个四次Blaschke乘积，向代理提供80个边界构型所对应的六条配对直线的数值坐标。任务说明中隐藏目标定理。研究日志记录被拒绝的几何假设以及拟合多边形各边的齐次三次式。随后用冻结系数对80个未见参数值预测480条直线，并计算无量纲RMS残差；另设单构型实验检验不变性，并在评审后加入确定性次数搜索基线作对照。

Result: 冻结的三次式系数在80个未见参数值上预测480条直线，记录的RMS尺度无关残差为8.88×10^-17。发现集对角线提供拟合外一致性检查，但并非完全留出测试。单独一个构型的运行报告不变性证据不足。评审后的确定性次数搜索基线同样能恢复该三次式，因此实验未能证明相较多项式拟合具有优势。

Conclusion: 该工作作为单一实例案例研究，提出了一套将猜想、数值验证与证明相分离的协议，并明确列出代理元数据、先验知识与可复现性方面的局限；同时作者谨慎指出，由于确定性基线也能恢复该三次式，本实验并不构成AI方法优于常规多项式拟合的证据。

Abstract: We study generalized Blaschke curves as a controlled environment for AI-assisted mathematical rediscovery. For one fixed degree-four Blaschke product, an agent receives numerical coordinates of the six pair-lines determined by each of 80 boundary configurations. The target theorem is withheld from the task instructions. The saved research log reports rejected geometric hypotheses and a homogeneous cubic fitted to polygon sides. Its frozen coefficients predict 480 lines from 80 unseen parameter values, with a recorded RMS scale-free residual of $8.88\times10^{-17}$. Discovery-set diagonals provide an out-of-fit consistency check, not a fully held-out test. A separate one-configuration run reports insufficient evidence for invariance. A post-review deterministic degree-search baseline also recovers the cubic, so the experiment does not establish an advantage over polynomial fitting. We present this single-instance case study as a protocol for separating conjecture, numerical validation, and proof, with explicit limitations concerning agent metadata, prior knowledge, and reproducibility.

</details>


### [133] [Risk-Aware Adaptive Evaluation: Finding High-Impact Failures Under Limited Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38914)
*Priyanath Maji,Spandan Ghose Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将交互式智能体评测建模为序贯分配问题，提出风险感知上下文Thompson Sampling策略，在固定试验预算下优先测试高影响力且易失败的场景，在极小预算下显著优于均匀分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式智能体评测成本高昂：行为随机需重复试验，但失败罕见且重要程度差异大。标准基准采用均匀采样，把只读查询与不可逆支付动作同等对待，导致预算浪费在从不失败或低影响的场景上。

Method: 将评测形式化为序贯分配问题：在固定试验预算与失败行为未知的场景集合下，决定运行与重复运行哪些场景。提出风险感知的上下文Thompson Sampling策略，将执行前场景上下文向量与固定影响分数，同评测中观察到的失败结果相结合进行采样分配。通过离线回放验证：70个τ-bench航空场景、824条记录试验。

Result: 最小预算下优势最显著：仅用50次试验（语料库6%）即可恢复oracle可找到的86%影响力加权失败，均匀分配仅25%；同样试验数下发现的影响力加权失败多3.5倍（215.4 vs 62.2），每美元发现量5倍，浪费预算从34%降至2.8%。预算扫描显示预算接近语料规模时优势缩小；配对显著性检验显示场景上下文主要在小预算下有益，基于后验的探索在中等预算下有益。

Conclusion: 风险感知自适应分配在评测预算最稀缺时收益最大，能高效定位高影响的罕见失败，是替代均匀采样评测的可行方案。

Abstract: Evaluating interactive agents is expensive. Agent behavior is stochastic, so reliability must be measured over repeated trials, but failures are rare and differ widely in how much they matter. Standard benchmarks spend this budget uniformly: a read-only lookup is sampled as often as an irreversible payment action. We instead formulate evaluation as a sequential allocation problem. Given a fixed trial budget and a set of scenarios whose failure behavior is unknown, which scenarios should be run, and run again? We propose a risk-aware contextual Thompson Sampling policy that combines a pre-execution scenario context vector and a fixed impact score with the failure outcomes observed during evaluation, and we test it by offline replay over 70 $τ$-bench airline scenarios and 824 recorded trials. Our main result is at the smallest budget: with only 50 trials ($6\%$ of the corpus), the policy recovers $86\%$ of the impact-weighted failures an oracle could find, compared to $25\%$ for uniform allocation. It discovers $3.5\times$ more impact-weighted failures (215.4 vs. 62.2) with the same number of trials, delivers $5\times$ the discovery per dollar, and cuts the budget wasted on scenarios that never fail from $34\%$ to $2.8\%$. The rest of our analysis demonstrates and qualifies this result: a budget sweep shows the advantage shrinks as the budget approaches the corpus size, and paired significance tests show that scenario context helps mainly at small budgets while posterior-based exploration helps at moderate ones. Risk-aware adaptive allocation therefore helps most exactly where evaluation budget is scarcest.

</details>


### [134] [Self-Evolving Harness on Multiple Tasks with the Agent as Its Own Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38372)
*Qiankai Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种接近递归自我改进的自进化harness框架：同一冻结模型在同一harness版本上先作solver解题、再作proposer读取完整运行记录并直接编辑harness；跨五领域多任务预训练+持续训练两阶段演化，泛化到五个分布外基准，整体超越或追平Codex。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化harness方法多由独立proposer在人工设计的harness上修改solver的harness，且每个基准单独演化一个harness；但真实任务跨多领域，harness的演化与评估都应覆盖多样化任务，并需检验泛化能力。

Method: 同一冻结模型、同一harness版本下，先作为solver解题，再作为proposer读取完整运行记录并直接编辑运行自身的harness；每个演化批次从五个不同领域基准抽取任务；严格分离训练与留出任务，并额外在五个演化中未使用的分布外基准上评估；将演化过程类比深度学习训练，分为多任务预训练与持续训练两阶段；初始harness仅49行。

Result: 第一阶段结束的harness在分布内基准上平均分提升4.48分，在分布外基准上提升12.64分，前者超越Codex、后者追平Codex；第二阶段在分布外基准Claw-Eval上继续演化，分数从66.17提升至68.06，超过Codex；并观察到演化中涌现的机制，包括输出截断、历史压缩与独立审查。

Conclusion: 让同一冻结模型兼任solver与proposer来直接编辑自身harness的递归式自进化框架可行且有效，能显著提升跨域泛化并超越/追平强基线；涌现出的上下文管理与审查机制值得进一步分析。

Abstract: A harness is the code around a language-model agent that organizes prompts, calls tools, manages context, and controls execution. As models grow stronger, recent work has begun to let agents improve their own harnesses, a line of work known as self-evolving harnesses. In most existing methods, a separate proposer running on a human-designed harness modifies the solver's harness, and a separate harness is evolved for each benchmark. Real-world tasks come from many domains, so both the evolution and the evaluation of a harness should cover a diverse range of tasks. We propose a framework close to recursive self-improvement: the same frozen model, on the same version of the harness, first solves tasks as the solver and then, as the proposer, reads the complete run records and directly edits the harness that runs it. Each evolution batch draws tasks from five benchmarks in different domains. To measure generalization, training and held-out tasks are strictly separated, and we additionally evaluate on five out-of-distribution benchmarks never used during evolution. We frame the evolution process as deep-learning training with two stages, multi-task pretraining and continual training. Starting from a 49-line seed harness, the harness obtained at the end of the first stage improves the average score by 4.48 points on the in-distribution benchmarks and by 12.64 points on the out-of-distribution benchmarks, surpassing Codex on the former and matching it on the latter. In the second stage, continued evolution on Claw-Eval, one of the out-of-distribution benchmarks, further raises the score on that benchmark from 66.17 to 68.06, exceeding Codex. We also provide an in-depth analysis of the mechanisms that emerged during evolution, including output truncation, history compaction, and independent review.

</details>


### [135] [Aligned Data Can Induce Misalignment via Context Confusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38379)
*Yavuz Bakman,Duygu Nur Yaldiz,Baris Askin,Swastik Roy,Morteza Ziyadi,Salman Avestimehr,Sai Praneeth Karimireddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现大模型后训练中存在"上下文混淆"现象：在某一语境下对齐的训练会导致其他语境下出现错位行为，并给出了机制解释与缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐本质上是依赖语境的：同一条建议在一种语境下是恰当的，在另一种语境下可能不当（如"保存数据"对科研复现是好的，但对用户隐私数据则不合适）。因此仅凭过滤错位样本无法保证更新后模型的对齐状态，需要重新审视后训练对齐评估。

Method: 作者提出并定义"上下文混淆"（context confusion）这一后训练现象，在性别平等、隐私、身体安全三个领域进行了实验验证；对比了窄化错位与涌现错位的区别；测试了注入通用对齐数据、注入目标领域对齐数据、以及推理时提供上下文学习示例等干预手段；并从表征角度给出机制解释，观察不同领域查询在微调中经历相似的表示漂移。

Result: 上下文混淆在三个领域均可复现，且表现为"窄化错位"而非涌现错位；注入通用对齐数据难以缓解，而加入针对错位领域的目标对齐数据或在推理时提供上下文示例可显著降低该现象。机制上，不同领域查询在微调中出现相似表示漂移，导致其他领域查询激活了微调中学到的同一行为特征，从而把行为迁移到错位语境。

Conclusion: 仅通过检查训练数据难以预测模型训练后的对齐状态，凸显了开展全面后训练对齐评估的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are frequently updated for various use cases, where filtering out misaligned training samples is a common practice for preventing post-update misalignment. However, alignment is inherently context-dependent: a recommendation that is aligned in one context may be inappropriate in another. For example, in response to the question "What should a researcher do with the research data?", recommending that the researcher preserve the data for reproducibility is aligned. In contrast, recommending data saving in response to "What should a mobile-app developer do with users' sensitive data?" may be inappropriate from a privacy perspective. Starting from this observation, we identify a post-training phenomenon where aligned training induces misaligned behavior in other contexts. We call this phenomenon **context confusion**. We demonstrate context confusion across three domains: (1) Gender Equality, (2) Privacy, and (3) Physical Safety. We further show that context confusion causes narrow misalignment, in contrast to emergent misalignment, and is not effectively reduced by injecting general alignment data, but can be substantially reduced by including targeted alignment data for the misaligned domain or providing in-context learning examples during inference. Lastly, we provide a mechanistic explanation of *context confusion*. We observe that queries from different domains can undergo similar representational shifts during the fine-tuning. Consequently, a query from a different domain may activate the same behavioral feature learned during fine-tuning, which causes the behavior to transfer to a context where it is misaligned. Based on our findings, we argue that it is difficult to predict the alignment state of a model after training by inspecting the training data alone, which highlights the importance of comprehensive post-training alignment evaluations.

</details>


### [136] [RefCon: Iterative Refinement and Contrastive Memory Extraction for Context-Evolving Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39143)
*Ubaidillah Ariq Prathama,Bo Liu,Yeo Boon Hong,Yu-Xuan Huang,Yangkai Ding,Tao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RefCon，通过序列自精炼与并行自对比实现无金标签的高质量记忆提取，在多个智能体基准上取得显著稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体交互产生大量有用但含噪的经验，而重新训练模型吸收这些经验代价高昂；因此上下文演化型智能体需要一种能在测试时增加算力即改善、且不依赖金标签的记忆提取方法。

Method: RefCon 将序列式自精炼（sequential self-refinement）与并行式自对比（parallel self-contrast）相结合，在无金标签条件下提取更高质量的记忆；同时提出侧重多样性的变体 DivCon。

Result: 在 AppWorld 与 BFCL-V3 上，跨多种上下文演化型智能体框架评测，RefCon 相对无扩展基线在 ACE 上提升 21.6%、ReMe 上提升 16.6%；多样性变体 DivCon 在 ReasoningBank 上提升 35.5%。RefCon 稳定优于无真值标签的现有基线，并能泛化到不同模型规模及软件工程任务，甚至超过金标签基线。

Conclusion: RefCon 在精度—token 权衡与扩展行为上保持良好效率，随可用轨迹增多而持续提升，而仅依赖多样性的扩展方式会较早饱和。

Abstract: Long-horizon agent interactions generate useful but noisy experience, and retraining models to absorb it is expensive. Context-evolving agents therefore need memory extraction methods that improve with more test-time compute without relying on gold labels. We propose RefCon, which combines sequential self-refinement with parallel self-contrast to extract higher-quality memories without gold labels. Evaluated on AppWorld and BFCL-V3 across multiple context-evolving agent frameworks, RefCon delivers strong and consistent gains, including relative improvements of 21.6% on ACE and 16.6% on ReMe over no-scaling baselines, while a diversity-focused variant (DivCon) achieves a 35.5% gain on ReasoningBank. RefCon consistently outperforms existing baselines without ground-truth labels, and generalizes across model scales and to software engineering tasks, where it surpasses even ground-truth baselines. We further analyze the accuracy-token trade-off and scaling behavior, showing RefCon maintains favorable efficiency and continues to improve as more trajectories are used, unlike diversity-only scaling which saturates earlier.

</details>


### [137] [Fine-Tuning Diffusion Language Models with Context Selection and Target Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38385)
*Loay Mualem,Lluís Pastor-Pérez,Vinh Tong,Andrei Manolache,Tanja Bien,Steffen Staab,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GoldiMask：通过子模优化的掩码选择为离散扩散语言模型的监督微调提供更优的训练信号


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散语言模型的监督微调依赖掩码策略决定可见上下文与预测目标，而均匀随机掩码未显式考虑二者之间的相互作用。

Method: 提出 GoldiMask，通过近似最大化一个子模目标来选择揭示为上下文的 token，该目标用模型信号平衡揭示收益与作为预测目标的价值；随后对剩余目标按受益程度与剩余学习潜力加权。

Result: 在三种骨干和三个训练数据集上，多数评估设置取得最高平均准确率，在推理与代码生成上均有提升；消融显示上下文选择与目标加权均有贡献；在 GSM8K 与 MATH-500 上减少了解码迭代次数，并在较高置信阈值下保持相当准确率。

Conclusion: GoldiMask 通过显式建模上下文选择与目标加权的交互，有效提升离散扩散语言模型微调效果与解码效率。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning of discrete diffusion language models masks some response tokens and trains the model to recover their original values from the visible context. The masking pattern therefore determines both the context available to the model and the tokens it learns to predict. Uniform random masking does not explicitly account for the interaction between these choices. We introduce GoldiMask, which selects tokens to reveal as context by approximately maximizing a submodular objective. This objective uses model signals to balance the benefit of revealing tokens against their value as prediction targets. GoldiMask then weights the remaining targets according to how they benefit from the selected context and their remaining learning potential. Across three backbones and three training datasets, GoldiMask achieves the highest average accuracy in most evaluated settings, demonstrating gains on both reasoning and code generation. Component ablations show that both context selection and target weighting contribute to the gains. GoldiMask also reduces decoding iterations on GSM8K and MATH-500 under confidence-threshold parallel decoding, while maintaining comparable accuracy at higher confidence thresholds.

</details>


### [138] [OverForge: Reasoning Through Strategies and Tactics Helps Cooperative Lifelong Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39727)
*Oana Madalina Fron,Ojas Shirekar,Chirag Raman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练分层架构 OverForge，将协作语言模型智能体的战略推理与战术执行分离，在 OvercookedV2 中显著优于扁平 LLM 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作语言模型智能体直接将观测映射为动作，无法把持久的协作策略与临场战术执行区分开来，因而难以在长时程协作、环境变化以及面对陌生协作规范时保持有效协调。

Method: OverForge 是一种免训练的分层架构：上层进行角色分工与劳动分配等战略推理，下层在每个智能体私有的、以伙伴为条件的世界模型中做动作层面的战术推理。中间由一个元认知"前额叶皮层模块"耦合两层，通过构造策略-动作分支、用前向模型想象其后果，并在置信时做出承诺。

Result: 在 OvercookedV2 中，OverForge 在连通厨房内完成 7 份汤，而每个扁平 LLM 基线仅 3 份；它能保持已达成的角色分工，并采纳陌生伙伴提出的角色。消融实验与固定策略探针表明，持久策略引导战术适应，且两个推理层级都对协调有贡献。记忆重启实验显示跨回合的伙伴知识支持任务表现与伙伴预测，将分层结构与持续适应联系起来。

Conclusion: 将战略与战术推理显式分离并配以元认知承诺机制，可在无需训练的前提下显著提升长时程协作表现与对陌生伙伴的适应能力。

Abstract: Cooperative language-model agents must coordinate over long horizons and adapt to changing environments and to partners with unfamiliar conventions, yet existing agents map observations to actions without separating persistent coordination strategies from their tactical execution. We introduce OverForge, a training-free hierarchical architecture that separates strategic reasoning over roles and divisions of labour from tactical reasoning over actions within each agent's private, partner-conditioned world model. A metacognitive Prefrontal Cortex Module couples the two levels by forming strategy-action branches, imagining their consequences with a forward model, and committing when confident. In OvercookedV2, OverForge delivers 7 soups in a connected kitchen versus 3 for each flat LLM baseline, retains agreed roles, and adopts roles proposed by unfamiliar partners. Ablations and a fixed-strategy probe show that persistent strategies guide tactical adaptation while each reasoning level contributes to coordination. Memory restarts show that cross-episode partner knowledge supports task performance and partner prediction, linking the hierarchy to continual adaptation.

</details>


### [139] [Decode-Latency Feedback Prefill: A Model-Free Controller and Its Generalization Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38386)
*Gaurav Agarwal,Ashish Garg,Isha Singhal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 DLFP（解码延迟反馈预填充）控制器，通过反馈调节重叠预填充块大小来降低并发自回归推理中的 P99 令牌间延迟，在小模型上有效，但在大模型与张量并行下失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发自回归推理存在根本性干扰：新到的长提示预填充会延迟正在解码请求的令牌生成。固定预填充分块虽能缓解干扰，但最优块大小取决于模型、硬件、负载与延迟目标，难以静态确定。

Method: 提出 DLFP，一种无模型控制器，仅调整与活跃解码重叠的预填充工作。经过带保护的调度周期后，将观测到的间隔作为比例反馈来调整下一个预填充块大小；孤立的预填充不受限制。在 vLLM 中实现，采用开环泊松到达、精确令牌计数、原始请求轨迹与 NVIDIA 遥测进行评估。

Result: 在单张 A100 80GB 上以 BF16 运行 Qwen3-0.6B，三次配对的 100 请求试验将 P99 令牌间延迟分别降低 24.8%、30.1%、28.2%（均值 27.7%，配对 95% 置信区间 21.0%–34.3%），输出完全一致、无失败、SLO 合规性不变。代价是平均 P99 首令牌时间增加 34.8%，但仍在声明的 SLO 内。然而该机制无法泛化到 Qwen3-8B、Qwen3-32B 或双 GPU 张量并行配置，失败原因在于异步调度调用间隔仅能近似反映 GPU 迭代完成时间。

Conclusion: 该负结果界定了贡献的边界，并推动面向 CPU 与端侧并发推理的"完成计时"控制器研究。作者不声称具备移动设备性能，本文为可复现的概念验证与泛化性研究。

Abstract: Concurrent autoregressive inference creates a fundamental interference problem: prefilling a newly arrived long prompt can delay tokens for requests that are already decoding. Fixed prefill chunks reduce this interference, but the best chunk size depends on the model, hardware, load, and latency objective. We introduce Decode-Latency Feedback Prefill (DLFP), a model-free controller that changes only prefill work that overlaps active decodes. After a guarded scheduling cycle, DLFP uses the observed interval as proportional feedback to resize the next prefill chunk; isolated prefills remain unrestricted.
  We implement DLFP in vLLM and evaluate it with open-loop Poisson arrivals, exact token accounting, raw request traces, and NVIDIA telemetry. On Qwen3-0.6B in BF16 on one A100 80 GB GPU, three paired 100-request trials reduce P99 inter-token latency by 24.8%, 30.1%, and 28.2% (mean 27.7%, paired 95% confidence interval 21.0% to 34.3%) with exact output agreement, no failures, and unchanged SLO compliance. The benefit is not free: mean P99 time to first token increases 34.8% while remaining inside the declared SLO.
  Crucially, the mechanism does not generalize to Qwen3-8B, Qwen3-32B, or a two-GPU tensor-parallel configuration. We trace the failure to an asynchronous scheduler-call interval that is only a proxy for completed GPU iteration time. This negative result defines the boundary of the contribution and motivates a completion-timed controller for concurrent CPU and on-device inference. We do not claim mobile-device performance; the present work is a reproducible proof-of-concept and generalization study.

</details>


### [140] [Safety of Latent Communication in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39788)
*Muhammad Huzaifa,Sina Mavali,Thorsten Eisenhofer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 潜在通信中的可训练链接即使用良性数据训练也可能提高有害合规率；攻击者可通过优化链接、投毒数据或强化学习攻击显著放大该效应（27.9→76.9），而通过调整奖励即可修复受损链接，说明安全对齐需以多智能体系统整体为对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在通信可降低多智能体系统在token、计算与延迟上的开销，但其安全性未被充分研究。作者关注：在底层智能体保持安全对齐不变的前提下，仅训练连接模块（链接）是否会影响系统的有害行为。

Method: 在多智能体潜在通信中引入轻量可训练链接，将发送方表示映射到接收方输入空间。研究三类情形：(1) 良性链接训练；(2) 攻击者用有害问答对优化链接或投毒良性训练数据；(3) 一种强化学习攻击，奖励有害合规与良性任务表现，且不需要有害目标回复。并在三种通信拓扑、四个安全基准及两个良性效用基准上评估，另尝试通过调整奖励进行修复。

Result: 良性链接训练即可使有害合规高于文本通信。强化学习攻击将平均有害合规得分从27.9（良性训练链接）提升至76.9；相比直接监督优化，在两个良性效用基准上平均准确率更高。将奖励导向更安全行为可修复受损链接，在所有评估攻击下大幅降低有害合规，且无需更新智能体本身。

Conclusion: 链接层面的训练与攻击即可在不改变智能体的情况下破坏系统安全性，也可通过奖励调整加以修复。因此安全对齐必须将多智能体系统作为整体来考虑，而非仅关注单个智能体。

Abstract: Latent communication enables multi-agent systems to exchange information directly in internal representation space, reducing the token, computation, and latency overhead of text-based communication. To this end, lightweight trainable links are introduced to map the sender's representations into the receiver's input space. In this work, we show that even benign link training can increase harmful compliance relative to text-based communication while the underlying safety-aligned agents remain unchanged. An attacker can amplify this effect by optimizing the links on harmful query--response pairs or poisoning otherwise benign training data. We further develop a reinforcement-learning attack that rewards harmful compliance alongside benign task performance without requiring harmful target responses. Across three communication topologies and four safety benchmarks, this attack raises the mean harmful-compliance score from 27.9 with benignly trained links to 76.9. Compared with direct supervised optimization, it also achieves higher average accuracy on two benign utility benchmarks. Adapting the rewards toward safer behavior also enables repair of compromised links, substantially reducing harmful compliance across all evaluated attacks without updating the agents. Overall, our results show that safety alignment requires considering the multi-agent system as a whole.

</details>


### [141] [MetaPersona: Task-Grounded Synthetic Populations from Empirical Social Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38392)
*Jinyi Ye,Yuangang Li,Chenxiao Yu,Preyashi Poddar,Priyanka Dey,Longtian Ye,Zihan Wang,Xiyang Hu,Emilio Ferrara,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 MetaPersona-DB 数据集与 MetaPersona 框架，基于实证研究解决LLM社会模拟中角色构造的冷启动问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM社会模拟中用于初始化的人设（persona）存在冷启动问题：现有方法缺乏选择哪些属性及如何赋值的原则性依据，导致合成人群在人口构成、潜在属性和依赖结构上可能失真，进而影响下游行为。

Method: 构建 MetaPersona-DB，包含11,000+项实证人类被试研究，标注任务相关变量、已报告关系及群体层面统计数据；提出 MetaPersona 框架，检索任务相关证据、构建源自文献的人设依赖图，并从连接人口统计、潜在属性与结果的实证先验中采样合成人群。

Result: 在三个下游案例、三个基线与三个前沿模型上，效果因任务和模型而异：在错误信息信念与AI工具情感任务上表现强，在收入再分配任务上结果参差；使用GPT-5.2可将人设构造成本降至每任务0.5美元以下。

Conclusion: MetaPersona 为基于实证的角色生成提供了可行路径，并附带交互原型 MetaPersona-Studio，但效果的任务依赖性表明其普适性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Personas used to seed LLM social simulations face a cold-start problem: existing methods lack a principled basis for deciding which attributes to include and how to assign their values.
