Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) improve performance on complex tasks through extended reasoning, yet the same process can degenerate into redundant verification and persistent generation loops. Such uncontrolled reasoning increases inference cost and creates risks of resource exhaustion and service degradation. However, existing mitigations largely truncate long outputs or react to surface repetition, and thus fail to distinguish normal thinking from uncontrolled reasoning or explain how benign reasoning degenerates into harmful behavior. In this paper, we operationalize LRM generation as four states and further introduce Reasoning-state Analysis via Dynamic Attention Responses (RADAR), which identifies the current reasoning state in real time and characterizes how effective reflection can develop into uncontrolled generation. Guided by RADAR's analysis, we further realign abnormal attention distributions toward patterns observed in normal requests and examine how this correction affects excessive reflection and persistent looping. Temporal analyses show that uncontrolled reasoning is characterized by attention distributions that deviate from normal generation, with abnormal trends becoming detectable before repetition begins. Correcting these deviations through Attention Realignment consistently reduces looping while largely preserving benign performance. Together, RADAR provide a mechanistic account of how reasoning becomes uncontrolled, offering actionable guidance for identifying critical failure stages and designing targeted runtime interventions.

</details>


### [177] [Whose Voice Survives the Summary? A Voice-Retention Audit of LLM Employee Listening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38818)
*Thilo Tamme,Anton Hantel,Bijan Khosrawi-Rad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM摘要员工反馈时的"代表性偏差"，提出"声音保留/代表比率"指标，并在英德双语语料上证明：摘要管道按"流行度"而非情感过滤，致使少数(仅一次提出)意见与德语内容被系统性丢弃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织越来越多地用大语言模型摘要把员工反馈转达给领导，这一未经审计的中间层会"消音"已经表达出来的意见。现有审计多聚焦情感倾向，可能遗漏由意见普遍程度驱动的代表性偏差，因此需要一个可量化的声音保留指标并给出实地证据。

Method: 提出"声音保留/代表比率(Voice Retention / Representation Ratio)"指标，用于衡量摘要中的代表性偏差；构建来自某全球专业服务公司的双语(英语/德语)语料，共2,586条自由文本反馈；分析45份领导层摘要，考察情感、提及频次、语言等因素对意见保留的影响，并测试针对性提示(prompt)的补救效果。

Result: 员工提出批评比赞扬更可靠( withheld praise 出现频率是批评的82倍)；摘要管道按流行度而非情感过滤：批评本身能保留，但仅被提及一次的关切有86%被丢弃；短文本和纯德语内容以同一轴被丢失(主题保留率0.14 vs 0.74，德语方向性缺失)；控制频次后情感无独立效应，危害由普遍程度(prevalence)驱动，而仅看情感的审计无法发现；针对性提示只能找回被点名的主题。

Conclusion: 该研究贡献了新的声音保留指标、实地证据以及一份"分项声音保留卡(disaggregated voice-retention card)"，揭示LLM摘要中的代表性偏差主要由意见普遍程度驱动而非情感，呼吁对此类未经审计的摘要层进行更细粒度的审计。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly route employee feedback to leaders through large language model (LLM) summaries, an unaudited layer that silences already-spoken voice. We introduce a Voice Retention / Representation Ratio metric for representational bias in summarization and apply it to a bilingual (English/German) corpus of 2,586 free-text responses from a global professional service company. First, employees supply criticism more reliably than praise (withholding praise is 82 times more common). Second, across 45 leader-summaries the pipeline filters by popularity, not sentiment: criticism survives, yet a concern voiced once is dropped 86% of the time, with short and German-only content lost on the same axis (theme retention 0.14 vs 0.74; German directional). Controlling for frequency, sentiment has no independent effect; the harm is prevalence-driven, which sentiment-only audits miss. A targeted prompt recovers only named themes. We contribute the metric, field evidence, and a disaggregated voice-retention card.

</details>


### [178] [More Choices, Fewer Decisions: Ordinal-Scale Bias in JEV-like Direct-Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38827)
*Tianxiang Gao,Jinzhe Li,Zhiyuan Li,Yi Chang,Yuan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现直接决策模型存在"序数尺度利用偏差"：候选空间在决策阶段被压缩，且该偏差可被针对性微调修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接决策模型在分类与自动评估中被广泛使用，但其可靠性不仅取决于准确率，还取决于能否忠实使用用户提供的序数决策尺度。研究动机是探究此类模型是否存在系统性的尺度利用不足问题。

Method: 分析 JEV 1.13 与三个开源 KEV 模型；以 ANLI 为起点考察预测分布；在 36 个序数数据集上对比 4 个名义任务；随机化候选顺序；在固定条目与源分数条件下，将尺度从 K=2 细化到 K=14 检验利用率；采用 BA-LoRA 后训练进行干预。

Result: JEV 在 ANLI 上准确率 74.95%，却将 38.8% 预测和 51.3% 错误集中于 Neutral，尽管金标近似平衡。36 个序数数据集的决策仅利用 67–76% 的有效金标支持范围，而 4 个名义任务为 87–102%。随机化候选顺序会减弱但不消除压缩。K=14 时利用率降至 26–75%。BA-LoRA 后训练将八种监督尺度上的金标相对利用率从约 47% 提升至 86%。

Conclusion: 该压缩现象被命名为"序数尺度利用偏差"，是独立于准确率、金标不平衡、固定位置与候选数量的决策阶段候选空间压缩，且是学习得来的、可修正的，而非架构固有限制。

Abstract: Direct-decision models turn text into low-latency structured labels and scores, making them attractive for classification and automatic evaluation. Yet reliability requires more than accuracy: a model must also use the ordinal decision scale supplied by the user faithfully. We analyze JEV~1.13 and three open KEV models. Our investigation begins with ANLI, where JEV assigns 38.8\% of all predictions and 51.3\% of errors to Neutral despite 74.95\% accuracy, nearly balanced gold labels, and balanced candidate positions. Across 36 ordinal datasets, final decisions use only 67--76\% of the effective gold support, versus 87--102\% on four nominal tasks. Randomizing candidate order weakens but does not remove this compression. Holding items and source scores fixed while balancing gold support and positions, we refine scales from $K=2$ to $14$; utilization falls for every model and reaches 26--75\% at $K=14$, although candidate probabilities remain broad for most models. Targeted BA-LoRA post-training raises gold-relative utilization from roughly 47\% to 86\% on eight supervised scales at both KEV sizes, showing that the compression is learned and modifiable rather than an immutable architectural limit. We call this ordinal scale-utilization bias: decision-stage candidate-space compression distinct from accuracy, gold imbalance, fixed position, and candidate count alone. The code and data are available at https://github.com/Glax147/jev_ordinal_scale_bia

</details>


### [179] [Diversity Combining for Multi-Path LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38829)
*Guangsheng Yu,Litianyi Zhang,Qin Wang,Xu Wang,Mingyuan Li,Shaoxiong Ji,Ren Ping Liu,Massimo Piccardi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将多路径LLM推理类比为无线通信中的分集合并问题，提出路径相关性限制投票有效样本量，并给出自适应K选择规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自洽性等多路径推理方法随采样路径数K增加收益迅速饱和，且现有方法无法预判饱和何时发生，因此需要理论刻画饱和机制并指导K的设定。

Method: 将每条推理路径视为噪声信道观测，用路径正确性的两两相关性界定投票的设计效应有效样本量上限；通过广义最小二乘（GLS）分析在可交换性假设下证明潜在嵌入的最优对称线性合并为均匀权重，支持多数投票作为标准自洽性的默认选择，同时为异质提示模板分支下的加权或剪枝留出空间；并在5个模型、12个基准上检验提示模板多样性对路径相关性的影响，提出用四条路径试点来选择K*的Adaptive-K规则。

Result: 在57个有效单元中有55个单元显示提示模板多样性降低路径相关性，在开放式问答上效果最强；Adaptive-K规则在数学、问答与自然语言理解任务上保留了MV@K=32准确率的96%–103%。

Conclusion: 多路径推理的增益受路径相关性导致的有限有效样本量上限约束，多数投票在可交换条件下具有理论合理性，而通过增加提示模板多样性并结合Adaptive-K可高效逼近大规模投票的精度。

Abstract: Multi-path reasoning methods such as self-consistency (SC) sample $K$ reasoning paths and choose the most frequent answer. However, their gains quickly plateau as $K$ increases, and existing methods do not predict when this saturation will occur. We formalize multi-path LLM reasoning as a diversity combining problem from wireless communications: each path is a noisy channel observation, and the pairwise correlation of path correctness caps the design-effect effective sample size of the vote at a finite ceiling. Generalized least squares (GLS) analysis shows that, under exchangeability, the optimal symmetric linear combiner of latent embeddings is uniform, supporting majority vote as the natural default in standard SC while leaving room for weighting or pruning under heterogeneous prompt-template branches. Across 5 models and 12 benchmarks, prompt-template diversity reduces path correlation in $55$ of $57$ valid cells, with the strongest effect on open-ended QA. We derive an Adaptive-K rule that uses a four-path pilot to select $K^*$, retaining $96$--$103\%$ of MV@$K{=}32$ accuracy across Math, QA, and NLU.

</details>


### [180] [OpenJev-RLCD: A Working RLCD Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38850)
*Zhimin Gao,Pichao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RLCD（面向校准决策的强化学习），用严格适当评分规则对推理模型作答分布打分，先校准再强化，在准确率与选择性预测上匹配或超越SFT、RFT/STaR与GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策模型（如Jev）给出的概率只有在校准后才有用。开源复现依赖监督微调加温度缩放，而从可验证奖励出发的强化学习（RLVR）会让推理模型过度自信，因此需要一种能让推理模型保持校准的强化学习方法。

Method: 提出RLCD的可运行实现：模型先采样一个推理过程（rationale），再用严格适当评分规则对其随后给出的答案分布打分。通过方差恒等式分析发现，对多个样本的混合打分等价于奖励推理过程之间的分歧，而RLVR正是缺少多样性项的该混合目标。由于直接优化会使每推理过程目标要么关闭推理、要么被策略梯度噪声淹没，故采用两阶段配方：先校准，再强化。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B上于两个推理任务、3个随机种子、配对检验设置下，RLCD在准确率上匹配或优于（均经温度缩放的）SFT、RFT/STaR与GRPO，并在选择性预测上全面胜出；在GSM8K答案验证中，单次查询可以≤5%误差判定约五分之二的条目，而GRPO仅为五分之一左右。当不确定性来自标注者分歧时，RLCD可证明无法优于交叉熵。

Conclusion: RLCD为推理模型提供了一种兼顾校准与性能的强化学习方案，两阶段（先校准后强化）设计是必要的；同时明确了该方法在标注者分歧型不确定性下的理论局限。代码与结果已开源。

Abstract: Decision models such as Jev answer questions with probabilities, which are only useful if they are calibrated. Open-source reproductions rely on supervised fine-tuning plus temperature scaling, while reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) makes reasoning models overconfident. We present a working implementation of reinforcement learning for calibrated decisions (RLCD) for reasoning models: the model samples a rationale, and we score the answer distribution it commits to afterwards with a strictly proper scoring rule. A variance identity shows that scoring the mixture of several samples rewards disagreeing rationales, and that RLVR is exactly this mixture objective without its diversity term. Optimized naively, the per-rationale objective either switches reasoning off or is drowned out by policy-gradient noise, which leads to a two-stage recipe: calibrate, then reinforce. With Qwen3-1.7B on two reasoning tasks (3 seeds, paired tests), RLCD matches or beats SFT, RFT/STaR and GRPO (each temperature-scaled) in accuracy and beats all of them in selective prediction; on GSM8K answer verification a single query decides \gvTwoCovFive\% of the items at $\le$5\% error, versus \gvGrpoCovFive\% for GRPO. When uncertainty comes from annotator disagreement, RLCD provably cannot beat cross-entropy. Code and results: https://github.com/ZimmyGao/openjev-rlcd.

</details>


### [181] [When Context Changes: Understanding Update Failures in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38866)
*Junyu Guo,Yuchen Fang,Shangding Gu,Costas Spanos,James Demmel,Javad Lavaei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体在上下文存在多版本信息时的"陈旧绑定"失效，提出CICM基准，发现注意力漂移机制，并用免训练注意力干预方法纠正大部分旧值错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着偏好、目标和事实不断变化，LLM智能体需要在保留旧版本上下文的同时使用最新状态。然而模型可能回答同一变量的过时值（stale binding）。作者希望系统研究模型何时以及为何使用过时信息。

Method: 提出Controlled In-Context Memory (CICM) 基准，用于在对话和智能体日志中追踪并使用更新信息；通过探针实验和Qwen、Pythia的组件测试分析选择失效机制；借助单层Transformer数学分析注意力漂移；并据此提出免训练的注意力重定向干预，针对每个输入进行调整。

Result: 即使是前沿推理模型也可能无法恢复当前状态。开源模型中，当模型回答旧值时，探针仍能恢复更新值，说明信息存在但未被选中。组件测试识别出"注意力漂移"——生成答案时注意力偏向旧值。单层Transformer分析表明，当注意力分数相近时，多个旧值可共同获得比当前值更多的注意力。该方法在直接请求当前值时，能纠正多种模型家族的大部分旧值错误，同时几乎保留所有原本正确的答案。

Conclusion: 可靠的上下文管理不仅需要记住更新后的信息，还要求模型在生成答案时真正使用这些信息来引导输出。

Abstract: As preferences, goals, and facts change, LLM agents must use the current state while earlier versions remain in context. Yet they can answer with an old value of the same variable, a failure that we call stale binding. To study when models use outdated information and why, we introduce Controlled In-Context Memory (CICM), a benchmark for tracking and using updated information in conversations and agent logs. We observe that even frontier reasoning models can fail to recover the current state. We find that in open-source models probes can still recover the updated value when the model answers with an old one, pointing to a failure to select information that remains available. Component tests in Qwen and Pythia identify a mechanism for this selection failure: attention drift, where attention favors old values over the current one when producing an answer. We study a one-layer transformer to mathematically understand how this phenomenon happens: when attention scores are similar, several old values can together receive more attention than the current value. Guided by this explanation, we redirect attention toward the current value without further training. When the current value is requested directly, adjusting this intervention for each input corrects most old-value errors across various model families while preserving nearly all initially correct answers. Reliable context management therefore requires more than remembering updated information: models must use it to guide their answers.

</details>


### [182] [Talk2Agent: Benchmarking Voice Interfaces for Text Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38867)
*Terumi Chiba,Guangzhi Sun,Zheqi Yuan,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Talk2Agent，一个评估语音接口向 LLM 计算机操作代理传递人类口述指令有效性的基准，并设计了免执行、任务条件化的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 计算机操作代理通常以干净的书面指令进行评估，但语音正成为流行交互方式。语音输入引入额外失败点：转录错误会在代理推理前改变任务关键实体、约束或目标，而传统 ASR 指标（如 WER/CER）无法直接衡量执行所需信息是否被保留，因此需要新的评测基准与指标。

Method: 基于 WildClawBench 与 OSWorld 构建人类口述版本任务；评估多种语音接口，包括专用 ASR 模型、音频能力 LLM、上下文偏置以及基于 LLM 的本体修复。由于长程计算机任务执行昂贵且随机，提出免执行、任务条件化的评估框架，将原始任务评分器投影到提示可处理的意图上，衡量语音接口后任务相关信息的保留程度。

Result: 在 WildClawBench 上，其免执行原生投影提供了与实际执行相关的语音接口质量度量，与下游任务完成情况相关，并在 32 小时真实人类语音上比 WER/CER 的 Pearson 相关性提升 0.246。

Conclusion: Talk2Agent 为语音接口到 LLM 计算机操作代理的信息保真度提供了可实际使用的评测方式，表明免执行投影指标优于传统 ASR 指标，能更好预测下游任务完成。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) computer-use agents are typically evaluated with clean written instructions, despite speech being an increasingly popular interface for interacting with such systems. Speech input introduces an additional failure point: transcription errors can alter task-critical entities, constraints, or targets before the agent begins reasoning, while conventional ASR metrics do not directly measure whether the information required for successful execution has been preserved. We introduce Talk2Agent, a benchmark for evaluating how effectively voice interfaces convey human-spoken instructions to LLM-based computer-use agents. Talk2Agent builds human-spoken versions of tasks from WildClawBench and OSWorld and evaluates a range of voice interfaces, including dedicated ASR models, audio-capable LLMs, contextual biasing, and LLM-based ontology repair. Because repeatedly executing long-horizon computer-use tasks is costly and stochastic, we further propose an execution-free, task-conditioned evaluation framework that projects the original task grader onto prompt-addressable intentions and measures how much task-relevant information is retained after the voice interface. On WildClawBench, Talk2Agent's execution-free native projection provides a practical, execution-grounded measure of voice-interface quality, correlating with downstream task completion and improving Pearson correlation by 0.246 over WER/CER on 32 hours of real human speech.

</details>


### [183] [Reasoning Externalization for Faithful Large Language Model Narratives of Stock Return Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38869)
*Sujung Kim,Seung Hwan Cho,Sangjin Park,Young-Min Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个LLM叙事框架，将时序SHAP证据与历史状态类比相结合，用于横截面股票收益预测的可解释叙事生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融领域中机器学习预测的可解释性至关重要，但可解释AI输出的数值结果对非专家而言难以理解。虽然大语言模型可将这些数值输出转化为自然语言，但在推断数值变化和特征关系时容易产生错误。

Method: 提出一个面向横截面股票收益预测的LLM叙事框架，结合时序SHAP证据与历史状态类比。时序证据追踪XGBoost模型归一化全局SHAP重要性在六个月内的变化；历史类比为SHAP重要性变化相似的过往时期，并提供其模型表现与后续市场收益作为对比背景。开展受控研究，逐步外化推理过程：依次提供原始SHAP序列、确定性时序描述符和特征关系，并对每条生成主张进行来源链接式证据校验。在Qwen3系列模型上进行评估。

Result: 外化数值与关系推理提升了证据忠实度以及时序与关系准确性。Qwen3-32B-Instruct的证据忠实度从0.696提升至0.996。历史类比虽未提升结构化自动忠实度，但在人工评分中获得了更高的有用性评分。

Conclusion: 外化可验证的推理过程能够增强叙事的忠实度，而历史背景信息则能增加解释价值。

Abstract: In finance, interpreting machine learning predictions is essential, yet the numerical outputs of explainable AI can be difficult for non-experts to understand. While large language models (LLMs) can translate these outputs into natural language, they may produce errors when inferring numerical changes and feature relations. We propose an LLM narrative framework for cross-sectional stock return prediction that combines temporal Shapley additive explanations (SHAP) evidence with historical regime analogs. Temporal evidence tracks changes in the normalized global SHAP importance of an XGBoost model over six months. Historical analogs are past periods with similar changes in SHAP importance, their model performance and subsequent market returns are provided as comparative context. Using this framework, we conduct a controlled study of progressive reasoning externalization, sequentially providing raw SHAP sequences, deterministic temporal descriptors, and feature relations. Each generated claim is verified against provenance-linked evidence. Across Qwen3, externalizing numerical and relational reasoning improved evidence faithfulness as well as temporal and relational accuracy. Evidence faithfulness increased from 0.696 to 0.996 for Qwen3-32B-Instruct. While historical analogs did not improve structured automatic faithfulness, they received higher human-rated usefulness scores. These results suggest that externalizing verifiable reasoning enhances narrative faithfulness and that historical context adds interpretive value.

</details>


### [184] [STRATA: Self-Learning Through Role-Aligned Tiered Agents for Real-Time Strategy Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38881)
*Xinhe Tian,Xiaoyue Zhang,Ziyou Zhang,Jiacheng Li,Xiaoqiang Jin,Qianchuan Zhao,Gaochen Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出STRATA，一个面向《红色警戒》的角色对齐分层系统，通过跨对局自学习生成经验卡片，将胜率从30%提升至100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将大语言模型用于RTS游戏指挥决策的研究存在两个问题：一是推理延迟高，容易错过关键战术事件；二是完整RTS对局复杂、战术多样，系统高度依赖人工编写的经验提示，难以从过往对局中持续学习。

Method: STRATA采用角色对齐的分层架构，将游戏内决策分派给三个智能体：战略智能体（SA）基于全局状态与相关经验卡片生成高层指令，后勤智能体（LA）与战术智能体（TA）分别负责后勤与战术执行。每局结束后，复盘智能体（RA）从对局轨迹中提取候选经验，利用后续对局证据进行验证与修正，并将跨多局可复用的战略经验压缩为简洁的经验卡片供SA检索。

Result: 通过经验卡片形成、学习前后完整对局对比、以及针对不同打法AI对手的经验学习三类实验进行评估。在固定场景下，使用学习到的经验卡片将观测胜率从30%提升到100%；针对不同风格AI对手的顺序学习也产生了各自不同的长期战略经验。

Conclusion: 分层角色对齐与跨对局自学习经验机制能有效缓解LLM在RTS中的延迟与经验依赖问题，显著提升决策表现，并针对不同对手风格形成差异化长期策略。

Abstract: Real-time strategy (RTS) games require agents to coordinate economic development, production and construction, base defense, unit organization, and attack timing over long matches. Existing studies have applied large language models to command decision-making in RTS games, enabling agents to read textual game states and generate high-level plans. However, long inference latency can cause them to miss critical tactical events. The complexity and tactical diversity of full RTS matches also leave existing systems heavily dependent on manually written experience-based prompts, with limited ability to learn continuously from past games. We present STRATA, a role-aligned hierarchical system with cross-game self-learning for Red Alert. STRATA assigns in-game strategic, logistical, and tactical decisions to a Strategic Agent (SA), Logistics Agent (LA), and Tactical Agent (TA), respectively. The SA generates high-level directives based on the global game state and relevant experience cards, while the LA and TA handle logistics and tactical execution. After each match, a Review Agent (RA) derives candidate experience from game traces, validates and revises it using evidence from subsequent matches, and compresses strategic experience supported across multiple games into concise experience cards for SA retrieval. We evaluate STRATA through the formation of experience cards, full-match comparisons before and after learning, and experience learning against AI opponents with different play styles. Under a fixed scenario, using the learned experience cards increases the observed win rate from 30% to 100%. Sequential learning against AI opponents with different play styles also produces distinct long-term strategic experience.

</details>


### [185] [Consistent Plan-Act for Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38891)
*Heng-Zhuang Li,Yi-Kai Zhang,Yu Wang,Yueqing Sun,Jiayuan Zhang,Qi Gu,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现长时程智能体任务中规划者与执行者存在系统性的"状态不一致"（planner-actor state mismatch），据此提出 ConPAct 方法，通过返回矛盾反馈并在一致交互上微调来提升协调与任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体任务需要强推理与高效执行，常见做法是将高层规划与低层执行解耦为规划者（planner）和执行者（actor）两种角色。但二者的协调失败问题缺乏系统研究，作者希望探究这种失败的根源。

Method: 通过提示两个智能体输出结构化状态断言，并以程序化方式比对二者报告，从而检测显式矛盾。基于该分析提出 ConPAct：将检测到的矛盾反馈给双方智能体，形成一致的状态解释，并在筛选出的一致交互数据上微调模型，以改善协调。同时考察向智能体提供任务相关状态信息的效果。

Result: 分析揭示双方对同一任务相关状态事实存在系统性分歧，即"规划者-执行者状态不一致"现象；提供任务相关状态信息可减少不一致并改善协调与任务表现。ConPAct 在多种环境和模型配置下提升性能，例如以 GPT-5.6-sol/terra 分别作为规划者与执行者时，MiniGrid 成功率从 38.6% 提升至 54.4%。

Conclusion: 状态一致性既可指导推理时的纠错，也可用于协调能力的训练，为长时程智能体任务的规划-执行协调提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: Long-horizon agentic tasks demand strong reasoning and efficient execution across successive interactions with dynamic environments. A common approach decouples high-level planning from low-level execution through separate planner and actor roles. To investigate coordination failures in these tasks, we prompt both agents for structured state assertions and compare their reports programmatically to detect explicit contradictions. Our analyses reveal systematic disagreement about the same task-relevant state facts, a phenomenon we term planner-actor state mismatch. We further find that providing agents with task-relevant state information reduces mismatch and improves coordination and task performance. Based on the systematic analysis of the state mismatch, we propose Consistent Plan-Act (ConPAct), which feeds detected contradictions back to both agents to form consistent state interpretations and fine-tunes them on curated consistent interactions for better coordination. ConPAct improves performance across various environments and model configurations, e.g., increasing MiniGrid success rate from 38.6% to 54.4% with GPT-5.6-sol/terra as planner and actor respectively, demonstrating that state consistency can guide both inference-time correction and coordination training.

</details>


### [186] [Composing Task-specific Agent Harnesses at Test Time with Reusable Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38912)
*Peng Kuang,Haibo Jin,Dehao Wu,Feiyang Deng,Xiaopeng Yuan,Jerry Wang,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Harness Primitives与STITCH框架，通过复用可组合的harness原语在测试时组装任务专用harness，避免从头生成代码，任务成功率最高提升12分，测试时开销仅2.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent harness决定LLM如何收集上下文、调用工具、验证结果、保存状态与终止，直接影响性能。但不同任务对机制的需求各异：某机制在一个任务上有效，在另一任务上可能带来开销或上下文干扰，因此全局统一的harness存在次优性。这种次优性源于固定机制选择带来的"错配"，从而需要任务专用的harness构建。然而为每个任务生成harness代码会带来生成与调试成本，机制越多执行风险越易累积。

Method: 作者从失败的任务轨迹中挖掘出"Harness Primitives"，即具有明确适用范围与组合契约的可复用harness机制。基于此提出STITCH框架：根据任务信息选（Select）合适的原语，并在测试时将其编译（compile）为任务专用harness。该"选择—编译"的分离设计使得无需在测试时生成或修复机制代码即可获得任务专用harness。实验与固定harness基线、人工设计的harness（如Codex CLI）进行对比，并考察原语库规模的影响。

Result: STITCH提升了harness的适应性与鲁棒性，并随原语库规模扩大而性能提升。相比固定harness基线，任务成功率最高提升12个百分点，超过Codex CLI等人工设计harness；测试时harness组装开销仅2.7%，比从头生成任务专用harness高效638倍。

Conclusion: 构建任务自适应harness有助于完成多样任务，而构建可复用原语是实现该目标的一条有前景的路径。

Abstract: Agent harnesses govern how large language models (LLMs) gather context, invoke tools, verify results, preserve state, and terminate, largely affecting agent performance. However, the value of each harness mechanism can differ across heterogeneous tasks: a mechanism that improves one task may impose overhead or context distraction on another, leading to the suboptimality of a global harness. We characterize this suboptimality as a mismatch induced by fixed mechanism choices, motivating task-specific harness construction. Nonetheless, generating harness code for each task introduces generation and debugging costs, with execution risks that can compound as more mechanisms are generated. To address those challenges, we introduce Harness Primitives, reusable harness mechanisms with clear application scope and composition contract mined from failed task trajectories. Based on Harness Primitives, we propose STITCH, a framework that Selects suitable primitives given Task Information and compiles them into Task-speCific Harnesses at test time. This separation enables task-specific harnesses without generating or repairing mechanism code at test time. Extensive experiments demonstrate that STITCH not only improves harness adaptability and robustness, but also scales with the primitive library size, boosting task success rates by up to 12 points over fixed harness baselines, surpassing human-designed harnesses like Codex CLI while maintaining a minimal test-time harness composition overhead of only 2.7%, 638 times more efficient than generating task-specific harnesses from scratch. Ultimately, our work demonstrates that building task-adaptive harnesses can be beneficial for completing diverse tasks and that building reusable primitives can be a promising path towards this goal.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [Travel Time Prediction in Supply Chain Management Using Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38190)
*Balaji Venkateswaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究旨在利用机器学习与深度学习方法，基于历史数据构建精确的运输时间预测模型，以提升供应链物流的规划与绩效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 供应链生态系统复杂，高度依赖原材料与成品的运输和物流。准确的运输时间估算对提升物流一致性与绩效至关重要，有助于规划、需求预测、提前期管理和装配计划；同时配送环节直接影响客户满意度。因此需要找到合适的数据与方法来实现精准预测。

Method: 收集大量历史数据，采用机器学习与深度学习技术，构建用于预测库存/货物运输时间的模型。

Result: 构建了一个能够较准确预测运输时间的模型，可用于支持供应链的规划与决策。（摘要未给出具体量化指标）

Conclusion: 机器学习与深度学习方法可有效用于供应链运输时间预测，从而改善物流一致性、绩效及客户体验。

Abstract: The purpose of this research is to find data and methods using machine learning and deep learning to correctly predict the estimated travel time for transportation and logistics in a supply chain system. The supply chain ecosystem is very complex and heavily relies on the transportation and logistics of raw materials and finished goods. Accurate travel time estimation is critical because it helps supply chain members to improve logistics consistency and performance. This helps in planning, demand forecasting, lead time management and assembly planning. The logistics on the delivery side of the customer also plays a crucial role in customer satisfaction and voice of customer. With the collection of huge historical data and using novel techniques, the research builds an accurate model to predict travel time of inventory.

</details>


### [188] [EHR2Trace: Auditable EHR Data Infrastructure for Patient World Models and Clinical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38193)
*Xinye Yang,Yuli Wang,Cheng Ting Lin,Harrison Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EHR2Trace 将异构电子健康记录转换为可追溯的患者事件流，支持患者世界模型与临床智能体的训练与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者世界模型与临床智能体需要可靠的患者病情、治疗及决策时可用信息的历史，但 EHR 事件记录方式不统一，难以一致使用。

Method: EHR2Trace 将不同来源的 EHR 转换为可追溯患者事件，链接事件与源记录，区分事件时间与信息可得时间，区分用药医嘱、发药与给药，并以共享事件表示支持 OMOP 与 MEDS 导出，含自动校验与可复现构建。

Result: 在三个临床数据集上转换 8.464 亿事件，除 MIMIC-IV 一项单位一致性检查外所有适用检查均通过，并检出全部 28 个注入故障；受控预测实验显示，将较晚诊断归到入院时间会显著虚高测量性能，且此类数据训练的模型在按信息可得性过滤的历史上部署时精度下降。

Conclusion: EHR2Trace 为患者世界模型与临床智能体提供可复用的数据基础，帮助研究者审视患者历史、核查转换决策并以明确数据规则评估模型。

Abstract: Patient world models and clinical agents aim to predict changes in patients' health and support clinical work. Developing these systems requires reliable histories of patient conditions, treatments, and the information available at each decision. Electronic health records (EHRs) contain these histories, but differences in how events are recorded make them difficult to use consistently. We present EHR2Trace, a system that converts EHRs from different sources into traceable patient events for model training and evaluation. It links events to source records, separates event time from information availability, and distinguishes medication orders, dispensing, and administration. A shared event representation supports both OMOP and MEDS exports, with automated validation and reproducible builds. Across three clinical datasets, EHR2Trace converted 846.4 million events, with every applicable check passing except one unit-consistency check on MIMIC-IV, and detected all 28 injected faults. A controlled prediction experiment showed that assigning later diagnoses to admission time substantially inflated measured performance, and that a model trained on such data lost accuracy when deployed on histories filtered by availability. EHR2Trace provides a reusable data foundation for patient world models and clinical agents, helping researchers inspect patient histories, check conversion decisions, and evaluate models with explicit data rules.

</details>


### [189] [A Moving-Horizon Approximate Branch-and-Reduce Method for Deep Classification Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38194)
*Chenxuanyin Zou,Jiayang Ren,Qiangqiang Mao,Jing Liu,Marcus Lai,Yankai Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出移动视界近似分支归约方法，在大规模连续特征数据上训练近最优深层决策树，兼顾精度与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树对可解释性很重要，但存在严重可扩展性问题：现有全局最优方法受限于二值特征选择与较浅树深，传统启发式方法又常牺牲预测精度。因此需要一种能在大规模数据、连续特征与深层结构下兼顾最优性与效率的训练方法。

Method: 构建层次化根-子树优化框架：根层问题用分支归约求解，诱导子树问题用贪心启发式近似；该近似相当于强化学习中的前瞻式 rollout，可保证全局最优性的框架下大幅提升深层结构效率；再用低成本移动视界策略迭代精炼模型精度。

Result: 大量数值实验表明，该方法在测试精度上超过现有启发式基线，并在数据集规模和树深两方面比全局最优求解器具有显著更好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提移动视界近似分支归约方法能在大规模连续特征数据上训练近最优深层分类树，实现了优于启发式方法的精度和优于全局最优求解器的可扩展性。

Abstract: Despite the importance for interpretability, decision trees face severe scalability challenges. Existing global optimal methods are often limited by binary feature selection and shallow tree depths, whereas traditional heuristic approaches frequently sacrifice predictive accuracy. To overcome these limitations, this paper proposes a moving-horizon approximate branch-and-reduce method to train near-optimal deep classification trees on large-scale datasets with continuous features. Built on a hierarchical root-subtree optimization framework, the method solves the root-level problem via branch-and-reduce while approximating the induced subtree problem using greedy heuristics. Although the underlying framework is capable of guaranteeing global optimality, the approximation, which functions as a lookahead rollout in a reinforcement learning context, significantly boosts efficiency for deeper structures. A low-cost moving-horizon strategy is then employed to iteratively refine model accuracy. Extensive numerical results demonstrate that our method exceeds the testing accuracy of existing heuristic baselines while offering significantly greater scalability, in terms of both dataset size and tree depth, than global optimal solvers.

</details>


### [190] [A Data-Free Physics-Informed Neural Operator for Level-Set Interface Advection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38195)
*Muhammad Akbar Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无数据的物理信息神经算子，用于水平集界面平流，无需参考解，仅凭输运残差与几何约束训练，性能接近监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有界面问题算子依赖其拟替代的求解器产生的参考解进行训练，成本高且受限于已有数值解。作者希望构建完全不依赖参考解的数据自由方法，并以监督与混合算子作为基线，量化"拒绝标签"所带来的代价。

Method: 面向水平集界面平流，将界面本身视为方程未知量，算子把初始界面映射为指定流场下的完整时空轨迹。训练目标仅含输运残差与几何（eikonal）约束，不含任何参考解。采用时空傅里叶骨干网络，一次性输出整条轨迹；初始条件以构造方式而非惩罚项施加，从而避免在无解数据时锚定项与残差项之间的竞争。监督、混合与无数据算子在同一架构、族、预算与测试集下训练对比。

Result: 在反向单涡算例上，无数据算子对100个留出初始界面的相对L2误差为1.614±0.067%，监督基线为0.369±0.035%，相差4.4倍；在刚体旋转算例上分别为3.804±1.075%与2.576±0.159%，相差1.5倍。两基准对两种方法的排序不同，归因于eikonal约束：精确解在旋转下仅0.3%区域违反|grad phi|=1，而在涡下高达86.9%。在约束有效的区域，物理训练算子尽管场误差更大，其封闭面积守恒比监督基线好2.7倍；使用8个参考解的混合方案优于使用16个参考解的监督方案。

Conclusion: 无数据物理信息神经算子可在不使用任何参考解的前提下实现水平集界面平流的高精度建模，其精度损失随eikonal约束的适用程度而变化；在约束成立时甚至能更好地保持几何守恒量，混合少量参考解即可超越纯监督方案。

Abstract: Operators for interfacial problems are trained on reference solutions produced by the solver they are intended to replace. This work develops a data-free physics-informed neural operator for level-set interface advection, in which the interface is the equation's unknown and the operator maps an initial interface to the full spatiotemporal trajectory under a prescribed flow. Training uses only the transport residual and a geometric constraint; no reference solution enters the objective at any point. A spacetime Fourier backbone emits the entire trajectory in one pass, and the initial condition is imposed by construction rather than by penalty, which removes the competition between the anchoring term and the residual that otherwise arises when no solution data are available. Supervised and hybrid operators are trained under an identical architecture, family, budget and test set, and are reported throughout as baselines that quantify what refusing labels costs. On a reversed single vortex the data-free operator reaches 1.614 +/- 0.067% relative L2 error on 100 held-out initial interfaces against 0.369 +/- 0.035% for the supervised baseline, a factor of 4.4; on solid-body rotation the corresponding figures are 3.804 +/- 1.075% and 2.576 +/- 0.159%, a factor of 1.5. The two benchmarks rank the arms differently, and the difference is attributable to the eikonal constraint: the exact solution violates |grad phi| = 1 over 0.3% of the domain under rotation and 86.9% under the vortex. Where the constraint is valid the physics-trained operator conserves enclosed area 2.7 times better than the supervised baseline despite a larger field error, and a hybrid arm using eight reference solutions outperforms a supervised arm using sixteen.

</details>


### [191] [Conformal Adversarial Generative Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38196)
*Ahmad Shahi,Mamehgol Yousefi,Brendon J. Woodford,Farhaan Mirza,Tapabrata Chakraborti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAGE框架，结合生成建模、对抗判别与共形预测，动态调整权重以提升时间序列预测的鲁棒性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集成方法易受极端预测值的过度影响，导致预测可靠性下降，需一种能抑制不可靠预测的机制。

Method: CAGE利用多个生成模型产生初始预测，再由基于共形预测的判别组件评估；通过非一致性得分得到P值，动态调整模型权重，仅让可信预测进入最终集成输出。

Result: 在新西兰牛奶收集数据与公开owid-monkeypox全球健康数据上，CAGE优于传统集成方法，尤其在处理离群点与噪声数据时表现更好。

Conclusion: 引入共形预测使CAGE能提供准确且统计严谨的预测，增强决策能力；该方法具通用性，可应用于金融、天气与供应链管理等领域。

Abstract: Accurate time series forecasting is critical across various domains, yet traditional ensemble methods often suffer from the disproportionate influence of extreme forecasts. We introduce the Conformal Adversarial Generative Ensemble (CAGE), a novel framework that combines generative modeling, adversarial discrimination, and conformal prediction to enhance forecast reliability and accuracy. CAGE employs multiple generative models to produce initial forecasts, which are then evaluated by a discriminative component using conformal prediction techniques. P-values derived from nonconformity scores help dynamically adjust model weights, minimizing the impact of unreliable forecasts. This approach ensures that only the most credible predictions contribute to the final ensemble output. Our empirical and statistical analyses of time series data from New Zealand's milk collection and the global health data from the public owid-monkeypox dataset show that the CAGE outperforms traditional ensemble methods, especially in handling outliers and noisy data. By incorporating conformal prediction, CAGE delivers accurate and statistically rigorous forecasts, enhancing decision-making. We have demonstrated performance on two different datasets deliberately to showcase that the proposed method offers a versatile solution potentially applicable across finance, weather, and supply chain management.

</details>


### [192] [DualCast: A Dual-Path Language Model for Bimodal Financial Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38197)
*Wentao Zhao,Hongqiang Wu,Shanghang Liu,Zhaochen Zan,Yu Zhang,Biqing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DualCast 是一个双路径金融时间序列预测框架：快速路径在冻结的 Qwen3-8B 上训练离散金融词汇的嵌入与输出头，慢速路径通过可切换 LoRA 适配器结合新闻进行预测修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列预测需同时捕捉异质资产的价格动态并融入预测时点可得的新闻信息，而现有方法难以兼顾数值动态与文本信息。

Method: 将每个对数收益片段表示为可学习摘要 token 加三个残差形状 token；提出自适应频率均衡残差向量量化以提升码本利用率；快路径仅训练新金融 token 嵌入与输出头，慢路径用 LoRA 适配器以快路径预测和新闻为条件，经监督微调初始化后用收益空间组相对策略优化（GRPO）奖励相对快路径的改进。

Result: 在股票与能源价格、5分钟/日/周三种分辨率的零样本评测中，慢路径在 12 个数据集-预测步长设定中的 8 个取得最低 MAPE，包括所有最长步长设定；新闻消融在多数设定带来额外增益，幅度因市场而异。

Conclusion: DualCast 将快速数值预测器与可选的文本条件修正机制相结合，有效提升金融时间序列预测精度。

Abstract: Financial time-series forecasting must capture price dynamics across heterogeneous assets while incorporating news available at prediction time. We introduce DualCast, a dual-path framework that extends a frozen language model with a discrete financial vocabulary. Each log-return patch is represented by a learned summary token and three residual shape tokens, preserving local drift and volatility while allowing shape patterns to be shared across assets. To improve codebook utilization, we develop adaptive frequency-equalizing residual vector quantization, which rebalances overloaded codewords without compromising reconstruction accuracy. The fast path trains only the new financial-token embeddings and output heads on a frozen Qwen3-8B backbone. A toggleable LoRA adapter enables a slow path that conditions on the fast forecast and news available at the forecast origin to produce a revised prediction. The reviser is initialized by supervised fine-tuning and further optimized with a return-space group relative policy optimization objective that rewards improvements over the fast forecast. In zero-shot evaluations covering equities and energy prices at five-minute, daily, and weekly resolutions, the slow path achieves the lowest mean absolute percentage error among the compared methods in 8 of 12 dataset-horizon settings, including every longest-horizon setting. News ablations indicate additional gains in most tested settings, although their magnitude varies across markets. DualCast thus combines a fast numerical forecaster with an optional text-conditioned revision mechanism.

</details>


### [193] [FlashDiffusion: Fused Tiled Kernel Spectral Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38198)
*Julio Candanedo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FlashDiffusion，一种无矩阵（matrix-free）的扩散映射谱学习方法，通过 GPU 分块计算稠密高斯核并结合经验 β-flow 选择分辨率尺度，避免 O(N²) 内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散映射与核方法可为几何学习提供可解释的非线性谱表示基，但在几何极限（小带宽）下相关矩阵趋于高秩，若直接物化稠密高斯核需要 O(N²) 内存，难以扩展到大规模数据。

Method: 提出 FlashDiffusion 的 matrix-free 方法：在融合的 GPU tile 上评估稠密高斯核块，无需物化完整核矩阵；将特征求解器与经验 β-flow 耦合以选择有限样本的分辨率尺度；并在样本量与带宽上做连续化（continuation），用较粗分辨率热启动（warm-start）越来越昂贵的谱求解。

Result: （原文摘要未给出具体实验数据与量化结果，主要贡献为方法层面：避免 O(N²) 内存、提升大规模谱求解效率。）

Conclusion: 通过无矩阵 GPU 分块计算与 β-flow 自适应尺度选择的结合，可在不牺牲扩散映射谱表示能力的前提下，缓解稠密核方法的内存与计算瓶颈。

Abstract: Diffusion maps, and kernel methods more generally, provide an interpretable nonlinear spectral representation basis for geometric learning. In the geometric limit, small bandwidth, these matrices tend to be high rank and thus require materializing dense Gaussian kernels requires $O(N^2)$ memory. We introduce FlashDiffusion, a matrix-free method that evaluates dense Gaussian kernel blocks in fused GPU tiles and couples the eigensolver to an empirical $β$-flow that selects the finite-sample resolution scale. A continuation over sample size and bandwidth warm-starts increasingly expensive spectral solves from coarser resolutions.

</details>


### [194] [Kinematic signatures of impairment: Detecting alcohol intoxication in e-scooter riders using sensor data and machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38276)
*Rahul Rajendra Pai,Marco Dozza,Alexander Rasch,Ali Mohammadi,Marco Capuccini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过仪器化电动滑板车与熵信号分析，可靠区分清醒与醉酒骑行状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 醉酒是电动滑板车致命与重伤事故的重要成因，而现有对策（如时段限制、骑行前认知筛查）无法实时、连续评估骑行者的身体运动控制或受损程度。

Method: 开展对照实验，25名参与者在清醒及两个目标血液酒精浓度（0.05%和0.08%）下骑行装有传感器的电动滑板车。设备包括六轴IMU及油门、刹车杆位置传感器，采样率100 Hz。计算两类互补信号特征：归一化排列熵（时间复杂性）与标准差（信号幅度）。采用重复测量相关分析，并用基于熵的多分类逻辑回归分类器，通过留一参与者交叉验证进行评估。

Result: 识别出七个运动学特征（均为IMU与油门信号），其熵随醉酒程度增加而下降（p < 0.001），标准差上升（p < 0.01），表明醉酒骑行者从连续、低幅度的微修正转向更少、高幅度的反应性修正。分类器总体准确率达85%，加权一对多AuROC为0.94，清醒对高醉度AuROC为1.00。转向速率与横向加速度是最重要的预测特征，说明酒精引发侧向平衡的明显崩溃。

Conclusion: 车载运动学传感结合基于熵的信号分析可可靠区分清醒与醉酒电动滑板车骑行，为自动醉酒检测系统奠定基础，同时保障清醒骑行者的出行便利。

Abstract: Alcohol intoxication is a leading contributor to fatal and severe-injured e-scooterist crashes. Current countermeasures, such as temporal restrictions or pre-ride cognitive screening, cannot continuously assess an e-scooterist's physical motor control or impairment in real time. We conducted a controlled experiment in which 25 participants rode an instrumented e-scooter through a test track while sober and at two targeted blood alcohol concentration levels (0.05% and 0.08%). The e-scooter was instrumented with a six-axis inertial measurement unit (IMU), and throttle and brake lever position sensors, all sampled at 100 Hz. Two complementary signal features were computed: normalised permutation entropy, which quantifies temporal complexity, and standard deviation, which quantifies signal amplitude. Repeated measures correlation identified seven kinematic features (all IMU and throttle signals) whose entropy decreased (p < 0.001) while standard deviation increased (p < 0.01) with increasing intoxication, indicating that intoxicated riders shift from continuous, low-amplitude micro-corrections to fewer, high-amplitude reactive corrections. An entropy based multi-class logistic regression classifier, evaluated through leave-one-participant-out cross-validation, achieved 85% overall accuracy and a weighted one-vs-rest area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AuROC) of 0.94, with a sober-vs-high AuROC of 1.00. Steering rate and lateral acceleration were the most important predictive features, indicating that alcohol induces a distinct collapse in lateral equilibrium during riding. Ultimately, these results demonstrate that onboard kinematic sensing combined with entropy-based signal analysis can reliably distinguish sober from intoxicated e-scooter riding, providing a foundation for automatic intoxication detection systems that preserve mobility for sober riders.

</details>


### [195] [Hermes: Learning Contextual Reasoning Unlocks Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38332)
*Xinyu Li,Mononito Goswami,Hao Liu,Nikos Kanakaris,Langlin Huang,Prithwish Jana,Patrick Blöbaum,Purak Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Hermes 系列可配置调用框架与 Hermes-Learn 两阶段训练框架，将多上下文窗口推理中的上下文分配与信息复用决策权交给模型，从而实现"上下文推理"能力并有效利用测试时算力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展（test-time scaling）通过增加推理阶段算力提升模型表现，但当推理需要跨多个上下文窗口时，必须决定如何分配新上下文、在窗口之间传递哪些信息。现有方法大多由外部框架（harness）预先规定这些决策，限制了模型的自主性与泛化能力，因此作者希望把决策权从框架转移到模型本身。

Method: 论文引入两部分工作：(1) Hermes，一族简单且可配置的调用框架，能够逐步改变模型对上下文分配与复用的控制程度；(2) Hermes-Learn，一个两阶段学习框架，用于训练模型掌握上述"上下文推理"能力。

Result: 实验发现，能力较强的大模型能够利用这种灵活性，随推理算力增加而获得性能提升；而较小的开源模型起初难以做到。使用 Hermes-Learn 训练后，小模型能够弥补这一差距，并学会随问题和推理进展而变化的自适应上下文推理策略。

Conclusion: 这些收益可跨基准与模型泛化，可外推到训练时未见过的推理算力规模，并能迁移到 Hermes 之外的互补测试时扩展方法，表明将上下文决策权交给模型并结合针对性训练是有效方向。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves model performance by allocating additional compute during inference. Using this compute effectively across multiple context windows requires deciding how to allocate fresh contexts and what information to carry between them. We call a model's ability to make these decisions contextual reasoning. Existing approaches largely prescribe these decisions through their harness; we instead shift them to the model. We introduce 1) Hermes, a family of simple, configurable harnesses that progressively varies model control over context allocation and reuse, and 2) Hermes-Learn, a two-stage framework for learning these capabilities. We find that capable models can exploit this flexibility to scale with additional inference-time compute, while smaller open-source models initially struggle to do so. Training with Hermes-Learn closes this gap, inducing adaptive contextual reasoning strategies that vary with both the problem and the progress of reasoning. These gains generalize across benchmarks and models, extrapolate beyond the inference-time compute seen during training, and transfer to complementary test-time scaling methods beyond Hermes.

</details>


### [196] [Activation-Conditioned Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38342)
*Zhexi Lu,Subhajit Chaudhury,Tejaswini Pedapati,Keerthiram Murugesan,Lei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出激活条件自蒸馏（ACSD）：用正确轨迹与其余轨迹的激活差异构造引导向量，由冻结基座模型在每步预测施加，实现无需参考文本与教师更新的自蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy自蒸馏常用参考解条件提供密集监督，但仅提供特权信息并不足以保证长响应中每个token层面监督都有效。

Method: 对比在生成预算内达到验证正确答案的自生成轨迹与其余轨迹的激活，提取引导向量；冻结的基座模型在每个预测位置施加该向量，学生在自己生成的prefix上学习其next-token分布；用结果验证构造与校准方向，蒸馏不需问题特定参考文本，也不更新教师参数；推理时仅用蒸馏后的学生模型。

Result: 在五个模型、四个数学基准上平均准确率最高；DeepSeek-R1-0528-Qwen3-8B上数学平均准确率71.9%、LiveCodeBench v6 pass@12达70.9%，优于参考条件OPSD的69.0%和66.3%；正确轨迹之间的对比也可支持蒸馏，提取方向可跨数学训练数据集复用；固定学生轨迹下，ACSD的后期位置logit更新幅度比OPSD更稳定。

Conclusion: ACSD通过激活级方向条件替代参考文本条件，在多个模型与基准上稳定优于OPSD基线，且方向可复用、后期监督更平稳。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation uses a model as its own teacher to provide dense supervision for reasoning, often through reference-solution conditioning. Providing privileged information does not by itself ensure effective token-level supervision throughout long responses. We introduce Activation-Conditioned Self-Distillation (ACSD), which extracts a steering vector by contrasting activations of self-generated trajectories that reach verified correct answers within a generation budget with those of all remaining trajectories. A frozen copy of the base model applies this vector at each prediction position, and the student learns from its next-token distributions on student-generated prefixes. Outcome verification is used for direction construction and calibration; distillation requires neither problem-specific reference text nor teacher parameter updates. The distilled student is used alone at inference. On each of five models, ACSD achieves the highest mean accuracy over four mathematical benchmarks among the evaluated methods. On DeepSeek-R1-0528-Qwen3-8B, mean mathematical accuracy reaches 71.9\% and LiveCodeBench v6 pass@12 reaches 70.9\%, compared with 69.0\% and 66.3\% for the reference-conditioned OPSD baseline. Contrasts among correct trajectories also support distillation, and extracted directions can be reused across mathematical training datasets. On fixed student trajectories, ACSD maintains more stable late-position logit-update magnitudes than OPSD.

</details>


### [197] [Function-Space Transformer with Adaptive Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38348)
*Guorui Sang,Pedram Rooshenas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Function-Space Transformer (FST)，一种基于空间自适应连续隐表示的函数学习框架，在PDE求解和图像分类任务上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多数据（物理场、几何形状、视觉信号）本质上由连续域上的函数描述，却只能通过离散采样观测。固定均匀网格表示面临局部变化分辨率与全域计算量之间的权衡，而现有隐注意力架构缺乏显式空间组织，无法自适应每个输入的空间结构。

Method: FST将特征存储在由输入观测预测位置的锚点上，通过函数空间交互递归细化锚点特征，使表示的空间组织随每个输入自适应调整，而非继承观测网格，同时支持空间分辨输出与有限维输出。

Result: 在PDEBench Burgers与Darcy流的PDE求解任务上显著优于Perceiver IO基线，与Fourier Neural Operator竞争力强；在ImageNet-1K上以更少参数取得高于Vision Transformer的分类精度；消融实验验证了函数空间更新与递归细化的有效性。

Conclusion: 自适应连续表示在科学预测与视觉识别中均展现出潜力。

Abstract: Many forms of data, including physical fields, geometric shapes, and visual signals, are naturally described by functions over continuous domains but are observed through discrete samples. Representing these functions on fixed uniform grids imposes a trade-off between resolving localized variation and increasing computation across the domain. Neural operators address this mismatch by learning mappings between functions, while latent-attention architectures provide flexible processing of sampled observations. We introduce the Function-Space Transformer (FST), a framework for learning from functions through a spatially adaptive continuous latent representation. FST stores features at anchors whose locations are predicted from the input observations and recursively refines these anchor features through function-space interactions. This allows the representation to adapt its spatial organization to each input rather than inherit that of the observation grid, while supporting both spatially resolved and finite-dimensional outputs. On PDE solution prediction using PDEBench Burgers and Darcy flow, FST substantially outperforms the Perceiver IO baseline, whose latent representation lacks explicit spatial organization, and is highly competitive with the Fourier Neural Operator. On ImageNet-1K, FST achieves higher classification accuracy than the Vision Transformer baseline, with fewer parameters across these comparisons. Ablations further support the benefits of function-space updates and recursive refinement. Together, these results highlight the potential of adaptive continuous representations for both scientific prediction and visual recognition.

</details>


### [198] [MILO: Automated Harness Discovery via Orchestrated Multi-Agent Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38349)
*Prithwish Jana,Mononito Goswami,Hao Liu,Xinyu Li,Langlin Huang,Zhehui Huang,Zhishen Huang,Patrick Blöbaum,Anoop Deoras,Purak Jain,Nikos Kanakaris,Sahika Genc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MILO 是一个协同进化智能体执行框架（harness）与其搜索策略的元进化框架，在多个基准上超越现有框架与搜索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体系统由 AI 模型与 harness（控制执行与环境交互的组件）共同组成，harness 设计严重影响长程任务表现，但其组合搜索空间巨大，需大量人力且随模型更新需反复重做。现有自动化方法搜索范围窄（仅优化提示或技能等单一组件），且受限于固定、利用式的搜索策略。

Method: MILO（Meta-evolutionary Island Orchestration）同时进化 harness 与其发现策略，包含三部分：(i) 基于岛屿树的层级谱系记忆，将被拒绝的变异用作负面证据；(ii) 各岛屿的变异智能体，利用全局搜索历史与父代特定反馈重写完整 harness；(iii) 编排器通过谱系嫁接与物种形成、变异器重新分配与课程修订来自适应调整搜索。

Result: 在 Terminal-Bench 2.1、PaperBench 和 DeepSWE 上，MILO 发现的 harness 优于 8 个先进 harness 和 6 种搜索方法（使用 Opus 4.8 与 gpt-oss-120b）。用 Opus 4.8 时相较初始 harness 分别提升 +12.0%、+28.3%、+10.3%，而先前最佳搜索增益仅为 +4.5%、+18.3%、0%。Terminal-Bench 2.1 上达 86.1±2.0%，超过官方榜首（83.8±2.3%），且比初始 harness 少用 26% token。在 EinsteinArena 开放问题上改进了 Erdős 最小重叠（0.3808586→0.3808568）及第一、第三自相关不等式（1.50274365→1.50274360；1.45081→1.44889）的已知上界。

Conclusion: 协同进化 harness 与搜索策略能显著提升智能体长程表现，克服固定搜索策略的局限，并具备跨模型与跨任务的泛化能力，甚至可推进数学开放问题的界限。

Abstract: Modern agentic systems combine an AI model with a harness that controls execution and environmental interactions. Harness design strongly affects long-horizon performance, yet its combinatorial search space demands substantial human effort that must be repeated as models change. Existing automated methods explore this space narrowly, optimizing only components such as prompts or skills or becoming trapped by fixed, exploitative search strategies. We introduce MILO (Meta-evolutionary Island Orchestration), a framework that co-evolves agent harnesses and the strategy used to discover them. MILO combines: (i) hierarchical lineage memory over island-based trees, using rejected mutations as negative evidence; (ii) per-island mutator agents that rewrite complete harnesses using global search history and parent-specific feedback; and (iii) an orchestrator that adapts search through lineage grafting and speciation, mutator reassignment and curriculum revision. Across Terminal-Bench 2.1, PaperBench, and DeepSWE, MILO-discovered harnesses outperform eight state-of-the-art harnesses and six search methods using frontier (Opus 4.8) and open-weight (gpt-oss-120b) models. With Opus 4.8, MILO improves resolution over its initial harness by $+12.0\%$, $+28.3\%$, and $+10.3\%$, respectively, compared with best prior-search gains of $+4.5\%$, $+18.3\%$, and $0\%$. On Terminal-Bench 2.1, it achieves $86.1 \pm 2.0\%$, exceeding the official leaderboard's top entry ($83.8 \pm 2.3\%$) while using 26\% fewer tokens than its initial harness. On EinsteinArena open problems, MILO improves best-known upper bounds for Erdős minimum-overlap ($0.3808586 \to 0.3808568$) and the first and third autocorrelation inequalities ($1.50274365 \to 1.50274360$; $1.45081 \to 1.44889$).

</details>


### [199] [Continual Learning of Dynamical Systems in Recurrent Neural Networks through Recyclable Unit Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38356)
*Sima Hashemi,Daniel Durstewitz,Georgia Koppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Continually-Recyclable Unit-Gating (CRUG)方法，用于持续动态系统重建(cDSR)。该方法通过可微分门控和L0惩罚选择任务特定单元并回收未用单元，在固定容量网络中实现零遗忘和最优重建-容量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续动态系统重建要求在学习新系统的同时保留已学动态，但循环模型中即使微小的参数更新也会在长时间自主演绎中定性改变其行为，因此需要兼顾稳定性与容量的持续学习方法。

Method: 在完全可训练且可解释的Almost-Linear RNN (AL-RNN)上，基准测试了参数正则化、回放和参数隔离三类持续学习方法；提出CRUG，利用基于L0惩罚训练的可微分门控选择任务特定单元，回收未用单元用于后续任务，并通过有向连接让后续任务复用早期表示而不影响已提交单元的动力学。

Result: 参数隔离最能有效保留早期动态，但过多任务分配会迅速耗尽固定规模网络；CRUG在测试方法中实现了最强的重建-容量权衡，零遗忘，并能可靠学习非线性与混沌系统的异质序列；当任务底层动态相似时，前向迁移更明显且更有用。

Conclusion: CRUG在自主cDSR和序列认知任务中均表现出优势，说明紧凑分配与前向迁移相结合的单元门控机制能有效解决持续动态系统重建中的容量与遗忘问题。

Abstract: Dynamical Systems Reconstruction (DSR) aims to infer models from observed time series that reproduce a system's qualitative long-term behavior. Continual DSR (cDSR) requires learning new systems while preserving previously learned dynamics, yet even small parameter updates in recurrent models can qualitatively alter their behavior over long autonomous rollouts. We benchmark established continual learning (CL) methods spanning parameter regularization, replay, and parameter isolation on the fully trainable and interpretable Almost-Linear RNN (AL-RNN). Parameter isolation preserves earlier dynamics most effectively, but excessive task-specific allocations can rapidly exhaust a fixed-size network. We therefore introduce Continually-Recyclable Unit-Gating (CRUG), which conserves capacity through compact allocation and forward transfer. Differentiable gates trained with an $L_0$-based penalty select task-specific units, while unused units are recycled for subsequent tasks. Directed connections allow later tasks to reuse earlier representations without affecting the dynamics of previously committed units. CRUG achieves the strongest reconstruction--capacity trade-off among the tested methods with zero forgetting and reliably learns a heterogeneous sequence of nonlinear and chaotic systems. Furthermore, we show that forward transfer is more pronounced and useful when tasks share similar underlying dynamics. Lastly, we demonstrate that CRUG's advantages extend beyond autonomous cDSR to sequential cognitive tasks.

</details>


### [200] [On the Off-Policy Teacher in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38360)
*Langlin Huang,Hao Liu,Mononito Goswami,Xinyu Li,Prithwith Jana,Nikos Kanakaris,Patrick Blöbaum,Purak Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOUT框架，通过协同训练使教师模型适应学生生成的推理前缀，从而提升在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏中，学生从自身策略生成的轨迹中学习并接受教师密集监督。然而存在根本性不对称：轨迹对学生是在线策略，但对教师是离线的。教师通常在自身策略生成的前缀上优化，但在OPD中需监督学生生成的前缀，导致其续写性能随前缀增长而下降。

Method: 提出SCOUT（Student-COnditioned Updates of the Teacher）协同训练框架：在标准OPD更新之外，定期使用可验证奖励的强化学习优化教师的条件续写能力——教师从学生前缀生成续写并根据结果奖励学习。

Result: 受控实验表明，SCOUT提升了教师从学生生成前缀续写的能力，验证了学生条件化教师适应的预期机制；在多种教师-学生配置、模型规模和推理领域上，SCOUT均持续提升在线策略蒸馏的效果。

Conclusion: 通过在训练中让教师适应学生前缀，SCOUT有效缓解了OPD中的师生前缀分布不对称问题，是一种通用且有效的在线策略蒸馏增强方案。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has recently emerged as a promising post-training paradigm in which the student learns from trajectories generated by its own policy under dense teacher supervision. However, OPD introduces a fundamental asymmetry: although the sampled trajectories are on-policy for the student, they are off-policy for the teacher. The teacher is typically optimized to continue from prefixes generated by its own policy, but during OPD it must instead supervise prefixes generated by the student. Empirically, we find that its continuation performance degrades as these prefixes grow longer. To address this issue, we propose Student-COnditioned Updates of the Teacher (SCOUT), a co-training framework that adapts the teacher to student-generated prefixes. Alongside standard OPD updates, SCOUT periodically optimizes the teacher's conditional ability using reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, where the teacher generates continuations from student prefixes and learns from outcome rewards. Controlled experiments show that SCOUT improves the teacher's ability to continue from student-generated prefixes, supporting the intended mechanism of student-conditioned teacher adaptation. Across multiple teacher--student configurations, model scales, and reasoning domains, SCOUT also consistently improves the effectiveness of on-policy distillation.

</details>


### [201] [Simulator-Refined Diffusion for Radio-Frequency Inverse Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38363)
*Jinhao Liang,Jacob K. Christopher,Michael Frei,Tommaso Dreossi,Nando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Simulator-Refined Diffusion (SRD)，将可微低精度代理模型与非可微高精度仿真器结合，用于印刷电路板逆向设计的扩散采样，显著提升 S 参数匹配精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型用于 PCB 布局逆向设计时难以满足定量电磁指标。梯度引导采样中，全波电磁仿真器准确但昂贵且不可微，可微代理模型虽有信息但不可靠，二者难以兼顾。

Method: 在扩散采样过程中同时引入低精度可微代理模型与高精度不可微仿真器：用代理模型的梯度提出扰动方向，再由仿真器沿该方向搜索，从而确定有效的设计更新，避免标准零阶优化所需的大量随机扰动。

Result: 在不同设置下持续优于当前最先进方法；生成布局的仿真 S 参数与目标规格的匹配度，分布内目标提升最高 21.2%，分布外目标提升最高 19.8%。

Conclusion: SRD 有效结合可微代理与高精度仿真器，缓解了电磁仿真不可微与代理不可靠的矛盾，提升了扩散式 PCB 逆向设计的定量指标符合度。

Abstract: Diffusion models have shown potential in inverse design of printed circuit boards (PCBs), enabling the generation of layouts conditioned on target S-parameters. Despite this promise, applying diffusion models to PCB layout generation remains challenging due to their difficulty in meeting the quantitative electromagnetic specifications. A common approach is gradient-based guidance, which biases the diffusion sampling process with the gradient of an objective used for evaluation. However, full-wave electromagnetic simulators are accurate but expensive and typically non-differentiable, whereas differentiable surrogates are informative but not always reliable. To address these limitations, this paper proposes Simulator-Refined Diffusion (SRD), a novel combination of a low-fidelity differentiable surrogate and a high-fidelity non-differentiable simulator within the diffusion sampling process. Unlike standard zeroth-order optimization, which requires a great number of random perturbations, our approach uses the surrogate's gradient to propose the perturbation direction while the simulator then searches based on this direction to identify an effective design update. Experimental results across different settings show that this method consistently outperforms current state-of-the-art methods, producing layouts whose simulated S-parameters match the target specifications up to 21.2% closer for in-distribution targets and up to 19.8% for out-of-distribution targets.

</details>


### [202] [Learning to Plan from Random Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38383)
*Deqian Kong,Guangyan Sun,Sheng Cheng,Sirui Xie,Bo Pang,Jianwen Xie,Tony Geng,Caiwen Ding,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 从随机游走数据中学习多尺度时间关系（能量模型估计时间对数密度比），无需策略改进训练即可实现长程规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机探索数据中隐含了环境可通行性信息，但能否在无策略改进训练的情况下支撑长程规划尚不明确；作者希望解释随机游走所蕴含的时间关系结构并加以利用。

Method: 通过随机游走分析指出：短时间尺度在扩散极限下呈现测地几何，长时间尺度揭示区域连通性。用条件能量模型经噪声对比估计（NCE）在观测对（无动作、无奖励标签）上训练，学习时间对数密度比。规划时在不同时间尺度查询该模型；测试时由单独的局部动力学模型预测候选动作结果，时间模型评估其向目标的进展并在多尺度上选择或聚合改进量，执行一步动作后以固定模型重规划。

Result: 在状态与图像上实现了基于随机探索的长程迷宫规划；学习到的得分场、嵌入探针与规划路径表现出多尺度认知地图特性；并进一步展示了基于随机探索的自我中心导航以及从次优数据中进行操作规划。

Conclusion: 随机探索经验可支持长程规划，无需策略改进训练，所学时间关系模型可作为多尺度认知地图用于导航与操作任务。

Abstract: Random exploration reveals how an environment can be traversed before a goal is specified. Can this experience support long-range planning without policy-improvement training? Our random-walk analysis explains what temporal relations contain: short horizons reveal geodesic geometry in the diffusion limit, while longer horizons reveal connectivity between regions before mixing removes these distinctions. We learn these relations with a conditional energy-based model that estimates temporal log-density ratios through horizon-conditioned embeddings. The model is trained on observation pairs by noise-contrastive estimation, without action or reward labels. The planner queries these learned relations at different horizons as it moves toward the goal. At test time, a separate local dynamics model predicts candidate action outcomes, and the temporal model evaluates their progress toward the goal by selecting or aggregating estimated improvements across horizons. The agent executes one action and replans with both models fixed. Experiments demonstrate long-range maze planning from random exploration using states and images. Learned score fields, embedding probes, and planned routes exhibit properties of a multiscale cognitive map. We further demonstrate egocentric navigation from random exploration and manipulation planning from suboptimal data.

</details>


### [203] [LeanPolish: Verified Supervision for Lean Proof Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38384)
*Pauline Bourigault*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LeanPolish 符号化 Lean 4 流水线，发布 33,402 条被接受的局部编辑与 65,596 条同状态失败尝试，并据此区分"模仿搜索策略"与"超越搜索"两类学习信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已验证的证明编辑是提升语言模型生成 Lean 证明的自然监督信号，但"验证通过"只能证明编辑正确，无法保证训练信号不含搜索过程的伪影（artifacts），因此需要可控的数据与评估来厘清模型究竟学到了什么。

Method: 构建 LeanPolish 符号化 Lean 4 流水线，收集被接受的局部编辑和同状态失败尝试；设计首成功搜索与延续菜单评估两种设置，训练排序器与神经编辑模型，并在 miniF2F、PutnamBench 等基准上与冻结模型对照进行消融与受控实验。

Result: 首成功搜索存在与目标无关的排序捷径，达到完美排序准确率；教师选点会奖励平凡删除。延续菜单评估消除该捷径后，训练排序器在 70.1% 的留出状态上选出最优候选，而最强冻结基线仅 36.9%。符号迭代使 miniF2F 节省率从 19.7% 提升至 27.5%，超过所测神经混合方法；神经编辑在其他证明源上有效，但匹配冻结模型对照显示增益未必来自训练。微调使 19 个 PutnamBench 证明的验证 token 缩减率由 2.8% 升至 5.5%。

Conclusion: 发布的数据、完整候选池与受控评估将"模仿搜索策略"与"改进搜索"分离，为在保持正确性、压缩率与编辑策略相互独立的前提下研究证明改进提供了可复现基础。

Abstract: Verified proof edits offer a natural source of supervision for improving language-model-generated Lean proofs. Yet verification establishes that an edit is correct, not that its training signal is free of search artifacts. We introduce LeanPolish, a symbolic Lean 4 pipeline that releases 33,402 accepted local edits and 65,596 same-state failed attempts, and use it to study what models learn from this supervision. First-success search admits a goal-independent rule with perfect ranking accuracy; teacher-selected evaluation sites also reward trivial deletions. Continuing menu evaluation beyond the first success removes the ordering shortcut: a trained ranker selects the best candidate on 70.1% of evaluated held-out states, versus 36.9% for the strongest frozen baseline. For compression, iterating the symbolic pass raises miniF2F savings from 19.7% to 27.5%, exceeding the neural hybrids we test there. Verified neural editing helps on other proof sources, but matched frozen-model controls show that its gains need not come from training. The supervision does improve whole-proof rewriting: fine-tuning raises verified token reduction from 2.8% to 5.5% on 19 PutnamBench proofs. Together, the released edits, complete candidate pools, and controlled evaluations separate learning to imitate a search policy from improving on that search. They provide a reproducible basis for studying proof improvement while keeping correctness, compression, and edit policy distinct.

</details>


### [204] [Which Tasks Survive Self-Supervised Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38393)
*Achleshwar Luthra,Lucas Bryant,Tracy Zhu,Tomer Galanti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从"语义可恢复性"角度刻画同实例自监督学习（SSL）保留下游任务信息的机制，给出谱刻画并验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同实例SSL通过同一实例两个视图的一致性来学习表征，但这一原则本身并不能说明哪些下游任务仍可从学到的表征中恢复，缺乏任务层面的理论解释。

Method: 定义"语义可恢复性"为表征函数空间所捕获的任务后验分数比例；针对中心化、白化表征，证明可恢复性决定方向性类别距离归一化方差（CDNV）、控制少样本最近质心分类，并支配任务相关语义方向的强度。随后分析典型两视图SSL目标，证明其总体最优解张成两视图算子的前若干跨视图稳定模态。

Result: 得到语义可恢复性的闭式谱刻画：下游任务被保留的程度取决于其后验是否落在所选谱子空间内；总体线性探针与质心轴重合，多个恢复良好的任务趋于因子化质心几何。在合成与真实数据集、多种SSL方法上验证了可恢复性、方向几何、谱结构与少样本迁移之间的预测关系。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了任务层面的解释，说明同实例SSL中哪些信息得以保留，以及所保留信息如何体现在下游几何与迁移性能中。

Abstract: Same-instance self-supervised learning (SSL) learns representations by enforcing consistency across two views of the same underlying instance. This principle alone, however, does not determine which downstream tasks remain recoverable from the learned representation. We study this question through \emph{semantic recoverability}, defined as the amount of a task's posterior score captured by the represented function space. We show that, for centered and whitened representations, recoverability exactly determines directional class-distance-normalized variance (CDNV), controls few-shot nearest-centroid classification, and governs the strength of task-relevant semantic directions. The population linear probe and centroid axis coincide, and multiple well-recovered tasks approach a factorial centroid geometry. We then analyze a canonical two-view SSL objective and show that its population optimum spans the leading cross-view-stable modes of the associated two-view operator. This yields a closed-form spectral characterization of semantic recoverability: a downstream task is preserved to the extent that its posterior lies in the selected spectral subspace. We validate these predictions on synthetic and real datasets across several SSL methods, testing the predicted relationships among recoverability, directional geometry, spectral structure, and few-shot transfer. Together, these results give a task-level account of what information survives same-instance SSL and how the retained information appears in downstream geometry and transfer.

</details>


### [205] [Disagreement-Regularized Imitation Learning for Image-Based Continuous Control with Gaussian and Beta Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38407)
*Irving Giovani Bronzatti Petrazzini,Eric Aislan Antonelo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究 Disagreement-Regularized Imitation Learning (DRIL) 在基于图像的连续控制中的效果，通过 CarRacing 上的受控实验比较不同策略表示、演示动作范围、轨迹数量与评估方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆在控制器进入演示分布之外的状态时会累积误差，因此需要评估能否利用克隆策略间的分歧作为强化学习奖励来改善模仿学习。

Method: 在 CarRacing 上进行受控实验，结合高斯与 Beta 学习器策略、来自裁剪高斯专家或内在有界 Beta 专家的演示、1 或 20 条轨迹、确定性与随机评估，并保留行为克隆、最高 10 回合训练得分检查点和最终 DRIL 检查点三个阶段；分歧集成始终包含五个高斯策略，每个保留策略在 100 个程序生成回合上评估。

Result: 得分选择的 DRIL 在少演示设定下提升最大：相比最强行为克隆均值，裁剪动作演示提升 61%，有界动作演示提升 112%。当轨迹增至 20 条时 DRIL 优势缩小；在有界动作情形下，Beta 行为克隆仍比最佳 DRIL 检查点高约 7%。实验还表明分歧奖励的信息量随学习器表示和训练阶段而变化。

Conclusion: DRIL 能显著改善少演示的视觉连续控制，而在演示较多时有界 Beta 策略可提供较强的行为克隆性能；结果凸显学习器支撑、集成响应与检查点选择三者共同的重要性。

Abstract: Purpose: Behavior cloning can accumulate errors when a learned controller visits states outside the demonstrated distribution. This study evaluates whether Disagreement-Regularized Imitation Learning (DRIL), which converts disagreement among cloned policies into a reinforcement-learning reward, improves image-based continuous control. Methods: A controlled CarRacing study combines Gaussian and Beta learner policies, demonstrations from either a clipped Gaussian expert or an intrinsically bounded Beta expert, one or 20 trajectories, deterministic and stochastic evaluation, and three retained stages: behavior cloning, the highest 10-episode training-score checkpoint, and the final DRIL checkpoint. The disagreement ensemble contains five Gaussian policies in every variant. Each retained policy is evaluated over 100 procedurally generated episodes. Results: Score-selected DRIL produced its largest gains in the few-demonstration setting, improving over the strongest behavior-cloning mean by 61% with clipped-action demonstrations and by 112% with bounded-action demonstrations. With 20 trajectories, the advantage of DRIL narrowed; in the bounded-action regime, Beta behavior cloning remained about 7% above the best DRIL checkpoint. The experiments also show that the informativeness of the disagreement reward changes with the learner representation and training stage. Conclusion: DRIL can substantially improve few-demonstration visual continuous control, while bounded Beta policies provide strong behavior-cloning performance when more demonstrations are available. The results highlight the joint importance of learner support,ensemble response, and checkpoint selection.

</details>


### [206] [Synthesis Without Training: An Inference-Only Pipeline for Tabular, Temporal, and Relational Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38414)
*Zilong Zhao,Abdul Raheem,Jiayu Li,Sohei Arisaka,Darius Lim Hong Yi,Milad Abdollahzadeh,Uzair Javaid,Biplab Sikdar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GENSSCRIPT是一种无需训练的推理式合成数据生成流程，利用语言模型与代码智能体将数据统计画像编译为可审计采样器，统一支持单表、时序与关系数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据生成依赖"先拟合再采样"范式，存在三大问题：每个数据集都需重新训练模型；不同模态（单表、时间序列、关系数据库）需任务特定模型与特征工程；模型不透明，难以检查数据约束下的行为。作者希望消除训练环节。

Method: 提出GENSCRIPT：先计算源数据的确定性统计画像（列类型、取值范围、缺失率、类别、相关性等），将该画像而非原始行数据传给语言模型，由其推断字段语义与跨列完整性约束；再由编码智能体把画像与约束编译成可执行、可审计的采样器。该方法统一支持单表、时序和关系数据，无需任务特定建模。

Result: 在四个单表基准上，2分钟即可构建生成器，6秒内采样5万行，边际保真度与领先方法仅差几个百分点；是唯一在Adult数据集上完美保持列间1对1映射的方法。在智能建筑数据集上，生成的条件时间序列比两个基线更接近真实分布，并在对应关系数据库中完美保持主键与外键关系。

Conclusion: GENSCRIPT证明无需训练的推理式流程可在速度、可审计性与多模态通用性上取得优势，同时保持有竞争力的数据保真度与约束一致性。

Abstract: Synthetic data generation is dominated by the fit-then-sample paradigm: a generative model is trained on a private dataset and then sampled from. Despite its widespread adoption, this paradigm faces three challenges: (1) a new training run is required for every dataset; (2) different data modalities, such as single tables, time series, and relational databases, require task-specific models and feature engineering; and (3) the resulting model is opaque, making its behavior under data constraints difficult to inspect. We propose GENSCRIPT, an inference-only pipeline that eliminates model training. GENSCRIPT computes a deterministic statistical profile of the source data (column types, ranges, missingness, categories, correlations, etc.) and passes it--rather than raw rows--to a language model to infer field semantics and cross-column integrity constraints. A coding agent then compiles the profile and constraints into an executable, auditable sampler. This unified approach supports single-table, temporal, and relational data without task-specific modeling. Across four single-table benchmarks, GENSCRIPT builds generators in 2 minutes and samples 50k rows within 6 seconds, while remaining within a few points of leading methods in marginal fidelity. Notably, it is the only method that perfectly preserves a 1-to-1 mapping between columns in the Adult dataset. On a smart-building dataset, it produces conditional time series that more closely match the real distribution than two baselines and perfectly preserves primary- and foreign-key relationships in the corresponding relational database.

</details>


### [207] [Graph Anomaly Detection as Finite-Horizon Control: Training-Free Scoring via Empirical Bayes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38424)
*Fred Xu,Thomas Markovich,Florence Regol,Yizhou Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 EB-GAD，一个免训练的图节点异常检测框架，将正常性建模为图感知的广义 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 松弛过程，并用经验贝叶斯拟合图精度，从而得到闭式控制能量评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图异常检测方法通过架构、消息传递、重构或对比目标间接编码正常性，导致图信任、图谱加权与异常评分相互纠缠，评分成本高、不透明且在不同图结构下不稳定。

Method: 将正常性建模为向图滤波模板的图感知广义 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck（GOU）松弛；用经验贝叶斯从残差场似然中拟合图精度；将评分转化为闭式有限视界控制能量（将特征中性节点沿图谱松弛引导到观测终点的最小代价）；扫描松弛视界与终点容差得到共享同一拟合先验的评分库，平衡 Mahalanobis 评分是其中一个极限；用无标签选择器依据特征同质性、边密度和特征维度选择评分族，并按拟合零假设偏差和排名稳定性排序候选。

Result: 在 11 个基准上全程无标签，EB-GAD 在 9 个数据集上取得最佳或并列最佳 AUROC，包括四个金融欺诈网络（最大 370 万节点）、YelpChi 与 Amazon 评论图、Weibo、Reddit 和 Facebook，领先幅度最高达 21.7 个百分点；在 BlogCatalog 和 ACM 上排名第二。

Conclusion: EB-GAD 通过免训练的经验贝叶斯与图谱松弛控制能量框架，解耦了图结构信任、谱加权与评分选择，实现了高效、可解释且跨图机制稳定的节点异常检测，并在多数基准上取得最优性能。

Abstract: Node-level graph anomaly detection (GAD) identifies nodes whose attributes and interactions deviate from dominant graph regularities. Existing GAD models encode normality and anomaly scoring indirectly through architectures, message passing, reconstruction or contrastive objectives, and tuned score families. This entangles graph trust (how strongly graph structure should define normality), graph-spectral weighting, and anomaly-score choice, yielding scores that are costly, opaque, and unstable across graph regimes. We propose EB-GAD (Empirical-Bayes GAD), a training-free framework that models normality as graph-aware generalized Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (GOU) relaxation toward a graph-filtered template. Empirical Bayes fits the graph precision from the residual-field likelihood; the GOU then turns scoring into a closed-form finite-horizon control energy, the minimum effort to steer a feature-neutral node to its observed endpoint along graph-spectral relaxation. Sweeping relaxation horizon and endpoint tolerance yields a bank of scores that share one fitted prior: equilibrium Mahalanobis scoring is one limit, while finite-horizon control-energy and scale-normalized ratio scores reveal anomalies that static equilibrium scoring can mask. A label-free selector chooses the score family from feature homophily, edge density, and feature dimension, then ranks candidates by fitted-null deviation and rank stability. On 11 benchmarks and without labels at any step, EB-GAD has the best or tied-best AUROC on 9: the four financial fraud networks (up to 3.7M nodes), the YelpChi and Amazon review graphs, Weibo, Reddit and Facebook, with margins of up to 21.7 points. It is second on BlogCatalog and ACM.

</details>


### [208] [What Pretraining and Midtraining Make Learnable from Rewards?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38446)
*Chiwun Yang,Xiaoyu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究预训练与中期训练如何为奖励适配提供信息与计算基础，通过顺序状态计算与上下文记忆两类机制，证明预测阶段习得执行或检索能力、奖励阶段习得其任务特定用法，并用Qwen2.5实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励只能标识正确答案，却无法确定新输入所需的计算过程，因此需要理解预训练与中期训练如何提供使奖励适配有效的信息与计算。作者关注在训练奖励上完全一致、但在留出答案上要求不同的机制之间的歧义问题。

Method: 在顺序状态计算与上下文记忆两种设定下刻画机制；引入任务无关的源观测以消解歧义；构造从指定随机初始化出发、在同一参数中经源预测与奖励适配的有限采样Adam路径；并使用预训练Qwen2.5检查点在八个"世界"及GSM8K、HotpotQA上开展实验。

Result: 顺序模型在正确源与首操作监督下达到82.61%成功率，私有随机源对照仅44.15%；记忆回放可在奖励适配中保持检索能力，独立八世界确认实验取得75.32%任务成功率，而匹配的替代检索训练后为49.86%；GSM8K与HotpotQA在奖励进入时的准确率、后续增益与最终表现上呈现分离。

Conclusion: 预测阶段习得可执行的执行或检索能力，奖励阶段学习其任务特定的使用方式，从而将信息获取、可执行计算与奖励引导的任务学习联系起来。

Abstract: A reward can identify a correct answer while leaving the computation needed for new inputs undetermined. We study how pretraining and midtraining supply the information and computation that make reward adaptation effective. In sequential state computation and contextual memory, we characterize mechanisms that agree on every training reward yet demand different held-out answers. Task-independent source observations resolve this ambiguity. We construct finite sampled Adam paths from specified random initializations through source prediction and reward adaptation in the same parameters, proving how prediction acquires execution or retrieval and rewards learn their task-specific use. Experiments with pretrained Qwen2.5 checkpoints test this division of labor. Across eight worlds, Sequential models trained with correct source and first-operation supervision reach 82.61% success, versus 44.15% for a private-random source control. Memory replay preserves retrieval during reward adaptation, and an independent eight-world confirmation achieves 75.32% task success versus 49.86% after matched alternative-retrieval training. GSM8K and HotpotQA separate accuracy at reward entry, subsequent gain and final performance. Together, these results connect information acquisition, executable computation and reward-guided task learning.

</details>


### [209] [An Input-Frugal Deep Learning Framework for Weather-Driven National Crop-Yield Forecasting: A Case Study of Brazilian Soybean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38447)
*Fernando Dupin da Cunha Mello,Prashant Kumar,Erick G. Sperandio Nascimento*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种仅依赖常规天气数据、架构无关的轻量深度学习框架用于作物产量预测，在巴西大豆20年数据上验证，Transformer精度最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的农作物产量预测对市场稳定和风险管理至关重要，但现有方法往往依赖成本高或难以规模化推广的输入数据，亟需一种节俭、可迁移的预测方案。

Method: 以常规天气为唯一时变输入，辅以作物年度和农业环境标签两个轻量静态上下文输入，构建架构无关的深度学习框架，支持多种序列编码器（MLP、CNN、LSTM、CNN-LSTM、Transformer、Mamba）。采用2001/02-2020/21共20季巴西大豆案例，使用留一年交叉验证，与岭回归及五年移动平均基线对比，并进行消融和SHAP诊断。

Result: 所有深度学习变体均优于岭回归，所有序列编码器优于非序列MLP。Transformer全国精度最佳（RMSE 149 kg/ha，rRMSE 5.3%，R²=0.784），较基线误差降低47.6%。季内预测从早季到末季误差单调下降，最新预测点较基线约降50%。消融显示农业环境标签与空间实例扩展有正向贡献。SHAP表明作物年度主导长期趋势，季内天气与农业环境主要驱动年际偏差。

Conclusion: 该框架输入简单、易于部署，可迁移至其他作物与地区，并天然兼容业务化天气预报，适用于日常监测。

Abstract: Reliable, timely crop-yield forecasts are essential for market stability and risk management, yet many approaches rely on costly or hard-to-scale inputs. We present a frugal, transferable, and architecture-agnostic deep learning framework that uses routine weather as the only time-varying input plus two lightweight static context inputs (crop year and an agro-environmental label) to capture long-run change and regional heterogeneity, while supporting multiple sequence encoders under identical data requirements. Using a 20-season Brazilian soybean case study (2001/02-2020/21) with leave-one-year-out cross-validation, we benchmark MLP, CNN, LSTM, CNN-LSTM, a Transformer encoder and the Mamba state-space model against linear ridge regression and a five-year moving-average "farmer" baseline. All deep learning variants outperform ridge, and all sequential encoders surpass the non-sequential MLP. The Transformer achieves the best national accuracy (RMSE 149 kg ha^-1; rRMSE 5.3%; R^2 = 0.784), reducing error by 47.6% relative to the farmer baseline. In-season forecasts improve monotonically from early- to late-season issuance, reaching approximately 50% lower error than the baseline at the latest forecast point. Ablations indicate that the agro-environmental label and spatial instance expansion (multiple grid-node weather sequences per municipality-year) contribute positively without increasing input complexity. SHAP diagnostics suggest crop year explains most of the long-run trajectory, whereas within-season weather and agro-environmental context primarily drive interannual deviations, with moisture/cloud and thermal-demand variables dominating. Overall, the framework is straightforward to deploy across other crops and geographic regions and is naturally compatible with operational weather forecasts for routine monitoring.

</details>


### [210] [Grokking through the Lens of Minimum-Norm Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38453)
*Gil Kur,Ileana Rugina,Clémentine Carla Juliette Dominé,Marco Mondelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文建立统计理论，刻画正则化几何与信号稀疏性如何控制插值附近的泛化，解释 grokking 的延迟泛化现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 grokking 理论难以定量说明延迟泛化如何依赖于归纳偏置与信号结构，论文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 聚焦高维回归原型设定，发展统计理论，分析稀疏促进正则化的插值器，证明零-一泛化律，并刻画 ℓ_r 正则化路径上的训练与泛化误差；在对角线性网络与模运算 Transformer 上验证。

Result: 证明强过参数无噪声下存在零-一泛化律与凸范数族，使插值器从全零预测器的平凡风险过渡到精确恢复且训练误差保持为 0；泛化增益随范数更促进稀疏、目标更稀疏而增大，无噪声 ℓ_1 正则化时泛化急剧提升。

Conclusion: 揭示了最小范数插值的统计不稳定性：正则化强度的微小扰动可在保持小训练误差的同时导致泛化差异巨大。

Abstract: Grokking shows that fitting the training data and learning the underlying signal can occur at very different stages. However, existing theories offer limited quantitative insight into how this delayed generalization depends on inductive bias and signal structure. Our work addresses the gap by developing a statistical theory that characterizes how regularization geometry and signal sparsity govern generalization near interpolation. In particular, we focus on the prototypical setting of high-dimensional regression and identify regimes in which sparsity-promoting regularization makes exact interpolation much more accurate than approximate fitting. In strongly overparameterized noiseless problems, we prove a zero--one generalization law and construct a family of convex norms whose interpolators transition from the trivial risk of the all-zero predictor to exact recovery, while keeping the training error equal to $0$. Furthermore, when feature dimension and sample size are proportional, we provide a precise characterization of training and generalization errors along $\ell_r$-regularization paths. This in turn allows us to quantify the generalization gain that remains near interpolation: we show that this gain increases as the norm becomes more sparsity-promoting and as the target becomes sparser, with a sharp drop in generalization reached for noiseless data and $\ell_1$ regularization.
